// ============================================================
// MAIN
// ============================================================
void main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  await Firebase.initializeApp(
    options: DefaultFirebaseOptions.currentPlatform,
  );
  await initializeDateFormatting('ar', null);
  await StorageService.instance.init();
  await AppThemeController.load();
  await NotificationService.instance.init();
  runApp(const GymApp());
}

// ============================================================
// APP PALETTES
// ============================================================
class AppPalette {
  final int primary;
  final int primaryDark;
  final int accent;
  final int softPrimary;
  final int error;
  final int success;
  final int info;
  final int purple;
  final int bgDark;
  final int bgLight;
  final int surfaceDark;
  final int surfaceLight;
  final int textPrimaryDark;
  final int textPrimaryLight;
  final int textSecondary;
  final int borderDark;
  final int borderLight;
  const AppPalette({
    required this.primary,
    required this.primaryDark,
    required this.accent,
    required this.softPrimary,
    required this.error,
    required this.success,
    required this.info,
    required this.purple,
    required this.bgDark,
    required this.bgLight,
    required this.surfaceDark,
    required this.surfaceLight,
    required this.textPrimaryDark,
    required this.textPrimaryLight,
    required this.textSecondary,
    required this.borderDark,
    required this.borderLight,
  });
  LinearGradient get gradient => LinearGradient(
        colors: [Color(primary), Color(accent)],
        begin: Alignment.topLeft,
        end: Alignment.bottomRight,
      );
}

const AppPalette kOrangePalette = AppPalette(
  primary: 0xFFFF6B00,
  primaryDark: 0xFFE65C00,
  accent: 0xFFFF8C33,
  softPrimary: 0xFFFFF4EC,
  error: 0xFFEF4444,
  success: 0xFF22C55E,
  info: 0xFF3B82F6,
  purple: 0xFF8B5CF6,
  bgDark: 0xFF0F1115,
  bgLight: 0xFFF5F5F7,
  surfaceDark: 0xFF181B21,
  surfaceLight: 0xFFFFFFFF,
  textPrimaryDark: 0xFFFFFFFF,
  textPrimaryLight: 0xFF1F2937,
  textSecondary: 0xFF9CA3AF,
  borderDark: 0xFF2A2E36,
  borderLight: 0xFFE5E7EB,
);

const AppPalette kPinkPalette = AppPalette(
  primary: 0xFFEC4899,
  primaryDark: 0xFFDB2777,
  accent: 0xFFF472B6,
  softPrimary: 0xFFFDF2F8,
  error: 0xFFEF4444,

  borderLight: 0xFFFBCFE8,
);

// ============================================================
// APP COLORS
// ============================================================
class AppColors {
  AppColors._();
  static AppPalette _palette = kOrangePalette;
  static AppPalette get palette => _palette;
  static set palette(AppPalette p) => _palette = p;
  static Color get primary => Color(_palette.primary);
  static Color get primaryDark => Color(_palette.primaryDark);
  static Color get accent => Color(_palette.accent);
  static Color get softPrimary => Color(_palette.softPrimary);
  static Color get error => Color(_palette.error);
  static Color get success => Color(_palette.success);
  static Color get info => Color(_palette.info);
  static Color get purple => Color(_palette.purple);
  static Color get bgDark => Color(_palette.bgDark);
  static Color get bgLight => Color(_palette.bgLight);
  static Color get surfaceDark => Color(_palette.surfaceDark);
  static Color get surfaceLight => Color(_palette.surfaceLight);
  static Color get textPrimaryDark => Color(_palette.textPrimaryDark);
  static Color get textPrimaryLight => Color(_palette.textPrimaryLight);
  static Color get textSecondary => Color(_palette.textSecondary);
  static Color get borderDark => Color(_palette.borderDark);
  static Color get borderLight => Color(_palette.borderLight);
  static LinearGradient get primaryGradient => _palette.gradient;
}

// ============================================================
// THEME CONTROLLER
// ============================================================
enum AppPaletteMode { orange, pink }

class AppThemeController {
  AppThemeController._();
  static const String _kPaletteKey = 'app_palette_mode_v1';
  static final ValueNotifier<AppPaletteMode> mode =
      ValueNotifier<AppPaletteMode>(AppPaletteMode.orange);
  static AppPalette paletteOf(AppPaletteMode m) =>
      m == AppPaletteMode.pink ? kPinkPalette : kOrangePalette;
  static Future<void> load() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final v = prefs.getString(_kPaletteKey);
      final m = v == 'pink' ? AppPaletteMode.pink : AppPaletteMode.orange;
      mode.value = m;
      AppColors.palette = paletteOf(m);
    } catch (_) {
      AppColors.palette = kOrangePalette;
    }
  }

  static Future<void> setMode(AppPaletteMode m) async {
    mode.value = m;
    AppColors.palette = paletteOf(m);
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await prefs.setString(
          _kPaletteKey, m == AppPaletteMode.pink ? 'pink' : 'orange');
    } catch (_) {}
  }
}

// ============================================================
// APP ROOT
// ============================================================
class GymApp extends StatefulWidget {
  const GymApp({super.key});

  @override
  State<GymApp> createState() => _GymAppState();
}

class _GymAppState extends State<GymApp> {
  ThemeMode _themeMode = ThemeMode.dark;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ValueListenableBuilder<AppPaletteMode>(
      valueListenable: AppThemeController.mode,
      builder: (context, paletteMode, _) {
        AppColors.palette = AppThemeController.paletteOf(paletteMode);
        return MaterialApp(
          title: 'مدير كورسات الجيم',
          debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
          themeMode: _themeMode,
          theme: _buildTheme(Brightness.light),
          darkTheme: _buildTheme(Brightness.dark),
          locale: const Locale('ar', 'SA'),
          supportedLocales: const [Locale('ar', 'SA'), Locale('en', 'US')],
          localizationsDelegates: const [
            GlobalMaterialLocalizations.delegate,
            GlobalWidgetsLocalizations.delegate,
            GlobalCupertinoLocalizations.delegate,
          ],
          builder: (context, child) => Directionality(
            textDirection: ui.TextDirection.rtl,
            child: child ?? const SizedBox.shrink(),
          ),
          home: UpdateGate(
            child: AuthGate(
              themeMode: _themeMode,
              onThemeChanged: (m) => setState(() => _themeMode = m),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  ThemeData _buildTheme(Brightness brightness) {
    final bool isDark = brightness == Brightness.dark;
    final Color surface =
        isDark ? AppColors.surfaceDark : AppColors.surfaceLight;
    final Color bg = isDark ? AppColors.bgDark : AppColors.bgLight;
    final Color border = isDark ? AppColors.borderDark : AppColors.borderLight;
    final Color textPrimary =
        isDark ? AppColors.textPrimaryDark : AppColors.textPrimaryLight;
    final ColorScheme scheme = ColorScheme.fromSeed(
      seedColor: AppColors.primary,
      brightness: brightness,
    ).copyWith(
      primary: AppColors.primary,
      secondary: AppColors.accent,
      surface: surface,
      error: AppColors.error,
    );
    final TextTheme baseText = ThemeData(brightness: brightness).textTheme;
    final TextTheme text = GoogleFonts.cairoTextTheme(baseText).apply(
      bodyColor: textPrimary,
      displayColor: textPrimary,
    );
    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      brightness: brightness,
      colorScheme: scheme,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: bg,
      textTheme: text,
      primaryTextTheme: text,
      appBarTheme: AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: surface,
        foregroundColor: textPrimary,
        elevation: 0,
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
        centerTitle: true,
        titleTextStyle: GoogleFonts.cairo(
            fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: textPrimary),
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: textPrimary),
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        color: surface,
        elevation: 0,
        margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          side: BorderSide(color: border.withOpacity(0.6)),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: isDark ? AppColors.surfaceDark : Colors.white,
        contentPadding:
            const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 18),
        labelStyle:
            GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14),
        hintStyle:
            GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14),
        prefixIconColor: AppColors.primary,
        suffixIconColor: AppColors.textSecondary,
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: border),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: border),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary, width: 2),
        ),
        errorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: AppColors.error, width: 1.5),
        ),
        focusedErrorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          borderSide: BorderSide(color: AppColors.error, width: 2),
        ),
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          elevation: 0,
          minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(52),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14, horizontal: 24),
          shape:
              RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
          textStyle:
              GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        ),
      ),
      filledButtonTheme: FilledButtonThemeData(
        style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(52),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14, horizontal: 24),
          shape:
              RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
          textStyle:
              GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        ),
      ),
      outlinedButtonTheme: OutlinedButtonThemeData(
        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: AppColors.primary,
          minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(52),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14, horizontal: 24),
          side: BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary, width: 1.4),
          shape:
              RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
          textStyle:
              GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        ),
      ),
      textButtonTheme: TextButtonThemeData(
        style: TextButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: AppColors.primary,
          textStyle:
              GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        ),
      ),
      navigationBarTheme: NavigationBarThemeData(
        backgroundColor: surface,
        elevation: 0,
        height: 68,
        indicatorColor: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.15),
        labelTextStyle: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith((states) {
          final selected = states.contains(WidgetState.selected);
          return GoogleFonts.cairo(
            fontSize: 11.5,
            fontWeight: selected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.w500,
            color: selected ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.textSecondary,
          );
        }),
        iconTheme: WidgetStateProperty.resolveWith((states) {
          final selected = states.contains(WidgetState.selected);
          return IconThemeData(
            color: selected ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.textSecondary,
            size: 24,
          );
        }),
      ),
      dividerTheme: DividerThemeData(color: border, thickness: 1, space: 1),
      snackBarTheme: SnackBarThemeData(
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        backgroundColor: isDark ? AppColors.surfaceDark : Colors.black87,
        contentTextStyle: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: Colors.white),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
      ),
      chipTheme: ChipThemeData(
        backgroundColor: surface,
        selectedColor: AppColors.primary,
        side: BorderSide(color: border),
        labelStyle: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12.5),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
      ),
      progressIndicatorTheme: ProgressIndicatorThemeData(
        color: AppColors.primary,
        linearTrackColor: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.13),
        circularTrackColor: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.13),
      ),
      popupMenuTheme: PopupMenuThemeData(
        color: surface,
        elevation: 6,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
        textStyle: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: textPrimary, fontSize: 14),
      ),
      dialogTheme: DialogThemeData(
        backgroundColor: surface,
        elevation: 8,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        titleTextStyle: GoogleFonts.cairo(
            fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: textPrimary),
        contentTextStyle: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14, color: textPrimary),
      ),
      bottomSheetTheme: BottomSheetThemeData(
        backgroundColor: surface,
        shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SHARED UI WIDGETS
// ============================================================
class GradientCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;
  final double radius;
  final Gradient? gradient;
  const GradientCard({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(20),
    this.radius = 16,
    this.gradient,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: padding,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: gradient ?? AppColors.primaryGradient,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radius),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.2),
            blurRadius: 12,
            offset: const Offset(0, 4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: child,
    );
  }
}

class SurfaceCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;
  final double radius;
  final Color? color;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  const SurfaceCard({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(16),
    this.radius = 16,
    this.color,
    this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final content = Container(
      padding: padding,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color ?? theme.cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radius),
        border: Border.all(color: theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.6)),
      ),
      child: child,
    );
    if (onTap == null) return content;
    return Material(
      color: Colors.transparent,
      child: InkWell(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radius),
        onTap: onTap,
        child: content,
      ),
    );
  }
}

class SectionTitle extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final Widget? trailing;
  const SectionTitle({super.key, required this.title, this.trailing});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Container(
          width: 4,
          height: 20,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 10),
        Expanded(
          child: Text(
            title,
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
              fontSize: 17,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyLarge?.color,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        if (trailing != null) trailing!,
      ],
    );
  }
}

class MiniChip extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text;
  final Color color;
  final IconData? icon;
  const MiniChip({
    super.key,
    required this.text,
    required this.color,
    this.icon,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 9, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          if (icon != null) ...[
            Icon(icon, color: color, size: 13),
            const SizedBox(width: 5),
          ],
          Text(
            text,
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                color: color, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class GradientButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final IconData? icon;
  final bool loading;
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;
  const GradientButton({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    this.icon,
    this.loading = false,
    this.onPressed,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.3),
            blurRadius: 16,
            offset: const Offset(0, 6),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Material(
        color: Colors.transparent,
        child: InkWell(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          onTap: loading ? null : onPressed,
          child: Container(
            height: 54,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
            child: loading
                ? const SizedBox(
                    width: 22,
                    height: 22,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                        color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2.4),
                  )
                : Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      if (icon != null) ...[
                        Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      ],
                      Text(
                        label,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontSize: 15.5,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class GradientLogo extends StatelessWidget {
  final IconData icon;
  final double size;
  const GradientLogo({super.key, required this.icon, this.size = 96});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: size,
      height: size,
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.35),
            blurRadius: 28,
            offset: const Offset(0, 10),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: size * 0.46),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// APP VERSION & UPDATE SERVICE
// ============================================================
const String kAppVersion = '1.0.0';

class AppUpdateInfo {
  final String latestVersion;
  final String minVersion;
  final String updateUrl;
  final String? telegramUrl;
  final String? title;
  final String? message;
  final bool enabled;
  AppUpdateInfo({
    required this.latestVersion,
    required this.minVersion,
    required this.updateUrl,
    this.telegramUrl,
    this.title,
    this.message,
    this.enabled = true,
  });

  factory AppUpdateInfo.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> d) {
    bool parseBool(dynamic v) {
      if (v == null) return true;
      if (v is bool) return v;
      if (v is String) {
        final s = v.trim().toLowerCase();
        return s == 'true' || s == '1' || s == 'yes';
      }
      if (v is num) return v != 0;
      return true;
    }

    return AppUpdateInfo(
      latestVersion: (d['latestVersion'] ?? '0.0.0').toString().trim(),
      minVersion: (d['minVersion'] ?? '0.0.0').toString().trim(),
      updateUrl: (d['updateUrl'] ?? '').toString().trim(),
      telegramUrl: d['telegramUrl']?.toString(),
      title: d['title']?.toString(),
      message: d['message']?.toString(),
      enabled: parseBool(d['enabled']),
    );
  }

  static int _cmp(String a, String b) {
    final pa = a.split('.').map((e) => int.tryParse(e.trim()) ?? 0).toList();
    final pb = b.split('.').map((e) => int.tryParse(e.trim()) ?? 0).toList();
    final len = pa.length > pb.length ? pa.length : pb.length;
    for (int i = 0; i < len; i++) {
      final va = i < pa.length ? pa[i] : 0;
      final vb = i < pb.length ? pb[i] : 0;
      if (va != vb) return va.compareTo(vb);
    }
    return 0;
  }

  bool get isForced => _cmp(kAppVersion, minVersion) < 0;
  bool get hasUpdate => _cmp(kAppVersion, latestVersion) < 0;
  String get currentVersion => kAppVersion;
}

class UpdateService {
  UpdateService._();
  static final UpdateService instance = UpdateService._();
  static String? _realVersion;

  static Future<String> getAppVersion() async {
    if (_realVersion != null) return _realVersion!;
    try {
      final info = await PackageInfo.fromPlatform();
      _realVersion = '${info.version}+${info.buildNumber}';
      return _realVersion!;
    } catch (_) {
      return kAppVersion;
    }
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> _defaults() => {
        'enabled': true,
        'latestVersion': kAppVersion,
        'minVersion': kAppVersion,
        'updateUrl': '',
        'telegramUrl': '',
        'title': 'تحديث جديد متوفر',
        'message': 'يتوفر إصدار أحدث من التطبيق مع تحسينات وإصلاحات جديدة.',
        'autoCreated': true,
      };

  Future<AppUpdateInfo?> fetch() async {
    try {
      final ref = FirebaseService.instance.firestore
          .collection('app_config')
          .doc('version');
      final doc = await ref.get();
      if (doc.exists) {
        final data = doc.data();
        if (data == null) return null;
        final merged = <String, dynamic>{..._defaults(), ...data};
        return AppUpdateInfo.fromMap(merged);
      }
      final defaults = _defaults();
      defaults['createdAt'] = FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
      defaults['createdBy'] = 'auto_init';
      try {
        await ref.set(defaults);
      } catch (_) {}
      return AppUpdateInfo.fromMap(defaults);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('UpdateService fetch ERROR: $e');
      return null;
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================
// UPDATE GATE
// ============================================================
class UpdateGate extends StatefulWidget {
  final Widget child;
  const UpdateGate({super.key, required this.child});

  @override
  State<UpdateGate> createState() => _UpdateGateState();
}

class _UpdateGateState extends State<UpdateGate> {
  bool _dialogShown = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _check());
  }

  Future<void> _check() async {
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 900));
    if (!mounted) return;
    AppUpdateInfo? info;
    try {
      info = await UpdateService.instance.fetch();
    } catch (_) {
      return;
    }
    if (info == null || !info.enabled || !info.hasUpdate || _dialogShown) {
      return;
    }
    _dialogShown = true;
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));
    if (!mounted) return;
    try {
      await _showUpdateDialog(info);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _showUpdateDialog(AppUpdateInfo info) async {
    final isForced = info.isForced;
    await showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: !isForced,
      builder: (ctx) => PopScope(
        canPop: !isForced,
        child: Dialog(
          insetPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
          shape:
              RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24)),
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(22),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Theme.of(ctx).cardColor,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
            ),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: 78,
                  height: 78,
                  alignment: Alignment.center,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
                  ),
                  child: Icon(
                    isForced ? Icons.system_update_alt : Icons.new_releases,
                    color: Colors.white,
                    size: 38,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 18),
                Text(
                  info.title ??
                      (isForced ? 'التحديث إجباري' : 'تحديث جديد متوفر'),
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 19, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 14),
                Text(
                  info.message ?? 'يتوفر إصدار أحدث من التطبيق.',
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 13,
                      height: 1.7,
                      color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                if (info.updateUrl.isNotEmpty)
                  GradientButton(
                    label: 'تحديث الآن',
                    icon: Icons.download,
                    onPressed: () => _openUrl(info.updateUrl),
                  ),
                if (!isForced) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),
                    child: Text('لاحقاً',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13.5)),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _openUrl(String url) async {
    final uri = Uri.tryParse(url);
    if (uri == null) return;
    try {
      await launchUrl(uri, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => widget.child;
}

// ============================================================
// NOTIFICATION SERVICE
// ============================================================
class NotificationService {
  NotificationService._();
  static final NotificationService instance = NotificationService._();
  final FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin _plugin =
      FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin();
  bool _initialized = false;

  Future<void> init() async {
    if (_initialized) return;
    const androidInit = AndroidInitializationSettings('@mipmap/launcher_icon');
    const iosInit = DarwinInitializationSettings(
      requestAlertPermission: true,
      requestBadgePermission: true,
      requestSoundPermission: true,
    );
    const initSettings =
        InitializationSettings(android: androidInit, iOS: iosInit);
    await _plugin.initialize(initSettings,
        onDidReceiveNotificationResponse: _onTap,
        onDidReceiveBackgroundNotificationResponse: _onTapBackground);
    await _requestPermissions();
    await _createAndroidChannels();
    _initialized = true;
  }

  Future<void> _requestPermissions() async {
    final android = _plugin.resolvePlatformSpecificImplementation<
        AndroidFlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin>();
    if (android != null) {
      await android.requestNotificationsPermission();
      try {
        await android.requestExactAlarmsPermission();
      } catch (_) {}
    }
    final ios = _plugin.resolvePlatformSpecificImplementation<
        IOSFlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin>();
    if (ios != null) {
      await ios.requestPermissions(alert: true, badge: true, sound: true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _createAndroidChannels() async {
    final android = _plugin.resolvePlatformSpecificImplementation<
        AndroidFlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin>();
    if (android == null) return;
    await android.createNotificationChannel(const AndroidNotificationChannel(
      'course_arrived_channel',
      'كورسات جديدة',
      description: 'إشعارات عند وصول كورس جديد',
      importance: Importance.max,
      playSound: true,
      enableVibration: true,
    ));
    await android.createNotificationChannel(const AndroidNotificationChannel(
      'course_status_channel',
      'تحديثات حالة الكورس',
      description: 'إشعارات عند إيقاف أو تفعيل أو إنهاء الكورس',
      importance: Importance.high,
      playSound: true,
      enableVibration: true,
    ));
  }

  void _onTap(NotificationResponse r) => debugPrint('Tap: ${r.payload}');

  @pragma('vm:entry-point')
  static void _onTapBackground(NotificationResponse r) =>
      debugPrint('BG Tap: ${r.payload}');

  Future<void> showCourseArrived({
    required String playerName,
    required String courseName,
    required String source,
    String? courseId,
  }) async {
    final sourceLabel = source == 'admin'
        ? 'من الإدارة'
        : source == 'captain'
            ? 'من الكابتن'
            : '';
    await _show(
      id: 1001,
      title: 'مبروك $playerName!',
      body: 'وصل كورسك الجديد $sourceLabel: "$courseName"\nابدأ تمرينك الآن',
      channelId: 'course_arrived_channel',
      channelName: 'كورسات جديدة',
      payload: courseId,
      priority: Priority.max,
    );
  }

  Future<void> showCoursePaused(
      {required String playerName, String? reason, String? courseId}) async {
    await _show(
      id: 1002,
      title: 'تم إيقاف كورسك',
      body:
          'مرحباً $playerName، تم إيقاف كورسك مؤقتاً${reason != null ? ': $reason' : ''}.',
      channelId: 'course_status_channel',
      channelName: 'تحديثات حالة الكورس',
      payload: courseId,
    );
  }

  Future<void> showCourseResumed(
      {required String playerName, String? courseId}) async {
    await _show(
      id: 1003,
      title: 'تم تفعيل كورسك',
      body: 'مرحباً $playerName، تم إعادة تفعيل كورسك.',
      channelId: 'course_status_channel',
      channelName: 'تحديثات حالة الكورس',
      payload: courseId,
    );
  }

  Future<void> showCourseCompleted(
      {required String playerName, String? courseId}) async {
    await _show(
      id: 1004,
      title: 'أحسنت $playerName!',
      body: 'لقد أكملت كورسك بنجاح!',
      channelId: 'course_status_channel',
      channelName: 'تحديثات حالة الكورس',
      payload: courseId,
    );
  }

  Future<void> showCourseUpdated(
      {required String playerName, String? courseId}) async {
    await _show(
      id: 1005,
      title: 'تم تحديث كورسك',
      body: 'مرحباً $playerName، تم تحديث بيانات كورسك.',
      channelId: 'course_status_channel',
      channelName: 'تحديثات حالة الكورس',
      payload: courseId,
    );
  }

  Future<void> _show({
    required int id,
    required String title,
    required String body,
    required String channelId,
    required String channelName,
    String? payload,
    Priority priority = Priority.high,
  }) async {
    final androidDetails = AndroidNotificationDetails(
      channelId,
      channelName,
      channelDescription: channelName,
      importance: Importance.max,
      priority: priority,
      playSound: true,
      enableVibration: true,
      styleInformation: BigTextStyleInformation(body),
    );
    const iosDetails = DarwinNotificationDetails(
        presentAlert: true, presentBadge: true, presentSound: true);
    await _plugin.show(id, title, body,
        NotificationDetails(android: androidDetails, iOS: iosDetails),
        payload: payload);
  }

  Future<void> cancelAll() async {
    await _plugin.cancelAll();
  }
}

// ============================================================
// COURSE WATCHER SERVICE
// ============================================================
class CourseWatcherService {
  CourseWatcherService._();
  static final CourseWatcherService instance = CourseWatcherService._();
  static const _kSeenCourses = 'watcher_seen_courses_v1';
  static const _kSeenStatuses = 'watcher_seen_statuses_v1';
  final Set<String> _seenCourseIds = {};
  final Map<String, String> _seenStatuses = {};
  bool _started = false;
  String? _uid;

  Future<void> start({
    required String uid,
    required Stream<List<dynamic>> courseStream,
  }) async {
    if (_started && _uid == uid) return;
    _started = true;
    _uid = uid;
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    _seenCourseIds
      ..clear()
      ..addAll(prefs.getStringList(_kSeenCourses) ?? []);
    _seenStatuses.clear();
    final raw = prefs.getString(_kSeenStatuses);
    if (raw != null) {
      try {
        final map = jsonDecode(raw) as Map;
        map.forEach((k, v) => _seenStatuses[k.toString()] = v.toString());
      } catch (_) {}
    }
    courseStream.listen((list) {
      _processCourses(list, prefs);
    }, onError: (e) => debugPrint('CourseWatcher: $e'));
  }

  Future<void> _processCourses(
      List<dynamic> courses, SharedPreferences prefs) async {
    bool changed = false;
    for (final course in courses) {
      final String id = course.id;
      final String status = course.status;
      final String playerName = course.player.name;
      final String source = course.source;
      final String courseName =
          course.days.isNotEmpty ? course.days.first.name : 'كورس جديد';
      final isNew = !_seenCourseIds.contains(id);
      final oldStatus = _seenStatuses[id];

      if (isNew) {
        await NotificationService.instance.showCourseArrived(
          playerName: playerName,
          courseName: courseName,
          source: source,
          courseId: id,
        );
        _seenCourseIds.add(id);
        _seenStatuses[id] = status;
        changed = true;
      } else if (oldStatus != null && oldStatus != status) {
        if (status == 'paused') {
          await NotificationService.instance
              .showCoursePaused(playerName: playerName, courseId: id);
        } else if (status == 'active' && oldStatus == 'paused') {
          await NotificationService.instance
              .showCourseResumed(playerName: playerName, courseId: id);
        } else if (status == 'completed') {
          await NotificationService.instance
              .showCourseCompleted(playerName: playerName, courseId: id);
        }
        _seenStatuses[id] = status;
        changed = true;
      }
    }
    if (changed) {
      await prefs.setStringList(_kSeenCourses, _seenCourseIds.toList());
      await prefs.setString(_kSeenStatuses, jsonEncode(_seenStatuses));
    }
  }

  Future<void> reset() async {
    _seenCourseIds.clear();
    _seenStatuses.clear();
    _started = false;
    _uid = null;
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    await prefs.remove(_kSeenCourses);
    await prefs.remove(_kSeenStatuses);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MODELS
// ============================================================
const uuid = Uuid();

class Exercise {
  String id;
  String nameAr;
  String nameEn;
  String primaryMuscle;
  List<String> secondaryMuscles;
  String type;
  String equipment;
  String difficulty;
  String description;
  String? videoUrl;
  String? imageUrl;
  String createdBy;
  String createdByUid;

  Exercise({
    String? id,
    required this.nameAr,
    required this.nameEn,
    required this.primaryMuscle,
    this.secondaryMuscles = const [],
    required this.type,
    required this.equipment,
    required this.difficulty,
    this.description = '',
    this.videoUrl,
    this.imageUrl,
    this.createdBy = 'user',
    this.createdByUid = '',
  }) : id = id ?? uuid.v4();

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'nameAr': nameAr,
        'nameEn': nameEn,
        'primaryMuscle': primaryMuscle,
        'secondaryMuscles': secondaryMuscles,
        'type': type,
        'equipment': equipment,
        'difficulty': difficulty,
        'description': description,
        'videoUrl': videoUrl,
        'imageUrl': imageUrl,
        'createdBy': createdBy,
        'createdByUid': createdByUid,
      };

  factory Exercise.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => Exercise(
        id: j['id'],
        nameAr: j['nameAr'] ?? '',
        nameEn: j['nameEn'] ?? '',
        primaryMuscle: j['primaryMuscle'] ?? '',
        secondaryMuscles: List<String>.from(j['secondaryMuscles'] ?? const []),
        type: j['type'] ?? '',
        equipment: j['equipment'] ?? '',
        difficulty: j['difficulty'] ?? '',
        description: j['description'] ?? '',
        videoUrl: j['videoUrl'],
        imageUrl: j['imageUrl'],
        createdBy: j['createdBy'] ?? 'user',
        createdByUid: j['createdByUid'] ?? '',
      );
}

class ExerciseSet {
  int reps;
  ExerciseSet({required this.reps});

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {'reps': reps};

  factory ExerciseSet.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => ExerciseSet(
        reps: j['reps'] ?? 10,
      );
}

class SubExercise {
  String id;
  String nameAr;
  String nameEn;
  String primaryMuscle;
  List<ExerciseSet> sets;
  String notes;
  int restSeconds;
  String? videoUrl;
  String? imageUrl;
  String? description;

  SubExercise({
    String? id,
    required this.nameAr,
    this.nameEn = '',
    this.primaryMuscle = '',
    List<ExerciseSet>? sets,
    this.notes = '',
    this.restSeconds = 60,
    this.videoUrl,
    this.imageUrl,
    this.description,
  })  : id = id ?? uuid.v4(),
        sets = sets ?? [ExerciseSet(reps: 10)];

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'nameAr': nameAr,
        'nameEn': nameEn,
        'primaryMuscle': primaryMuscle,
        'sets': sets.map((s) => s.toJson()).toList(),
        'notes': notes,
        'restSeconds': restSeconds,
        'videoUrl': videoUrl,
        'imageUrl': imageUrl,
        'description': description,
      };

  factory SubExercise.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => SubExercise(
        id: j['id'],
        nameAr: j['nameAr'] ?? '',
        nameEn: j['nameEn'] ?? '',
        primaryMuscle: j['primaryMuscle'] ?? '',
        sets: (j['sets'] as List? ?? [])
            .map((e) => ExerciseSet.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(e)))
            .toList(),
        notes: j['notes'] ?? '',
        restSeconds: j['restSeconds'] ?? 60,
        videoUrl: j['videoUrl'],
        imageUrl: j['imageUrl'],
        description: j['description'],
      );
}

class DayExercise {
  String id;
  String exerciseIdSnapshot;
  String nameAr;
  String nameEn;
  String primaryMuscle;
  String notes;
  int restSeconds;
  List<ExerciseSet> sets;
  bool done;
  String? videoUrl;
  String? imageUrl;
  String? description;
  bool isSuper;
  List<SubExercise> subExercises;

  DayExercise({
    String? id,
    required this.exerciseIdSnapshot,
    required this.nameAr,
    required this.nameEn,
    required this.primaryMuscle,
    this.notes = '',
    this.restSeconds = 60,
    List<ExerciseSet>? sets,
    this.done = false,
    this.videoUrl,
    this.imageUrl,
    this.description,
    this.isSuper = false,
    List<SubExercise>? subExercises,
  })  : id = id ?? uuid.v4(),
        sets = sets ?? [ExerciseSet(reps: 10)],
        subExercises = subExercises ?? [];

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'exerciseIdSnapshot': exerciseIdSnapshot,
        'nameAr': nameAr,
        'nameEn': nameEn,
        'primaryMuscle': primaryMuscle,
        'notes': notes,
        'restSeconds': restSeconds,
        'sets': sets.map((s) => s.toJson()).toList(),
        'done': done,
        'videoUrl': videoUrl,
        'imageUrl': imageUrl,
        'description': description,
        'isSuper': isSuper,
        'subExercises': subExercises.map((s) => s.toJson()).toList(),
      };

  factory DayExercise.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => DayExercise(
        id: j['id'],
        exerciseIdSnapshot: j['exerciseIdSnapshot'] ?? '',
        nameAr: j['nameAr'] ?? '',
        nameEn: j['nameEn'] ?? '',
        primaryMuscle: j['primaryMuscle'] ?? '',
        notes: j['notes'] ?? '',
        restSeconds: j['restSeconds'] ?? 60,
        sets: (j['sets'] as List? ?? [])
            .map((e) => ExerciseSet.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(e)))
            .toList(),
        done: j['done'] ?? false,
        videoUrl: j['videoUrl'],
        imageUrl: j['imageUrl'],
        description: j['description'],
        isSuper: j['isSuper'] ?? false,
        subExercises: (j['subExercises'] as List? ?? [])
            .map((e) => SubExercise.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(e)))
            .toList(),
      );

  int get totalSets => isSuper
      ? subExercises.fold<int>(0, (sum, s) => sum + s.sets.length)
      : sets.length;

  String get summaryLine {
    if (isSuper) {
      return subExercises.map((s) {
        final reps = s.sets.isNotEmpty ? s.sets.first.reps : 0;
        return '${s.nameAr} ${s.sets.length}×$reps';
      }).join(' + ');
    }
    final reps = sets.isNotEmpty ? sets.first.reps : 0;
    return '${sets.length} × $reps';
  }
}

class WorkoutDay {
  String id;
  int number;
  String name;
  String notes;
  List<DayExercise> exercises;

  WorkoutDay({
    String? id,
    required this.number,
    required this.name,
    this.notes = '',
    List<DayExercise>? exercises,
  })  : id = id ?? uuid.v4(),
        exercises = exercises ?? [];

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'number': number,
        'name': name,
        'notes': notes,
        'exercises': exercises.map((e) => e.toJson()).toList(),
      };

  factory WorkoutDay.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => WorkoutDay(
        id: j['id'],
        number: j['number'] ?? 1,
        name: j['name'] ?? '',
        notes: j['notes'] ?? '',
        exercises: (j['exercises'] as List? ?? [])
            .map((e) => DayExercise.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(e)))
            .toList(),
      );
}

class Player {
  String id;
  String name;
  int age;
  double weight;
  double height;
  String gender;
  DateTime startDate;
  DateTime endDate;
  String goal;
  String notes;
  String activityLevel;
  double dailyCaloriesIntake;

  Player({
    String? id,
    required this.name,
    required this.age,
    required this.weight,
    required this.height,
    this.gender = 'male',
    required this.startDate,
    required this.endDate,
    required this.goal,
    this.notes = '',
    this.activityLevel = 'moderate',
    this.dailyCaloriesIntake = 0,
  }) : id = id ?? uuid.v4();

  bool get isFemale => gender == 'female';

  double get bmr {
    if (weight <= 0 || height <= 0 || age <= 0) return 0;
    final base = 10 * weight + 6.25 * height - 5 * age;
    return isFemale ? base - 161 : base + 5;
  }

  double get activityFactor {
    switch (activityLevel) {
      case 'sedentary':
        return 1.2;
      case 'light':
        return 1.375;
      case 'moderate':
        return 1.55;
      case 'active':
        return 1.725;
      case 'veryActive':
        return 1.9;
      default:
        return 1.55;
    }
  }

  double get dailyCaloriesNeeded => bmr * activityFactor;

  double get targetCalories {
    final tdee = dailyCaloriesNeeded;
    if (tdee <= 0) return 0;
    switch (goal) {
      case 'تضخيم':
        return tdee + 300;
      case 'تنشيف':
        return tdee - 400;
      case 'خسارة وزن':
        return tdee - 500;
      case 'زيادة قوة':
        return tdee + 200;
      case 'لياقة':
        return tdee;
      default:
        return tdee;
    }
  }

  double get calorieDifference => dailyCaloriesIntake - targetCalories;

  String get calorieStatus {
    if (dailyCaloriesIntake <= 0) return 'لم يُدخل';
    final diff = calorieDifference;
    if (diff.abs() <= 150) return 'مطابق تقريبًا';
    if (diff > 0) return 'زيادة ${diff.round()} سعر حراري';
    return 'نقص ${diff.abs().round()} سعر حراري';
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'name': name,
        'age': age,
        'weight': weight,
        'height': height,
        'gender': gender,
        'startDate': startDate.toIso8601String(),
        'endDate': endDate.toIso8601String(),
        'goal': goal,
        'notes': notes,
        'activityLevel': activityLevel,
        'dailyCaloriesIntake': dailyCaloriesIntake,
      };

  factory Player.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => Player(
        id: j['id'],
        name: j['name'] ?? '',
        age: j['age'] ?? 0,
        weight: (j['weight'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0,
        height: (j['height'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0,
        gender: j['gender'] ?? 'male',
        startDate: DateTime.parse(j['startDate']),
        endDate: DateTime.parse(j['endDate']),
        goal: j['goal'] ?? '',
        notes: j['notes'] ?? '',
        activityLevel: j['activityLevel'] ?? 'moderate',
        dailyCaloriesIntake:
            (j['dailyCaloriesIntake'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0,
      );
}

class Course {
  String id;
  Player player;
  List<WorkoutDay> days;
  String status;
  DateTime createdAt;
  String ownerId;
  String targetUserId;
  String source;
  String ownerName;
  String ownerRole;
  String ownerPhone;
  bool isPrivate;

  Course({
    String? id,
    required this.player,
    required this.days,
    this.status = 'not_started',
    DateTime? createdAt,
    this.ownerId = '',
    this.targetUserId = '',
    this.source = 'user',
    this.ownerName = '',
    this.ownerRole = '',
    this.ownerPhone = '',
    this.isPrivate = false,
  })  : id = id ?? uuid.v4(),
        createdAt = createdAt ?? DateTime.now();

  double get progress {
    int total = 0, done = 0;
    for (final d in days) {
      for (final e in d.exercises) {
        total++;
        if (e.done) done++;
      }
    }
    return total == 0 ? 0 : done / total;
  }

  int get totalExercises {
    int t = 0;
    for (final d in days) {
      t += d.exercises.length;
    }
    return t;
  }

  int get doneExercises {
    int t = 0;
    for (final d in days) {
      for (final e in d.exercises) {
        if (e.done) t++;
      }
    }
    return t;
  }

  bool get isPaused => status == 'paused';
  bool get isActive => status == 'active';
  bool get isCompleted => status == 'completed';

  String get coachLabel {
    if (ownerName.isEmpty) return '';
    final role = ownerRole == 'superAdmin'
        ? 'إشراف عام'
        : ownerRole == 'admin'
            ? 'مدير'
            : ownerRole == 'captain'
                ? 'كابتن'
                : '';
    if (role.isEmpty) return ownerName;
    return '$role: $ownerName';
  }

  int get currentDayIndex {
    if (days.isEmpty) return -1;
    final start = DateTime(
        player.startDate.year, player.startDate.month, player.startDate.day);
    final today = DateTime.now();
    final todayDate = DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day);
    final diff = todayDate.difference(start).inDays;
    if (diff < 0) return 0;
    return diff % days.length;
  }

  WorkoutDay? get todayDay {
    final idx = currentDayIndex;
    if (idx < 0 || idx >= days.length) return null;
    return days[idx];
  }

  bool get completedToday {
    final d = todayDay;
    if (d == null || d.exercises.isEmpty) return false;
    return d.exercises.every((e) => e.done);
  }

  double get todayProgress {
    final d = todayDay;
    if (d == null || d.exercises.isEmpty) return 0;
    final done = d.exercises.where((e) => e.done).length;
    return done / d.exercises.length;
  }

  int get daysSinceStart {
    final start = DateTime(
        player.startDate.year, player.startDate.month, player.startDate.day);
    final today = DateTime.now();
    final todayDate = DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day);
    final d = todayDate.difference(start).inDays;
    return d < 0 ? 0 : d;
  }

  double get expectedProgress {
    if (days.isEmpty) return 0;
    final totalDays = days.length;
    final elapsed = daysSinceStart + 1;
    if (elapsed >= totalDays) return 1.0;
    return elapsed / totalDays;
  }

  int get adherenceScore {
    final expected = expectedProgress;
    if (expected <= 0) return 0;
    final ratio = progress / expected;
    final score = (ratio * 100).round();
    return score.clamp(0, 100);
  }

  String get adherenceLabel {
    final s = adherenceScore;
    if (s >= 90) return 'ممتاز';
    if (s >= 70) return 'جيد جداً';
    if (s >= 50) return 'متوسط';
    if (s >= 25) return 'ضعيف';
    return 'متأخر جداً';
  }

  Color get adherenceColor {
    final s = adherenceScore;
    if (s >= 90) return AppColors.success;
    if (s >= 70) return AppColors.primary;
    if (s >= 50) return const Color(0xFFF59E0B);
    if (s >= 25) return AppColors.error;
    return const Color(0xFF991B1B);
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'player': player.toJson(),
        'days': days.map((d) => d.toJson()).toList(),
        'status': status,
        'createdAt': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
        'ownerId': ownerId,
        'targetUserId': targetUserId,
        'source': source,
        'ownerName': ownerName,
        'ownerRole': ownerRole,
        'ownerPhone': ownerPhone,
        'isPrivate': isPrivate,
      };

  factory Course.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => Course(
        id: j['id'],
        player: Player.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(j['player'])),
        days: (j['days'] as List? ?? [])
            .map((e) => WorkoutDay.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(e)))
            .toList(),
        status: j['status'] ?? 'not_started',
        createdAt: DateTime.parse(j['createdAt']),
        ownerId: j['ownerId'] ?? '',
        targetUserId: j['targetUserId'] ?? '',
        source: j['source'] ?? 'user',
        ownerName: j['ownerName'] ?? '',
        ownerRole: j['ownerRole'] ?? '',
        ownerPhone: j['ownerPhone'] ?? '',
        isPrivate: j['isPrivate'] == true,
      );
}

class CourseMessage {
  String id;
  String senderId;
  String senderName;
  String senderRole;
  String text;
  DateTime createdAt;

  CourseMessage({
    String? id,
    required this.senderId,
    required this.senderName,
    required this.senderRole,
    required this.text,
    DateTime? createdAt,
  })  : id = id ?? uuid.v4(),
        createdAt = createdAt ?? DateTime.now();

  bool get isFromCoach =>
      senderRole == 'superAdmin' ||
      senderRole == 'admin' ||
      senderRole == 'captain';

  String get senderRoleLabel {
    switch (senderRole) {
      case 'superAdmin':
        return 'إشراف عام';
      case 'admin':
        return 'مدير';
      case 'captain':
        return 'كابتن';
      default:
        return 'لاعب';
    }
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'senderId': senderId,
        'senderName': senderName,
        'senderRole': senderRole,
        'text': text,
        'createdAt': Timestamp.fromDate(createdAt),
      };

  factory CourseMessage.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => CourseMessage(
        id: j['id'],
        senderId: j['senderId'] ?? '',
        senderName: j['senderName'] ?? '',
        senderRole: j['senderRole'] ?? 'user',
        text: j['text'] ?? '',
        createdAt: j['createdAt'] is Timestamp
            ? (j['createdAt'] as Timestamp).toDate()
            : DateTime.now(),
      );
}

class UserProfile {
  String uid;
  String email;
  String displayName;
  String role;
  bool emailVerified;
  bool isVerified;
  DateTime? subscriptionStart;
  DateTime? subscriptionEnd;
  String subscriptionStatus;
  String? phoneNumber;
  String? photoUrl;
  int? playerAge;
  double? playerWeight;
  double? playerHeight;
  DateTime? createdAt;

  UserProfile({
    required this.uid,
    required this.email,
    required this.displayName,
    this.role = 'user',
    this.emailVerified = false,
    this.isVerified = false,
    this.subscriptionStart,
    this.subscriptionEnd,
    this.subscriptionStatus = 'none',
    this.phoneNumber,
    this.photoUrl,
    this.playerAge,
    this.playerWeight,
    this.playerHeight,
    this.createdAt,
  });

  bool get isSuperAdmin => role == 'superAdmin';
  bool get isAdmin => role == 'admin';
  bool get isCaptain => role == 'captain';
  bool get canManageCourses => isSuperAdmin || isAdmin || isCaptain;
  bool get canAddServerExercises => isSuperAdmin || isAdmin || isCaptain;
  bool get canCreateAccounts => isSuperAdmin || isAdmin || isCaptain;
  bool get canViewAllStats => isSuperAdmin;
  bool get canManageAdmins => isSuperAdmin;
  bool get canManageUsers => isSuperAdmin || isAdmin;
  bool get isOwner => email.toLowerCase().trim() == kOwnerEmail.toLowerCase();

  bool canEditServerExercise(Exercise e) {
    if (isSuperAdmin || isAdmin) return true;
    if (isCaptain && e.createdByUid == uid) return true;
    return false;
  }

  bool canDeleteServerExercise(Exercise e) {
    if (isSuperAdmin || isAdmin) return true;
    if (isCaptain && e.createdByUid == uid) return true;
    return false;
  }

  String get roleLabel {
    switch (role) {
      case 'superAdmin':
        return 'إشراف عام';
      case 'admin':
        return 'مدير';
      case 'captain':
        return 'كابتن';
      default:
        return 'لاعب';
    }
  }

  bool get isTrial => subscriptionStatus == 'trial';

  bool get hasActiveSubscription {
    if (canManageCourses) return true;
    if (subscriptionEnd == null) return false;
    return subscriptionEnd!.isAfter(DateTime.now()) &&
        (subscriptionStatus == 'active' || subscriptionStatus == 'trial');
  }

  bool get isExpired {
    if (canManageCourses) return false;
    if (subscriptionEnd == null) return true;
    return subscriptionEnd!.isBefore(DateTime.now());
  }

  bool get needsActivation {
    if (canManageCourses) return false;
    return subscriptionStatus == 'none' &&
        subscriptionStart == null &&
        subscriptionEnd == null;
  }

  int get daysRemaining {
    if (subscriptionEnd == null) return 0;
    final d = subscriptionEnd!.difference(DateTime.now()).inDays;
    return d > 0 ? d : 0;
  }

  String get subscriptionLabel {
    if (isSuperAdmin) return 'إشراف عام';
    if (isAdmin) return 'مسؤول';
    if (isCaptain) return 'كابتن • صلاحيات إدارة';
    if (isTrial && hasActiveSubscription) {
      return 'تجريبي • $daysRemaining يوم';
    }
    if (hasActiveSubscription) return 'نشط • $daysRemaining يوم';
    if (isExpired) return 'منتهي';
    return 'غير مفعّل';
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'uid': uid,
        'email': email,
        'displayName': displayName,
        'role': role,
        'emailVerified': emailVerified,
        'isVerified': isVerified,
        'subscription': {
          'startDate': subscriptionStart != null
              ? Timestamp.fromDate(subscriptionStart!)
              : null,
          'endDate': subscriptionEnd != null
              ? Timestamp.fromDate(subscriptionEnd!)
              : null,
          'status': subscriptionStatus,
        },
        'phoneNumber': phoneNumber,
        'photoUrl': photoUrl,
        if (playerAge != null || playerWeight != null || playerHeight != null)
          'playerInfo': {
            'age': playerAge,
            'weight': playerWeight,
            'height': playerHeight,
          },
      };

  factory UserProfile.fromDoc(DocumentSnapshot doc) {
    final d = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
    final sub = d['subscription'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
    final pi = d['playerInfo'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
    return UserProfile(
      uid: doc.id,
      email: d['email'] ?? '',
      displayName: d['displayName'] ?? '',
      role: d['role'] ?? 'user',
      emailVerified: d['emailVerified'] ?? false,
      isVerified: (d['isVerified'] ?? d['emailVerified'] ?? false) as bool,
      subscriptionStart: sub?['startDate'] != null
          ? (sub!['startDate'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : null,
      subscriptionEnd: sub?['endDate'] != null
          ? (sub!['endDate'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : null,
      subscriptionStatus: sub?['status'] ?? 'none',
      phoneNumber: d['phoneNumber'],
      photoUrl: d['photoUrl'],
      playerAge: (pi?['age'] as num?)?.toInt(),
      playerWeight: (pi?['weight'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      playerHeight: (pi?['height'] as num?)?.toDouble(),
      createdAt: d['createdAt'] is Timestamp
          ? (d['createdAt'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : null,
    );
  }
}

class CoachStats {
  final UserProfile coach;
  final int totalCourses;
  final int activeCourses;
  final int pausedCourses;
  final int completedCourses;
  final int playersCreated;
  final int totalPlayersWithCourses;
  final int totalExercises;
  final int doneExercises;
  final double avgAdherence;
  final DateTime? lastCourseAt;
  final List<Course> courses;
  final List<UserProfile> players;

  const CoachStats({
    required this.coach,
    required this.totalCourses,
    required this.activeCourses,
    required this.pausedCourses,
    required this.completedCourses,
    required this.playersCreated,
    required this.totalPlayersWithCourses,
    required this.totalExercises,
    required this.doneExercises,
    required this.avgAdherence,
    required this.lastCourseAt,
    required this.courses,
    required this.players,
  });

  double get progressRatio =>
      totalExercises == 0 ? 0 : doneExercises / totalExercises;
  int get adherenceRounded => avgAdherence.round().clamp(0, 100);

  String get adherenceLabel {
    final s = adherenceRounded;
    if (s >= 90) return 'ممتاز';
    if (s >= 70) return 'جيد جداً';
    if (s >= 50) return 'متوسط';
    if (s >= 25) return 'ضعيف';
    return 'متأخر';
  }

  Color get adherenceColor {
    final s = adherenceRounded;
    if (s >= 90) return AppColors.success;
    if (s >= 70) return AppColors.primary;
    if (s >= 50) return const Color(0xFFF59E0B);
    if (s >= 25) return AppColors.error;
    return const Color(0xFF991B1B);
  }

  int get malePlayers => players.where((p) => _genderOf(p) == 'male').length;
  int get femalePlayers =>
      players.where((p) => _genderOf(p) == 'female').length;

  String _genderOf(UserProfile p) {
    for (final c in courses) {
      if (c.targetUserId == p.uid) return c.player.gender;
    }
    return 'male';
  }
}

class GlobalStats {
  final int totalUsers;
  final int superAdmins;
  final int admins;
  final int captains;
  final int players;
  final int verified;
  final int malePlayers;
  final int femalePlayers;
  final int totalCourses;
  final int activeCourses;
  final int pausedCourses;
  final int completedCourses;
  final int totalExercises;

  const GlobalStats({
    required this.totalUsers,
    required this.superAdmins,
    required this.admins,
    required this.captains,
    required this.players,
    required this.verified,
    required this.malePlayers,
    required this.femalePlayers,
    required this.totalCourses,
    required this.activeCourses,
    required this.pausedCourses,
    required this.completedCourses,
    required this.totalExercises,
  });

  int get staffCount => superAdmins + admins + captains;
}

// 💳 PAYMENT MODELS & SERVICE (Alqaseh) - ديناميكي السعر
