class PaymentOrder {
  final String orderId;
  final String? paymentId;
  final String userId;
  final int amount;
  final int days;
  final String status;
  final String? paymentUrl;
  final DateTime? createdAt;

  PaymentOrder({
    required this.orderId,
    this.paymentId,
    required this.userId,
    required this.amount,
    required this.days,
    required this.status,
    this.paymentUrl,
    this.createdAt,
  });

  factory PaymentOrder.fromDoc(DocumentSnapshot doc) {
    final d = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
    return PaymentOrder(
      orderId: d['orderId'] ?? doc.id,
      paymentId: d['paymentId'],
      userId: d['userId'] ?? '',
      amount: (d['amount'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 5000,
      days: (d['days'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 90,
      status: d['status'] ?? 'pending',
      paymentUrl: d['paymentUrl'],
      createdAt: d['createdAt'] is Timestamp
          ? (d['createdAt'] as Timestamp).toDate()
          : null,
    );
  }
}

class PaymentService {
  PaymentService._();
  static final PaymentService instance = PaymentService._();

  Future<int> getPrice() async =>
      await FirebaseService.instance.getSubscriptionPrice();
  Future<int> getDays() async => 90;

  Future<String?> createPaymentAndGetUrl() async {
    try {
      final price = await getPrice();
      final callable = FirebaseFunctions.instance.httpsCallable(
        'createAlqasehPayment',
        options: HttpsCallableOptions(timeout: const Duration(seconds: 60)),
      );
      final result = await callable.call({'amount': price});
      final data = Map<String, dynamic>.from(result.data as Map);
      if (data['success'] == true && data['paymentUrl'] != null) {
        return data['paymentUrl'].toString();
      }
      debugPrint('PaymentService: ${data['message']}');
      return null;
    } on FirebaseFunctionsException catch (e) {
      debugPrint('PaymentService FirebaseFunctionsException: ${e.message}');
      return null;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('PaymentService Exception: $e');
      return null;
    }
  }

  Future<PaymentVerifyResult> verifyPayment({String? orderId}) async {
    try {
      final callable = FirebaseFunctions.instance.httpsCallable(
        'verifyAlqasehPayment',
        options: HttpsCallableOptions(timeout: const Duration(seconds: 60)),
      );
      final result = await callable.call({'orderId': orderId});
      final data = Map<String, dynamic>.from(result.data as Map);
      return PaymentVerifyResult(
        success: data['success'] == true,
        message: (data['message'] ?? '').toString(),
      );
    } on FirebaseFunctionsException catch (e) {
      return PaymentVerifyResult(
        success: false,
        message: e.message ?? 'فشل التحقق من الدفع',
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return PaymentVerifyResult(
        success: false,
        message: 'حدث خطأ غير متوقع: $e',
      );
    }
  }
}

class PaymentVerifyResult {
  final bool success;
  final String message;
  PaymentVerifyResult({required this.success, required this.message});
}

// ============================================================
// STORAGE SERVICE
// ============================================================
class StorageService {
  StorageService._();
  static final StorageService instance = StorageService._();
  static const _kLocalExercises = 'local_exercises_v1';
  static const _kLocalCourses = 'local_courses_v1';
  static const _kCourseProgress = 'course_progress_v1';
  late SharedPreferences _prefs;

  Future<void> init() async {
    _prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  }

  List<Exercise> loadLocalExercises() {
    final raw = _prefs.getString(_kLocalExercises);
    if (raw == null) return [];
    try {
      final list = jsonDecode(raw) as List;
      return list
          .map((e) => Exercise.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(e)))
          .toList();
    } catch (_) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveLocalExercises(List<Exercise> list) async {
    await _prefs.setString(
        _kLocalExercises, jsonEncode(list.map((e) => e.toJson()).toList()));
  }

  Future<void> upsertLocalExercise(Exercise e) async {
    final list = loadLocalExercises();
    final idx = list.indexWhere((x) => x.id == e.id);
    if (idx >= 0) {
      list[idx] = e;
    } else {
      list.add(e);
    }
    await saveLocalExercises(list);
  }

  Future<void> deleteLocalExercise(String id) async {
    final list = loadLocalExercises()..removeWhere((e) => e.id == id);
    await saveLocalExercises(list);
  }

  List<Course> loadLocalCourses() {
    final raw = _prefs.getString(_kLocalCourses);
    if (raw == null) return [];
    try {
      final list = jsonDecode(raw) as List;
      return list
          .map((e) => Course.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(e)))
          .toList();
    } catch (_) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveLocalCourses(List<Course> list) async {
    await _prefs.setString(
        _kLocalCourses, jsonEncode(list.map((c) => c.toJson()).toList()));
  }

  Future<void> upsertLocalCourse(Course c) async {
    final list = loadLocalCourses();
    final idx = list.indexWhere((x) => x.id == c.id);
    if (idx >= 0) {
      list[idx] = c;
    } else {
      list.add(c);
    }
    await saveLocalCourses(list);
  }

  Future<void> deleteLocalCourse(String id) async {
    final list = loadLocalCourses()..removeWhere((c) => c.id == id);
    await saveLocalCourses(list);
    await clearCourseProgress(id);
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> _loadAllProgress() {
    final raw = _prefs.getString(_kCourseProgress);
    if (raw == null) return {};
    try {
      return Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(raw) as Map);
    } catch (_) {
      return {};
    }
  }

  Map<String, bool> loadCourseProgress(String courseId) {
    final all = _loadAllProgress();
    final raw = all[courseId];
    if (raw == null) return {};
    try {
      final m = Map<String, dynamic>.from(raw as Map);
      return m.map((k, v) => MapEntry(k, v == true));
    } catch (_) {
      return {};
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveExerciseDone({
    required String courseId,
    required String exerciseId,
    required bool done,
  }) async {
    final all = _loadAllProgress();
    final cp = Map<String, dynamic>.from((all[courseId] as Map?) ?? {});
    cp[exerciseId] = done;
    all[courseId] = cp;
    await _prefs.setString(_kCourseProgress, jsonEncode(all));
  }

  Future<void> applyProgressToCourse(Course course) async {
    final p = loadCourseProgress(course.id);
    if (p.isEmpty) return;
    for (final d in course.days) {
      for (final e in d.exercises) {
        final saved = p[e.id];
        if (saved != null) e.done = saved;
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> clearCourseProgress(String courseId) async {
    final all = _loadAllProgress();
    all.remove(courseId);
    await _prefs.setString(_kCourseProgress, jsonEncode(all));
  }
}

// ============================================================
// FIREBASE SERVICE
// ============================================================
class FirebaseService {
  FirebaseService._();
  static final FirebaseService instance = FirebaseService._();
  static const int kMaxCourseMessages = 20;

  final auth = FirebaseAuth.instance;
  final firestore = FirebaseFirestore.instance;
  final storage = FirebaseStorage.instance;

  User? get currentUser => auth.currentUser;

  Future<UserCredential> signIn(String email, String password) =>
      auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email: email, password: password);

  Future<void> signOut() async {
    await CourseWatcherService.instance.reset();
    await auth.signOut();
  }

  Future<UserCredential> signUp({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String displayName,
    String? phoneNumber,
    int trialDays = 7,
  }) async {
    final lowerEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
    final cred = await auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
      email: lowerEmail,
      password: password,
    );
    final user = cred.user!;
    await user.updateDisplayName(displayName);
    try {
      await user.sendEmailVerification();
    } catch (_) {}

    final now = DateTime.now();
    final trialEnd = now.add(Duration(days: trialDays));
    await firestore.collection('users').doc(user.uid).set({
      'email': lowerEmail,
      'displayName': displayName,
      'phoneNumber': phoneNumber,
      'role': 'user',
      'emailVerified': false,
      'isVerified': false,
      'subscription': {
        'startDate': Timestamp.fromDate(now),
        'endDate': Timestamp.fromDate(trialEnd),
        'status': 'trial',
        'plan': '${trialDays}_day_trial',
        'activatedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      },
      'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
    return cred;
  }

  Future<void> resendVerificationEmail() async {
    final user = auth.currentUser;
    if (user == null) throw Exception('لا يوجد مستخدم مسجّل');
    if (user.emailVerified) return;
    await user.sendEmailVerification();
  }

  Future<void> refreshEmailVerifiedStatus() async {
    final user = auth.currentUser;
    if (user == null) return;
    await user.reload();
    final refreshed = auth.currentUser;
    if (refreshed == null) return;
    final patch = <String, dynamic>{
      'emailVerified': refreshed.emailVerified,
    };
    if (refreshed.emailVerified) {
      patch['isVerified'] = true;
    }
    await firestore.collection('users').doc(refreshed.uid).update(patch);
  }

  Future<void> sendPasswordResetEmail(String email) async {
    final lowerEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
    await auth.sendPasswordResetEmail(email: lowerEmail);
  }

  Stream<UserProfile?> userProfileStream() {
    final uid = currentUser?.uid;
    if (uid == null) return Stream.value(null);
    return firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).snapshots().map(
          (doc) => doc.exists ? UserProfile.fromDoc(doc) : null,
        );
  }

  Future<UserProfile?> getUserProfile() async {
    final uid = currentUser?.uid;
    if (uid == null) return null;
    final doc = await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).get();
    return doc.exists ? UserProfile.fromDoc(doc) : null;
  }

  Future<UserProfile?> getUserProfileById(String uid) async {
    if (uid.isEmpty) return null;
    final doc = await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).get();
    return doc.exists ? UserProfile.fromDoc(doc) : null;
  }

  Stream<UserProfile?> userProfileByIdStream(String uid) {
    if (uid.isEmpty) return Stream.value(null);
    return firestore
        .collection('users')
        .doc(uid)
        .snapshots()
        .map((doc) => doc.exists ? UserProfile.fromDoc(doc) : null);
  }

  Future<void> setUserVerified(String uid, bool verified) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || (!me.isAdmin && !me.isSuperAdmin)) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح: الإدارة فقط');
    }
    await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).update({
      'isVerified': verified,
    });
  }

  Future<void> updateMyPlayerInfo({
    required int age,
    required double weight,
    required double height,
  }) async {
    final uid = currentUser?.uid;
    if (uid == null) throw Exception('لا يوجد مستخدم');
    await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).update({
      'playerInfo': {
        'age': age,
        'weight': weight,
        'height': height,
        'updatedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      },
    });

    final coursesSnap = await firestore
        .collection('courses')
        .where('targetUserId', isEqualTo: uid)
        .get();
    if (coursesSnap.docs.isNotEmpty) {
      final batch = firestore.batch();
      for (final doc in coursesSnap.docs) {
        batch.update(doc.reference, {
          'player.age': age,
          'player.weight': weight,
          'player.height': height,
        });
      }
      await batch.commit();
    }
  }

  Future<String> createPlayerAccount({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String displayName,
    int activateDays = 365,
  }) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.canCreateAccounts) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    final lowerEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
    final existing = await firestore
        .collection('users')
        .where('email', isEqualTo: lowerEmail)
        .limit(1)
        .get();
    if (existing.docs.isNotEmpty) return existing.docs.first.id;

    FirebaseApp? secondaryApp;
    try {
      secondaryApp = await Firebase.initializeApp(
        name: 'player_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
        options: DefaultFirebaseOptions.currentPlatform,
      );
      final secondaryAuth = FirebaseAuth.instanceFor(app: secondaryApp);
      final cred = await secondaryAuth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
        email: lowerEmail,
        password: password,
      );
      final newUid = cred.user!.uid;
      await cred.user!.updateDisplayName(displayName);
      await secondaryAuth.signOut();

      final now = DateTime.now();
      final end = now.add(Duration(days: activateDays));
      await firestore.collection('users').doc(newUid).set({
        'email': lowerEmail,
        'displayName': displayName,
        'role': 'user',
        'emailVerified': true,
        'isVerified': true,
        'subscription': {
          'startDate': Timestamp.fromDate(now),
          'endDate': Timestamp.fromDate(end),
          'status': 'active',
          'plan': '${activateDays}_days',
          'activatedBy': me.uid,
          'activatedByRole': me.role,
          'activatedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        },
        'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        'createdBy': me.uid,
        'createdByRole': me.role,
      });
      return newUid;
    } finally {
      try {
        await secondaryApp?.delete();
      } catch (_) {}
    }
  }

  Stream<List<Exercise>> exercisesStream() {
    return firestore.collection('exercises').orderBy('nameAr').snapshots().map(
          (s) => s.docs
              .map((d) => Exercise.fromJson({...d.data(), 'id': d.id}))
              .toList(),
        );
  }

  Future<void> saveExercise(Exercise e, {bool isNew = false}) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.canAddServerExercises) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح: الإدارة أو الكابتن فقط');
    }
    if (!isNew) {
      final doc = await firestore.collection('exercises').doc(e.id).get();
      if (!doc.exists) throw Exception('التمرين غير موجود');
      final existing = Exercise.fromJson({...doc.data()!, 'id': doc.id});
      if (!me.canEditServerExercise(existing)) {
        throw Exception('غير مصرح: لا يمكنك تعديل هذا التمرين');
      }
      e.createdBy = existing.createdBy;
      e.createdByUid = existing.createdByUid;
    } else {
      e.createdBy = me.role;
      e.createdByUid = me.uid;
    }
    await firestore.collection('exercises').doc(e.id).set(e.toJson());
  }

  Future<void> deleteExercise(String id) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.canAddServerExercises) throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    final doc = await firestore.collection('exercises').doc(id).get();
    if (!doc.exists) throw Exception('التمرين غير موجود');
    final existing = Exercise.fromJson({...doc.data()!, 'id': doc.id});
    if (!me.canDeleteServerExercise(existing)) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح: لا يمكنك حذف هذا التمرين');
    }
    await firestore.collection('exercises').doc(id).delete();
  }

  Future<int> seedDefaultExercises({
    bool onlyMissing = true,
    void Function(int current, int total)? onProgress,
  }) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.canAddServerExercises) throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    final existingSnap = await firestore.collection('exercises').get();
    final existingNames = existingSnap.docs
        .map((d) => (d.data()['nameAr'] ?? '').toString())
        .toSet();

    int added = 0;
    final total = kDefaultExercises.length;
    int current = 0;
    final chunks = <List<DefaultExerciseData>>[];
    for (int i = 0; i < kDefaultExercises.length; i += 400) {
      chunks.add(kDefaultExercises.sublist(
          i,
          (i + 400 > kDefaultExercises.length)
              ? kDefaultExercises.length
              : i + 400));
    }

    for (final chunk in chunks) {
      final batch = firestore.batch();
      for (final data in chunk) {
        current++;
        onProgress?.call(current, total);
        if (onlyMissing && existingNames.contains(data.nameAr)) continue;

        final id = uuid.v4();
        final ref = firestore.collection('exercises').doc(id);
        final ex = Exercise(
          id: id,
          nameAr: data.nameAr,
          nameEn: data.nameEn,
          primaryMuscle: data.primaryMuscle,
          secondaryMuscles: data.secondaryMuscles,
          type: data.type,
          equipment: data.equipment,
          difficulty: data.difficulty,
          description: data.description,
          createdBy: me.role,
          createdByUid: me.uid,
        );
        batch.set(ref, ex.toJson());
        added++;
      }
      await batch.commit();
    }
    return added;
  }

  Stream<List<Course>> myCoursesStream() {
    final uid = currentUser!.uid;
    return firestore
        .collection('courses')
        .where('targetUserId', isEqualTo: uid)
        .snapshots()
        .map((s) {
      final list = s.docs
          .map((d) => Course.fromJson({...d.data(), 'id': d.id}))
          .toList();
      list.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.compareTo(a.createdAt));
      return list;
    });
  }

  Stream<List<Course>> myCreatedCoursesStream() {
    final uid = currentUser?.uid;
    if (uid == null) return Stream.value(const []);
    return firestore
        .collection('courses')
        .where('ownerId', isEqualTo: uid)
        .snapshots()
        .map((s) {
      final list = s.docs
          .map((d) => Course.fromJson({...d.data(), 'id': d.id}))
          .toList();
      list.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.compareTo(a.createdAt));
      return list;
    });
  }

  Stream<Course?> courseByIdStream(String courseId) {
    if (courseId.isEmpty) return Stream.value(null);
    return firestore.collection('courses').doc(courseId).snapshots().map(
          (doc) => doc.exists
              ? Course.fromJson({...doc.data()!, 'id': doc.id})
              : null,
        );
  }

  Future<void> syncCourseDaysToServer(Course course) async {
    final uid = currentUser?.uid;
    if (uid == null) return;
    try {
      final docRef = firestore.collection('courses').doc(course.id);
      final doc = await docRef.get();
      if (!doc.exists) return;
      final data = doc.data() ?? {};
      final targetUserId = (data['targetUserId'] ?? '').toString();
      final ownerId = (data['ownerId'] ?? '').toString();
      if (targetUserId != uid && ownerId != uid) return;

      await docRef.update({
        'days': course.days.map((d) => d.toJson()).toList(),
        'lastProgressUpdate': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('syncCourseDaysToServer ERROR: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveCourseForUser(Course course,
      {required String targetUid}) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.canManageCourses) throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    final map = course.toJson();
    map['ownerId'] = me.uid;
    map['targetUserId'] = targetUid;
    map['source'] = me.isSuperAdmin
        ? 'superAdmin'
        : me.isAdmin
            ? 'admin'
            : 'captain';
    map['ownerName'] = me.displayName;
    map['ownerRole'] = me.role;
    map['ownerPhone'] = me.phoneNumber ?? '';
    await firestore.collection('courses').doc(course.id).set(map);
  }

  Future<void> updateCourse(Course course) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.canManageCourses) throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    final doc = await firestore.collection('courses').doc(course.id).get();
    final ownerId = (doc.data()?['ownerId'] ?? '').toString();
    if (!me.isSuperAdmin && !me.isAdmin && ownerId != me.uid) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    final map = course.toJson();
    map['ownerId'] = doc.data()?['ownerId'] ?? me.uid;
    map['ownerName'] = doc.data()?['ownerName'] ?? me.displayName;
    map['ownerRole'] = doc.data()?['ownerRole'] ?? me.role;
    map['ownerPhone'] = doc.data()?['ownerPhone'] ?? (me.phoneNumber ?? '');
    await firestore.collection('courses').doc(course.id).update(map);
  }

  Future<void> setCourseStatus(String courseId, String status) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.canManageCourses) throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    final doc = await firestore.collection('courses').doc(courseId).get();
    final ownerId = (doc.data()?['ownerId'] ?? '').toString();
    if (!me.isSuperAdmin && !me.isAdmin && ownerId != me.uid) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    await firestore
        .collection('courses')
        .doc(courseId)
        .update({'status': status});
  }

  Future<void> deleteCourse(String id) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null) throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    final doc = await firestore.collection('courses').doc(id).get();
    final ownerId = (doc.data()?['ownerId'] ?? '').toString();
    final targetUserId = (doc.data()?['targetUserId'] ?? '').toString();
    if (!me.isSuperAdmin &&
        !me.isAdmin &&
        ownerId != me.uid &&
        targetUserId != me.uid) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    await firestore.collection('courses').doc(id).delete();
  }

  Stream<List<CourseMessage>> courseMessagesStream(String courseId) {

  Future<void> sendCourseMessage({
    required String courseId,
    required String text,
  }) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null) throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    final trimmed = text.trim();
    if (trimmed.isEmpty) throw Exception('الرسالة فارغة');
    if (trimmed.length > 500) {
      throw Exception('الرسالة طويلة جداً (500 حرف كحد أقصى)');
    }
    final coll =
        firestore.collection('courses').doc(courseId).collection('messages');
    final agg = await coll.count().get();
    final count = agg.count ?? 0;
    if (count >= kMaxCourseMessages) {
      throw Exception('تم الوصول للحد الأقصى ($kMaxCourseMessages رسالة).');
    }
    await coll.add({
      'senderId': me.uid,
      'senderName': me.displayName,
      'senderRole': me.role,
      'text': trimmed,
      'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  Future<void> deleteCourseMessage({
    required String courseId,
    required String messageId,
  }) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null) throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    final docRef = firestore
        .collection('courses')
        .doc(courseId)
        .collection('messages')
        .doc(messageId);
    final doc = await docRef.get();
    if (!doc.exists) return;
    final senderId = (doc.data()?['senderId'] ?? '').toString();
    if (!me.isSuperAdmin && !me.isAdmin && senderId != me.uid) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    await docRef.delete();
  }

  }

  Future<void> adminActivateSubscription({
    required String uid,
    required int days,
    String plan = 'custom',
  }) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || (!me.isAdmin && !me.isSuperAdmin)) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    final doc = await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).get();
    final data = doc.data() ?? {};
    final sub = data['subscription'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
    final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    DateTime? currentEnd;
    DateTime? currentStart;
    if (sub['endDate'] != null) {
      currentEnd = (sub['endDate'] as Timestamp).toDate();
    }
    if (sub['startDate'] != null) {
      currentStart = (sub['startDate'] as Timestamp).toDate();
    }
    final bool isCurrentlyActive =
        currentEnd != null && currentEnd.isAfter(now);
    final DateTime baseDate = isCurrentlyActive ? currentEnd : now;
    final DateTime newEnd = baseDate.add(Duration(days: days));
    final DateTime newStart =
        (isCurrentlyActive && currentStart != null) ? currentStart : now;

    await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).update({
      'subscription.startDate': Timestamp.fromDate(newStart),
      'subscription.endDate': Timestamp.fromDate(newEnd),
      'subscription.status': 'active',
      'subscription.plan': plan,
      'subscription.activatedBy': currentUser?.uid,
      'subscription.activatedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });

    try {
      final targetProfile = await getUserProfileById(uid);
      final logRef = firestore.collection('activation_logs').doc();
      await logRef.set({
        'logId': logRef.id,
        'userId': uid,
        'userName': targetProfile?.displayName ?? '',
        'userEmail': targetProfile?.email ?? '',
        'type': 'manual_activation',
        'source': me.role,
        'daysAdded': days,
        'wasExtension': isCurrentlyActive,
        'previousEndDate':
            currentEnd != null ? Timestamp.fromDate(currentEnd) : null,
        'newEndDate': Timestamp.fromDate(newEnd),
        'activatedByName': me.displayName,
        'activatedByUid': me.uid,
        'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });

      await logSystemActivity(
        action: isCurrentlyActive ? 'تمديد اشتراك' : 'تفعيل اشتراك جديد',
        targetName: targetProfile?.displayName ?? 'مستخدم',
        targetId: uid,
        performedBy: me.displayName,
        performedByRole: me.role,
        details: {'daysAdded': days, 'newEndDate': newEnd.toIso8601String()},
      );
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('activation_logs write ERROR: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> adminCancelSubscription(String uid) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || (!me.isAdmin && !me.isSuperAdmin)) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).update({
      'subscription.status': 'cancelled',
      'subscription.endDate': Timestamp.fromDate(DateTime.now()),
    });

    final target = await getUserProfileById(uid);
    await logSystemActivity(
      action: 'إلغاء اشتراك',
      targetName: target?.displayName ?? 'مستخدم',
      targetId: uid,
      performedBy: me.displayName,
      performedByRole: me.role,
      details: {'reason': 'إلغاء يدوي من الإدارة'},
    );
  }

  Future<void> setUserRole(String uid, String role) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null) throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    if (!me.isSuperAdmin && !me.isAdmin) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    if (!['user', 'captain', 'admin', 'superAdmin'].contains(role)) {
      throw Exception('دور غير معروف');
    }
    if ((role == 'superAdmin' || role == 'admin') && !me.isSuperAdmin) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    final target = await getUserProfileById(uid);
    if (target != null) {
      if ((target.isSuperAdmin || target.isAdmin) && !me.isSuperAdmin) {
        throw Exception('غير مصرح');
      }
    }
    if (uid == me.uid) {
      throw Exception('لا يمكنك تغيير دورك بنفسك');
    }
    await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).update({'role': role});

    await logSystemActivity(
      action: 'تغيير دور مستخدم',
      targetName: target?.displayName ?? 'مستخدم',
      targetId: uid,
      performedBy: me.displayName,
      performedByRole: me.role,
      details: {'newRole': role},
    );
  }

  Future<List<UserProfile>> getAllUsers() async {
    final snap = await firestore.collection('users').get();
    return snap.docs.map((d) => UserProfile.fromDoc(d)).toList();
  }

  Future<List<UserProfile>> getPlayersForCoach() async {
    final uid = currentUser?.uid;
    if (uid == null) return [];
    final uids = <String>{};
    final createdSnap = await firestore
        .collection('users')
        .where('createdBy', isEqualTo: uid)
        .get();
    for (final d in createdSnap.docs) {
      uids.add(d.id);
    }
    final coursesSnap = await firestore
        .collection('courses')
        .where('ownerId', isEqualTo: uid)
        .get();
    for (final d in coursesSnap.docs) {
      final target = (d.data()['targetUserId'] ?? '').toString();
      if (target.isNotEmpty) uids.add(target);
    }
    if (uids.isEmpty) return [];

    final result = <UserProfile>[];
    final ids = uids.toList();
    for (int i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 10) {
      final chunk = ids.sublist(i, i + 10 > ids.length ? ids.length : i + 10);
      final snap = await firestore
          .collection('users')
          .where(FieldPath.documentId, whereIn: chunk)
          .get();
      for (final d in snap.docs) {
        final p = UserProfile.fromDoc(d);
        if (!p.canManageCourses) result.add(p);
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  Future<void> extendSubscription(String uid, int days) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || (!me.isAdmin && !me.isSuperAdmin)) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    final doc = await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).get();
    final data = doc.data() ?? {};
    final sub = data['subscription'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
    DateTime currentEnd = sub['endDate'] != null
        ? (sub['endDate'] as Timestamp).toDate()
        : DateTime.now();
    if (currentEnd.isBefore(DateTime.now())) currentEnd = DateTime.now();
    final DateTime previousEnd = currentEnd;
    final newEnd = currentEnd.add(Duration(days: days));

    await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).update({
      'subscription.endDate': Timestamp.fromDate(newEnd),
      'subscription.status': 'active',
    });

    try {
      final targetProfile = await getUserProfileById(uid);
      final logRef = firestore.collection('activation_logs').doc();
      await logRef.set({
        'logId': logRef.id,
        'userId': uid,
        'userName': targetProfile?.displayName ?? '',
        'userEmail': targetProfile?.email ?? '',
        'type': 'manual_activation',
        'source': me.role,
        'daysAdded': days,
        'wasExtension': true,
        'previousEndDate': Timestamp.fromDate(previousEnd),
        'newEndDate': Timestamp.fromDate(newEnd),
        'activatedByName': me.displayName,
        'activatedByUid': me.uid,
        'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });

      await logSystemActivity(
        action: 'تمديد اشتراك',
        targetName: targetProfile?.displayName ?? 'مستخدم',
        targetId: uid,
        performedBy: me.displayName,
        performedByRole: me.role,
        details: {'daysAdded': days, 'newEndDate': newEnd.toIso8601String()},
      );
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('activation_logs write ERROR: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<CoachStats> getCoachStats(String uid) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.canViewAllStats) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    return _computeCoachStatsFor(uid);
  }

  Future<List<CoachStats>> getAllCoachesStats() async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.canViewAllStats) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    final snap = await firestore
        .collection('users')
        .where('role', whereIn: ['superAdmin', 'admin', 'captain']).get();
    final list = <CoachStats>[];
    for (final doc in snap.docs) {
      try {
        final s = await _computeCoachStatsFor(doc.id);
        list.add(s);
      } catch (_) {}
    }
    list.sort((a, b) => b.totalCourses.compareTo(a.totalCourses));
    return list;
  }

  Future<CoachStats> _computeCoachStatsFor(String uid) async {
    final userDoc = await firestore.collection('users').doc(uid).get();
    if (!userDoc.exists) {
      throw Exception('المستخدم غير موجود');
    }
    final coach = UserProfile.fromDoc(userDoc);
    final coursesSnap = await firestore
        .collection('courses')
        .where('ownerId', isEqualTo: uid)
        .get();
    final courses = coursesSnap.docs
        .map((d) => Course.fromJson({...d.data(), 'id': d.id}))
        .toList()
      ..sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.compareTo(a.createdAt));

    final createdSnap = await firestore
        .collection('users')
        .where('createdBy', isEqualTo: uid)
        .get();
    final playersCreated = createdSnap.docs
        .map((d) => UserProfile.fromDoc(d))
        .where((u) => !u.canManageCourses)
        .toList();

    final playerUids = <String>{};
    for (final c in courses) {
      if (c.targetUserId.isNotEmpty) playerUids.add(c.targetUserId);
    }

    int active = 0, paused = 0, completed = 0;
    int totalEx = 0, doneEx = 0;
    double adherenceSum = 0;
    int adherenceCount = 0;
    DateTime? lastAt;

    for (final c in courses) {
      if (c.isPaused) {
        paused++;
      } else if (c.isCompleted) {
        completed++;
      } else if (c.isActive) {
        active++;
      }
      totalEx += c.totalExercises;
      doneEx += c.doneExercises;
      if (c.adherenceScore > 0) {
        adherenceSum += c.adherenceScore;
        adherenceCount++;
      }
      if (lastAt == null || c.createdAt.isAfter(lastAt)) {
        lastAt = c.createdAt;
      }
    }

    final players = <UserProfile>[];
    final ids = playerUids.toList();
    for (int i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 10) {
      final chunk = ids.sublist(i, i + 10 > ids.length ? ids.length : i + 10);
      if (chunk.isEmpty) continue;
      final snap = await firestore
          .collection('users')
          .where(FieldPath.documentId, whereIn: chunk)
          .get();
      for (final d in snap.docs) {
        players.add(UserProfile.fromDoc(d));
      }
    }

    return CoachStats(
      coach: coach,
      totalCourses: courses.length,
      activeCourses: active,
      pausedCourses: paused,
      completedCourses: completed,
      playersCreated: playersCreated.length,
      totalPlayersWithCourses: playerUids.length,
      totalExercises: totalEx,
      doneExercises: doneEx,
      avgAdherence: adherenceCount > 0 ? adherenceSum / adherenceCount : 0,
      lastCourseAt: lastAt,
      courses: courses,
      players: players,
    );
  }

  Future<GlobalStats> getGlobalStats() async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.canViewAllStats) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح');
    }
    final usersSnap = await firestore.collection('users').get();
    final coursesSnap = await firestore.collection('courses').get();
    final exercisesSnap = await firestore.collection('exercises').get();

    int superAdmins = 0, admins = 0, captains = 0, players = 0, verified = 0;
    for (final d in usersSnap.docs) {
      final role = (d.data()['role'] ?? 'user').toString();
      final v = d.data()['isVerified'] == true;
      if (v) verified++;
      switch (role) {
        case 'superAdmin':
          superAdmins++;
          break;
        case 'admin':
          admins++;
          break;
        case 'captain':
          captains++;
          break;
        default:
          players++;
      }
    }

    int activeCourses = 0, pausedCourses = 0, completedCourses = 0;
    int malePlayers = 0, femalePlayers = 0;
    for (final d in coursesSnap.docs) {
      final data = d.data();
      final s = (data['status'] ?? '').toString();
      if (s == 'paused') {
        pausedCourses++;
      } else if (s == 'completed') {
        completedCourses++;
      } else if (s == 'active') {
        activeCourses++;
      }
      final playerMap = data['player'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
      if (playerMap != null) {
        final g = (playerMap['gender'] ?? 'male').toString();
        if (g == 'female') {
          femalePlayers++;
        } else {
          malePlayers++;
        }
      }
    }

    return GlobalStats(
      totalUsers: usersSnap.docs.length,
      superAdmins: superAdmins,
      admins: admins,
      captains: captains,
      players: players,
      verified: verified,
      malePlayers: malePlayers,
      femalePlayers: femalePlayers,
      totalCourses: coursesSnap.docs.length,
      activeCourses: activeCourses,
      pausedCourses: pausedCourses,
      completedCourses: completedCourses,
      totalExercises: exercisesSnap.docs.length,
    );
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 👑 OWNER & SYSTEM LOGS METHODS
  // ============================================================
  Future<void> logSystemActivity({
    required String action,
    required String targetName,
    required String targetId,
    required String performedBy,
    required String performedByRole,
    Map<String, dynamic>? details,
  }) async {
    await firestore.collection('system_logs').add({
      'action': action,
      'targetName': targetName,
      'targetId': targetId,
      'performedBy': performedBy,
      'performedByRole': performedByRole,
      'details': details ?? {},
      'timestamp': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    });
  }

  Future<int> getSubscriptionPrice() async {
    try {
      final doc =
          await firestore.collection('app_config').doc('subscription').get();
      if (doc.exists) {
        return (doc.data()?['price'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 5000;
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    return 5000;
  }

  Future<void> updateSubscriptionPrice({
    required int newPrice,
    required String performedBy,
  }) async {
    final me = await getUserProfile();
    if (me == null || !me.isOwner) {
      throw Exception('غير مصرح: هذه الخاصية للمالك فقط');
    }
    await firestore.collection('app_config').doc('subscription').set({
      'price': newPrice,
      'days': 90,
      'updatedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      'updatedBy': performedBy,
    }, SetOptions(merge: true));

    await logSystemActivity(
      action: 'تعديل سعر الاشتراك',
      targetName: 'إعدادات النظام',
      targetId: 'system',
      performedBy: performedBy,
      performedByRole: me.role,
      details: {'newPrice': newPrice, 'currency': 'IQD'},
    );
  }
}

  final String nameEn;
  final String primaryMuscle;

  final String type;
  final String equipment;
  final String difficulty;

    required this.nameAr,
    required this.nameEn,
    required this.primaryMuscle,
    this.secondaryMuscles = const [],
    this.type = 'مركب',
    this.equipment = 'بار',
    this.difficulty = 'متوسط',
    this.description = '',
  });
}

      primaryMuscle: 'العضلات الجانبية للبطن',
      type: 'وزن الجسم',
      equipment: 'وزن الجسم'),
  DefaultExerciseData(
      nameAr: 'البلانك الثابت المستوي',
      nameEn: 'Plank',
      primaryMuscle: 'البطن',
      type: 'وزن الجسم',
      equipment: 'وزن الجسم'),
  DefaultExerciseData(
      nameAr: 'البلانك الجانبي',
      nameEn: 'Side Plank',
      primaryMuscle: 'العضلات الجانبية للبطن',
      type: 'وزن الجسم',
      equipment: 'وزن الجسم'),
];

// ============================================================
// PDF SERVICE
// ============================================================
class PdfService {
  PdfService._();
  static final PdfService instance = PdfService._();
  pw.Font? _font;
  pw.Font? _boldFont;

  Future<pw.Font> _loadFont() async {
    if (_font != null) return _font!;
    try {
      _font = pw.Font.ttf(
        await rootBundle.load('assets/fonts/Cairo-Regular.ttf'),
      );
    } catch (_) {
      _font = await PdfGoogleFonts.cairoRegular();
    }
    return _font!;
  }

  Future<pw.Font> _loadBoldFont() async {
    if (_boldFont != null) return _boldFont!;
    try {
      _boldFont = pw.Font.ttf(
        await rootBundle.load('assets/fonts/Cairo-Bold.ttf'),
      );
    } catch (_) {
      _boldFont = await PdfGoogleFonts.cairoBold();
    }
    return _boldFont!;
  }

  String _fmtDate(DateTime d) =>
      '${d.year}/${d.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/${d.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

  PdfColor get _primary => PdfColor.fromInt(AppColors.palette.primary);
  PdfColor get _primaryDark => PdfColor.fromInt(AppColors.palette.primaryDark);
  PdfColor get _soft => PdfColor.fromInt(AppColors.palette.softPrimary);
  PdfColor get _border => PdfColor.fromInt(AppColors.palette.borderLight);
  PdfColor get _textDark => PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF1F2937);
  PdfColor get _textGray => PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF6B7280);

  PdfColor _withOpacity(PdfColor color, double opacity) {
    final alpha = (opacity * 255).round();
    return PdfColor.fromInt((alpha << 24) | (color.toInt() & 0x00FFFFFF));
  }

  Future<Uint8List> build(Course course) async {
    final font = await _loadFont();
    final boldFont = await _loadBoldFont();
    pw.MemoryImage? watermarkImage;
    try {
      final imageData = await rootBundle.load('assets/icons/icon.png');
      watermarkImage = pw.MemoryImage(imageData.buffer.asUint8List());
    } catch (_) {}

    final theme = pw.ThemeData.withFont(base: font, bold: boldFont);
    final doc = pw.Document(theme: theme);

    doc.addPage(
      pw.MultiPage(
        pageTheme: pw.PageTheme(
          pageFormat: PdfPageFormat.a4,
          margin: const pw.EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 16),
          textDirection: pw.TextDirection.rtl,
          buildBackground: (ctx) => watermarkImage != null
              ? _watermark(watermarkImage)
              : pw.SizedBox.shrink(),
        ),
        header: (ctx) => _pageHeader(course, boldFont, font),
        footer: (ctx) => _pageFooter(ctx, font),
        build: (ctx) {
          final w = <pw.Widget>[];
          w.add(_cvHeroCard(course, boldFont, font));
          w.add(pw.SizedBox(height: 8));
          w.add(_personalInfoRow(course, font, boldFont));
          w.add(pw.SizedBox(height: 4));
          w.add(_metricsRow(course, font, boldFont));
          w.add(pw.SizedBox(height: 10));
          w.add(_sectionTitle('البرنامج التدريبي', boldFont));
          w.add(pw.SizedBox(height: 4));

          if (course.days.isEmpty) {
            w.add(pw.Center(
              child: pw.Text('لا توجد أيام في هذا الكورس',
                  style:
                      pw.TextStyle(font: font, fontSize: 10, color: _textGray)),
            ));
          } else {
            int globalIdx = 1;
            for (final day in course.days) {
              w.add(_dayHeader(day, boldFont));
              w.add(pw.SizedBox(height: 3));
              for (final ex in day.exercises) {
                if (ex.isSuper) {
                  w.add(_superExerciseItem(globalIdx, ex, font, boldFont));
                } else {
                  w.add(_exerciseItem(globalIdx, ex, font, boldFont));
                }
                globalIdx++;
              }
              w.add(pw.SizedBox(height: 6));
            }
          }
          return w;
        },
      ),
    );
    return doc.save();
  }

  pw.Widget _watermark(pw.MemoryImage image) {
    return pw.Align(
      alignment: pw.Alignment.bottomCenter,
      child: pw.Opacity(
        opacity: 0.3,
        child: pw.Image(
          image,
          width: 600,
          height: 1000,
          fit: pw.BoxFit.contain,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  pw.Widget _pageHeader(Course c, pw.Font boldFont, pw.Font font) {
    return pw.Container(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
      decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
        border: pw.Border(
          bottom: pw.BorderSide(color: _primary, width: 1.5),
        ),
      ),
      child: pw.Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.end,
        children: [
          pw.Expanded(
            child: pw.Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                pw.Text(
                  'ملف تدريبي',
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: boldFont,
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                    color: _primaryDark,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.end,
            children: [
              pw.Text(
                c.ownerName.isNotEmpty ? c.ownerName : 'الكابتن',
                style: pw.TextStyle(
                  font: boldFont,
                  fontSize: 10,
                  fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                  color: _primaryDark,
                ),
              ),
              if (c.ownerPhone.isNotEmpty) ...[
                pw.SizedBox(height: 1),
                pw.Text(
                  c.ownerPhone,
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: font,
                    fontSize: 9,
                    color: _textGray,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  pw.Widget _pageFooter(pw.Context ctx, pw.Font font) {
    return pw.Container(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
      decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
        border: pw.Border(
          top: pw.BorderSide(color: _border, width: 0.5),
        ),
      ),
      child: pw.Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          pw.Text(
            'Gym Course Manager',
            style: pw.TextStyle(
              font: font,
              fontSize: 7,
              color: _textGray,
              fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
            ),
          ),
          pw.Text(
            'صفحة ${ctx.pageNumber} من ${ctx.pagesCount}',
            style: pw.TextStyle(font: font, fontSize: 7, color: _textGray),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  pw.Widget _cvHeroCard(Course c, pw.Font boldFont, pw.Font font) {
    final isPrivate = c.isPrivate;
    return pw.Container(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(10),
      decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
        gradient: pw.LinearGradient(
          colors: [_primary, _primaryDark],
          begin: pw.Alignment.topLeft,
          end: pw.Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
        borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(10),
      ),
      child: pw.Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          pw.Container(
            width: 50,
            height: 50,
            alignment: pw.Alignment.center,
            decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
              color: PdfColors.white,
              shape: pw.BoxShape.circle,
            ),
            child: pw.Text(
              c.player.name.isNotEmpty ? c.player.name[0] : '?',
              style: pw.TextStyle(
                font: boldFont,
                fontSize: 22,
                fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                color: _primary,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          pw.SizedBox(width: 10),
          pw.Expanded(
            child: pw.Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                pw.Text(
                  c.player.name,
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: boldFont,
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                    color: PdfColors.white,
                  ),
                ),
                pw.SizedBox(height: 2),
                pw.Text(
                  'الهدف: ${c.player.goal}   •   ${c.days.length} يوم',
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: font,
                    fontSize: 9,
                    color: PdfColors.white,
                  ),
                ),
                if (c.coachLabel.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  pw.SizedBox(height: 1),
                  pw.Text(
                    c.coachLabel,
                    style: pw.TextStyle(
                      font: font,
                      fontSize: 8,
                      color: PdfColors.white,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
          pw.Container(
            padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
            decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
              color: PdfColors.white,
              borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(8),
            ),
            child: pw.Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                pw.Text(
                  isPrivate ? 'خاص' : 'عام',
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: boldFont,
                    fontSize: 16,
                    fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                    color: isPrivate ? PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF8B5CF6) : _primary,
                  ),
                ),
                pw.Text(
                  'نوع الكورس',
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: font,
                    fontSize: 7,
                    color: _primaryDark,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  pw.Widget _personalInfoRow(Course c, pw.Font font, pw.Font boldFont) {
    final p = c.player;
    final items = <pw.Widget>[
      _infoPill('العمر', '${p.age} سنة', font, boldFont),
      pw.SizedBox(width: 4),
      _infoPill('الوزن', '${p.weight} كجم', font, boldFont),
      pw.SizedBox(width: 4),
      _infoPill('الطول', '${p.height} سم', font, boldFont),
      pw.SizedBox(width: 4),
      _infoPill('الجنس', p.isFemale ? 'أنثى' : 'ذكر', font, boldFont),
      pw.SizedBox(width: 4),
      _infoPill('من', _fmtDate(p.startDate), font, boldFont),
      pw.SizedBox(width: 4),
      _infoPill('إلى', _fmtDate(p.endDate), font, boldFont),
    ];
    return pw.Row(children: items);
  }

  pw.Widget _infoPill(
      String label, String value, pw.Font font, pw.Font boldFont) {
    return pw.Expanded(
      child: pw.Container(
        padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5, vertical: 5),
        decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
          color: _soft,
          borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(6),
          border: pw.Border.all(color: _border, width: 0.5),
        ),
        child: pw.Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            pw.Text(label,
                style: pw.TextStyle(font: font, fontSize: 7, color: _textGray)),
            pw.SizedBox(height: 1),
            pw.Text(
              value,
              style: pw.TextStyle(
                font: boldFont,
                fontSize: 8.5,
                fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                color: _primaryDark,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  pw.Widget _metricsRow(Course c, pw.Font font, pw.Font boldFont) {
    final p = c.player;
    pw.Widget box(String label, String value) {
      return pw.Expanded(
        child: pw.Container(
          padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 5, horizontal: 4),
          decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
            gradient: pw.LinearGradient(
              colors: [_primary, _primaryDark],
              begin: pw.Alignment.topLeft,
              end: pw.Alignment.bottomRight,
            ),
            borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(6),
          ),
          child: pw.Column(
            children: [
              pw.Text(value,
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                      font: boldFont,
                      fontSize: 11,
                      fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                      color: PdfColors.white)),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 1),
              pw.Text(label,
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                      font: font, fontSize: 7, color: PdfColors.white)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    return pw.Row(
      children: [
        box('BMR', '${p.bmr.round()}'),
        pw.SizedBox(width: 4),
        box('TDEE', '${p.dailyCaloriesNeeded.round()}'),
        pw.SizedBox(width: 4),
        box('المستهدف', '${p.targetCalories.round()}'),
      ],
    );
  }

  pw.Widget _sectionTitle(String t, pw.Font boldFont) {
    return pw.Container(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
        gradient: pw.LinearGradient(
          colors: [_primary, _primaryDark],
          begin: pw.Alignment.topLeft,
          end: pw.Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
        borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(6),
      ),
      child: pw.Row(
        children: [
          pw.Expanded(
            child: pw.Text(t,
                style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: boldFont,
                    fontSize: 10,
                    fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                    color: PdfColors.white)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  pw.Widget _dayHeader(WorkoutDay d, pw.Font boldFont) {
    return pw.Container(
      margin: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(top: 4, bottom: 2),
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
        color: _primaryDark,
        borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(4),
      ),
      child: pw.Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          pw.Text('اليوم ${d.number}: ${d.name}',
              style: pw.TextStyle(
                  font: boldFont,
                  fontSize: 9,
                  fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                  color: PdfColors.white)),
          pw.Text('${d.exercises.length} تمرين',
              style: pw.TextStyle(
                  font: boldFont, fontSize: 8, color: PdfColors.white)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  pw.Widget _exerciseItem(
      int idx, DayExercise ex, pw.Font font, pw.Font boldFont) {
    final reps = ex.sets.isNotEmpty ? ex.sets.first.reps : 0;
    final stats = '${ex.sets.length}×$reps';
    final nameWithStats = '${ex.nameAr} ($stats)';
    return pw.Container(
      margin: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 2),
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 3, vertical: 2),
      child: pw.Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          pw.Container(
            width: 18,
            height: 18,
            alignment: pw.Alignment.center,
            decoration:
                pw.BoxDecoration(color: _primary, shape: pw.BoxShape.circle),
            child: pw.Text('$idx',
                style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: boldFont,
                    fontSize: 8,
                    fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                    color: PdfColors.white)),
          ),
          pw.SizedBox(width: 6),
          pw.Expanded(
            child: pw.Text(nameWithStats,
                style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: boldFont,
                    fontSize: 9,
                    fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                    color: _textDark)),
          ),
          if (ex.restSeconds > 0) ...[
            pw.SizedBox(width: 3),
            pw.Container(
              padding:
                  const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4, vertical: 2),
              decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
                  color: _soft, borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(4)),
              child: pw.Text('${ex.restSeconds}s',
                  style: pw.TextStyle(
                      font: font, fontSize: 6.5, color: _textGray)),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  pw.Widget _superExerciseItem(
      int idx, DayExercise ex, pw.Font font, pw.Font boldFont) {
    final parts = <String>[];
    for (final s in ex.subExercises) {
      final reps = s.sets.isNotEmpty ? s.sets.first.reps : 0;
      parts.add('${s.nameAr} (${s.sets.length}×$reps)');
    }
    final combined = parts.join('  +  ');
    return pw.Container(
      margin: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 2),
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 3, vertical: 2),
      child: pw.Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          pw.Container(
            width: 18,
            height: 18,
            alignment: pw.Alignment.center,
            decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
              gradient: pw.LinearGradient(colors: [
                PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF8B5CF6),
                PdfColor.fromInt(0xFFEC4899)
              ], begin: pw.Alignment.topLeft, end: pw.Alignment.bottomRight),
              shape: pw.BoxShape.circle,
            ),
            child: pw.Text('$idx',
                style: pw.TextStyle(
                    font: boldFont,
                    fontSize: 8,
                    fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                    color: PdfColors.white)),
          ),
          pw.SizedBox(width: 6),
          pw.Expanded(
            child: pw.Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                pw.Row(
                  children: [
                    pw.Container(
                      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 4, vertical: 1),
                      decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
                        gradient: pw.LinearGradient(colors: [
                          PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF8B5CF6),
                          PdfColor.fromInt(0xFFEC4899)
                        ]),
                        borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(3),
                      ),
                      child: pw.Text('سوبر',
                          style: pw.TextStyle(
                              font: boldFont,
                              fontSize: 7,
                              fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                              color: PdfColors.white)),
                    ),
                    pw.SizedBox(width: 4),
                    pw.Text('(${ex.subExercises.length})',
                        style: pw.TextStyle(
                            font: font, fontSize: 7.5, color: _textGray)),
                  ],
                ),
                pw.SizedBox(height: 2),
                pw.Text(combined,
                    style: pw.TextStyle(
                        font: boldFont,
                        fontSize: 9,
                        fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                        color: _textDark,
                        lineSpacing: 2)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          pw.Container(
            padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4, vertical: 2),
            decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
                color: _soft, borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(4)),
            child: pw.Text('${ex.restSeconds}s',
                style:
                    pw.TextStyle(font: font, fontSize: 6.5, color: _textGray)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> preview(Course c) async {
    final b = await build(c);
    await Printing.layoutPdf(onLayout: (_) async => b);
  }

  Future<void> share(Course c) async {
    final b = await build(c);
    await Printing.sharePdf(bytes: b, filename: 'كورس_${c.player.name}.pdf');
  }
}

// ============================================================
// AUTH GATE
// ============================================================
class AuthGate extends StatelessWidget {
  final ThemeMode themeMode;
  final ValueChanged<ThemeMode> onThemeChanged;
  const AuthGate({
    super.key,
    required this.themeMode,
    required this.onThemeChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return StreamBuilder<User?>(
      stream: FirebaseService.instance.auth.authStateChanges(),
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
          return const Scaffold(
              body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
        }
        if (!snapshot.hasData) return const LoginScreen();
        return SubscriptionGate(
          themeMode: themeMode,
          onThemeChanged: onThemeChanged,
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SUBSCRIPTION GATE
// ============================================================
class SubscriptionGate extends StatelessWidget {
  final ThemeMode themeMode;
  final ValueChanged<ThemeMode> onThemeChanged;
  const SubscriptionGate({
    super.key,
    required this.themeMode,
    required this.onThemeChanged,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return StreamBuilder<User?>(
      stream: FirebaseService.instance.auth.authStateChanges(),
      builder: (context, authSnap) {
        final user = authSnap.data;
        if (user == null) return const LoginScreen();

        if (!user.emailVerified) {
          return StreamBuilder<UserProfile?>(
            stream: FirebaseService.instance.userProfileStream(),
            builder: (context, profSnap) {
              final profile = profSnap.data;
              if (profile == null) {
                return const Scaffold(
                    body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
              }
              if (profile.canManageCourses) return _buildProfileGate(profile);
              if (profile.subscriptionStatus == 'trial' &&
                  profile.hasActiveSubscription) {
                return _buildProfileGate(profile);
              }
              return EmailVerificationScreen(email: user.email ?? '');
            },
          );
        }

        return StreamBuilder<UserProfile?>(
          stream: FirebaseService.instance.userProfileStream(),
          builder: (context, snapshot) {
            if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
              return const Scaffold(
                  body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
            }
            final profile = snapshot.data;
            if (profile == null) {
              return const Scaffold(
                  body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
            }
            return _buildProfileGate(profile);
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildProfileGate(UserProfile profile) {
    if (profile.canManageCourses) {
      return HomeShell(
        profile: profile,
        themeMode: themeMode,
        onThemeChanged: onThemeChanged,
      );
    }
    if (profile.needsActivation || profile.isExpired) {
      return SubscriptionPaywallScreen(profile: profile);
    }
    if (profile.hasActiveSubscription) {
      return HomeShell(
        profile: profile,
        themeMode: themeMode,
        onThemeChanged: onThemeChanged,
      );
    }
    return SubscriptionPaywallScreen(profile: profile);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// 💳 SUBSCRIPTION PAYWALL SCREEN (ديناميكي السعر)
// ============================================================
class SubscriptionPaywallScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final UserProfile profile;
  const SubscriptionPaywallScreen({super.key, required this.profile});

  @override
  State<SubscriptionPaywallScreen> createState() =>
      _SubscriptionPaywallScreenState();
}

class _SubscriptionPaywallScreenState extends State<SubscriptionPaywallScreen> {
  int _price = 5000;
  bool _loading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadPrice();
  }

  Future<void> _loadPrice() async {
    final price = await PaymentService.instance.getPrice();
    if (mounted) {
      setState(() {
        _price = price;
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isExpired = widget.profile.isExpired;
    final isNew = widget.profile.needsActivation;

    if (_loading) {
      return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
    }

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(isNew ? 'تفعيل الحساب' : 'تجديد الاشتراك'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.logout),
            onPressed: () => FirebaseService.instance.signOut(),
          ),
