// ============================================================
// 💳 PAYMENT SCREEN
// ============================================================
class PaymentScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final UserProfile profile;
  const PaymentScreen({super.key, required this.profile});

  @override
  State<PaymentScreen> createState() => _PaymentScreenState();
}

class _PaymentScreenState extends State<PaymentScreen> {
  WebViewController? _controller;
  bool _loading = true;
  bool _verifying = false;
  String? _error;
  bool _paymentStarted = false;
  late AppLinks _appLinks;
  StreamSubscription<Uri>? _linkSub;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _initDeepLinks();
    _startPayment();
  }

  Future<void> _initDeepLinks() async {
    _appLinks = AppLinks();
    try {
      final initialUri = await _appLinks.getInitialLink();
      if (initialUri != null) _handleDeepLink(initialUri);
    } catch (_) {}
    _linkSub = _appLinks.uriLinkStream.listen(_handleDeepLink);
  }

  void _handleDeepLink(Uri uri) {
    debugPrint('Deep Link received: $uri');
    if (uri.scheme == 'gymapp') {
      _verifyPayment();
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _linkSub?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _startPayment() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
      _paymentStarted = false;
    });
    try {
      final url = await PaymentService.instance.createPaymentAndGetUrl();
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (url == null || url.isEmpty) {
        setState(() {
          _error = 'تعذر إنشاء طلب الدفع.\n'
              'يرجى التأكد من:\n'
              '1. اتصال الإنترنت\n'
              '2. أن السيرفر يستقبل الطلبات\n'
              '3. التواصل مع الإدارة إذا استمرت المشكلة.';
          _loading = false;
        });
        return;
      }
      _controller = WebViewController()
        ..setJavaScriptMode(JavaScriptMode.unrestricted)
        ..setBackgroundColor(Colors.white)
        ..setNavigationDelegate(NavigationDelegate(
          onPageStarted: (_) {
            if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = true);
          },
          onPageFinished: (pageUrl) {
            if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
            if (pageUrl.contains('success') ||
                pageUrl.contains('payment/result') ||
                pageUrl.contains('callback')) {
              _verifyPayment();
            }
          },
          onNavigationRequest: (request) {
            final url = request.url;
            debugPrint('NavigationRequest: $url');
            if (url.startsWith('gymapp://')) {
              _handleDeepLink(Uri.parse(url));
              return NavigationDecision.prevent;
            }
            if (url.contains('success') || url.contains('payment/result')) {
              _verifyPayment();
            }
            return NavigationDecision.navigate;
          },
          onWebResourceError: (e) {
            debugPrint('WebView Error: ${e.description}');
            if (e.description.contains('gymapp') ||
                e.description.contains('UNKNOWN_URL_SCHEME')) {
              return;
            }
          },
        ))
        ..loadRequest(Uri.parse(url));
      setState(() {
        _loading = false;
        _paymentStarted = true;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = 'خطأ: $e';
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _verifyPayment() async {
    if (_verifying) return;
    setState(() => _verifying = true);
    try {
      PaymentVerifyResult? result;
      for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        result = await PaymentService.instance.verifyPayment();
        if (result.success) break;
        debugPrint('Verify attempt ${i + 1}/10: ${result.message}');
        await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 3));
      }
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (result != null && result.success) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text('🎉 تم تفعيل اشتراكك بنجاح لمدة 90 يوماً!'),
            backgroundColor: Color(0xFF22C55E),
            duration: Duration(seconds: 4),
          ),
        );
        Navigator.pop(context, true);
      } else {
        setState(() {
          _error = result?.message ??
              'لم نتمكن من تأكيد الدفع بعد. إذا خُصم المبلغ، سيتم التفعيل تلقائياً خلال دقائق.';
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _error = 'خطأ في التحقق: $e');
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _verifying = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('الدفع بالبطاقة'),
        actions: [
          if (_paymentStarted && _controller != null)
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
              tooltip: 'إعادة تحميل الصفحة',
              onPressed: () => _controller?.reload(),
            ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.check_circle_outline),
            tooltip: 'تحقّق من الدفع',
            onPressed: _verifying ? null : _verifyPayment,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          if (_error != null)
            _buildError()
          else if (_controller == null)
            const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          else
            WebViewWidget(controller: _controller!),
          if (_loading || _verifying)
            Container(
              color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.25),
              child: Center(
                child: Card(
                  elevation: 8,
                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        const CircularProgressIndicator(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 14),
                        Text(
                          _verifying
                              ? 'جارٍ التحقق من الدفع...'
                              : 'جارٍ التحميل...',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildError() {
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.error_outline, size: 64, color: Color(0xFFEF4444)),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
              _error!,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14, height: 1.7),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            GradientButton(
              label: 'إعادة المحاولة',
              icon: Icons.refresh,
              onPressed: _startPayment,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
              child: Text('رجوع',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// LOGIN SCREEN
// ============================================================
class LoginScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const LoginScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<LoginScreen> createState() => _LoginScreenState();
}

class _LoginScreenState extends State<LoginScreen>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  final _form = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _email = TextEditingController();
  final _password = TextEditingController();
  bool _loading = false;
  bool _obscure = true;
  late AnimationController _animationController;
  late Animation<double> _fadeIn;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _animationController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 900),
    );
    _fadeIn =
        CurvedAnimation(parent: _animationController, curve: Curves.easeOut);
    _animationController.forward();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _email.dispose();
    _password.dispose();
    _animationController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    if (!_form.currentState!.validate()) return;
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.signIn(
        _email.text.trim(),
        _password.text,
      );
    } on FirebaseAuthException catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError(_friendlyError(e));
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError('حدث خطأ غير متوقع، حاول مجدداً');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
    }
  }

  void _showError(String msg) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Row(children: [
          const Icon(Icons.error_outline, color: Colors.white),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(child: Text(msg)),
        ]),
        backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
      ),
    );
  }

  String _friendlyError(FirebaseAuthException e) {
    switch (e.code) {
      case 'user-not-found':
        return 'لا يوجد حساب مسجّل بهذا البريد';
      case 'wrong-password':
        return 'كلمة المرور غير صحيحة';
      case 'invalid-email':
        return 'البريد الإلكتروني غير صالح';
      case 'invalid-credential':
        return 'بيانات الدخول غير صحيحة';
      case 'user-disabled':
        return 'تم تعطيل هذا الحساب';
      case 'too-many-requests':
        return 'عدد كبير من المحاولات، حاول لاحقاً';
      case 'network-request-failed':
        return 'تحقق من اتصال الإنترنت';
      default:
        return e.message ?? 'فشل تسجيل الدخول';
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final isDark = theme.brightness == Brightness.dark;
    return Scaffold(
      body: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topRight,
            end: Alignment.bottomLeft,
            colors: isDark
                ? [AppColors.bgDark, AppColors.surfaceDark]
                : [AppColors.softPrimary, AppColors.bgLight],
          ),
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Center(
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: FadeTransition(
                opacity: _fadeIn,
                child: Form(
                  key: _form,
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      const Center(
                        child: GradientLogo(icon: Icons.fitness_center),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      Text('أهلاً بعودتك',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 26, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Text('سجّل دخولك لمتابعة تمارينك وتقدمك',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 13.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 36),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _email,
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'البريد الإلكتروني',
                          prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.email_outlined, size: 20),
                        ),
                        validator: (v) {
                          if (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) {
                            return 'البريد الإلكتروني مطلوب';
                          }
                          final regex = RegExp(r'^[\w\.-]+@[\w\.-]+\.\w{2,}$');
                          if (!regex.hasMatch(v.trim())) {
                            return 'صيغة البريد غير صحيحة';
                          }
                          return null;
                        },
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 14),
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _password,
                        obscureText: _obscure,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'كلمة المرور',
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline, size: 20),
                          suffixIcon: IconButton(
                            icon: Icon(
                              _obscure
                                  ? Icons.visibility_outlined
                                  : Icons.visibility_off_outlined,
                              size: 20,
                            ),
                            onPressed: () =>
                                setState(() => _obscure = !_obscure),
                          ),
                        ),
                        validator: (v) {
                          if (v == null || v.isEmpty) {
                            return 'كلمة المرور مطلوبة';
                          }
                          if (v.length < 6) return '6 أحرف على الأقل';
                          return null;
                        },
                      ),
                      Align(
                        alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                        child: TextButton(
                          onPressed: _loading
                              ? null
                              : () => Navigator.push(
                                    context,
                                    MaterialPageRoute(
                                      builder: (_) =>
                                          const PasswordResetScreen(),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                          child: const Text('نسيت كلمة المرور؟'),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      GradientButton(
                        label: 'تسجيل الدخول',
                        loading: _loading,
                        onPressed: _submit,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      Row(children: [
                        const Expanded(child: Divider()),
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                          child: Text('أو',
                              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                  color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                                  fontSize: 12.5)),
                        ),
                        const Expanded(child: Divider()),
                      ]),
                      const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      OutlinedButton.icon(
                        onPressed: _loading
                            ? null
                            : () => Navigator.push(
                                  context,
                                  MaterialPageRoute(
                                    builder: (_) => const SignUpScreen(),
                                  ),
                                ),
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.person_add_alt_1, size: 20),
                        label:
                            const Text('إنشاء حساب جديد (تجربة 7 أيام مجاناً)'),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SIGN UP SCREEN
// ============================================================
class SignUpScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SignUpScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<SignUpScreen> createState() => _SignUpScreenState();
}

class _SignUpScreenState extends State<SignUpScreen> {
  final _form = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _name = TextEditingController();
  final _email = TextEditingController();
  final _phone = TextEditingController();
  final _password = TextEditingController();
  final _confirmPassword = TextEditingController();
  bool _loading = false;
  bool _obscurePass = true;
  bool _obscureConfirm = true;
  bool _acceptTerms = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _name.dispose();
    _email.dispose();
    _phone.dispose();
    _password.dispose();
    _confirmPassword.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    if (!_form.currentState!.validate()) return;
    if (!_acceptTerms) {
      _showError('يجب الموافقة على الشروط والأحكام');
      return;
    }
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.signUp(
        email: _email.text.trim(),
        password: _password.text,
        displayName: _name.text.trim(),
        phoneNumber: _phone.text.trim().isEmpty ? null : _phone.text.trim(),
      );
      if (mounted) {
        Navigator.pushReplacement(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(
            builder: (_) => EmailVerificationScreen(email: _email.text.trim()),
          ),
        );
      }
    } on FirebaseAuthException catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError(_friendlyError(e));
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) _showError('تعذّر إنشاء الحساب، حاول لاحقاً');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
    }
  }

  void _showError(String msg) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(content: Text(msg), backgroundColor: AppColors.error),
    );
  }

  String _friendlyError(FirebaseAuthException e) {
    switch (e.code) {
      case 'email-already-in-use':
        return 'هذا البريد الإلكتروني مستخدم مسبقاً';
      case 'invalid-email':
        return 'البريد الإلكتروني غير صالح';
      case 'weak-password':
        return 'كلمة المرور ضعيفة جداً';
      case 'operation-not-allowed':
        return 'التسجيل الذاتي غير مفعّل حالياً';
      case 'network-request-failed':
        return 'تحقق من اتصال الإنترنت';
      default:
        return e.message ?? 'فشل إنشاء الحساب';
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final isDark = theme.brightness == Brightness.dark;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_ios_new, size: 18),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
      ),
      extendBodyBehindAppBar: true,
      body: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topRight,
            end: Alignment.bottomLeft,
            colors: isDark
                ? [AppColors.bgDark, AppColors.surfaceDark]
                : [AppColors.softPrimary, AppColors.bgLight],
          ),
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Form(
              key: _form,
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  const Center(
                    child: GradientLogo(icon: Icons.person_add_alt_1, size: 80),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 20),
                  Text('إنشاء حساب جديد',
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  Text('ابدأ رحلتك الرياضية بـ 7 أيام تجريبية مجاناً',
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 13, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 32),
                  _buildField(
                    controller: _name,
                    label: 'الاسم الكامل',
                    icon: Icons.person_outline,
                    validator: (v) => v == null || v.trim().isEmpty
                        ? 'الاسم مطلوب'
                        : (v.trim().length < 3 ? 'الاسم قصير جداً' : null),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  _buildField(
                    controller: _email,
                    label: 'البريد الإلكتروني',
                    icon: Icons.email_outlined,
                    keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                    validator: (v) {
                      if (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) {
                        return 'البريد الإلكتروني مطلوب';
                      }
                      final regex = RegExp(r'^[\w\.-]+@[\w\.-]+\.\w{2,}$');
                      return regex.hasMatch(v.trim())
                          ? null
                          : 'صيغة البريد غير صحيحة';
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  _buildField(
                    controller: _phone,
                    label: 'رقم الهاتف (اختياري)',
                    icon: Icons.phone_outlined,
                    keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  _buildField(
                    controller: _password,
                    label: 'كلمة المرور',
                    icon: Icons.lock_outline,
                    obscure: _obscurePass,
                    suffixIcon: IconButton(
                      icon: Icon(
                        _obscurePass
                            ? Icons.visibility_outlined
                            : Icons.visibility_off_outlined,
                        size: 20,
                      ),
                      onPressed: () =>
                          setState(() => _obscurePass = !_obscurePass),
                    ),
                    validator: (v) => v == null || v.isEmpty
                        ? 'كلمة المرور مطلوبة'
                        : (v.length < 6 ? '6 أحرف على الأقل' : null),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  _buildField(
                    controller: _confirmPassword,
                    label: 'تأكيد كلمة المرور',
                    icon: Icons.lock_outline,
                    obscure: _obscureConfirm,
                    suffixIcon: IconButton(
                      icon: Icon(
                        _obscureConfirm
                            ? Icons.visibility_outlined
                            : Icons.visibility_off_outlined,
                        size: 20,
                      ),
                      onPressed: () =>
                          setState(() => _obscureConfirm = !_obscureConfirm),
                    ),
                    validator: (v) {
                      if (v == null || v.isEmpty) return 'التأكيد مطلوب';
                      if (v != _password.text) {
                        return 'كلمتا المرور غير متطابقتين';
                      }
                      return null;
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 20),
                  SurfaceCard(
                    padding:
                        const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 4),
                    child: CheckboxListTile(
                      value: _acceptTerms,
                      onChanged: (v) =>
                          setState(() => _acceptTerms = v ?? false),
                      controlAffinity: ListTileControlAffinity.leading,
                      contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      title: Text(
                        'أوافق على الشروط والأحكام وسياسة الخصوصية',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 13),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  GradientButton(
                    label: 'إنشاء الحساب',
                    loading: _loading,
                    onPressed: _submit,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      Text('لديك حساب بالفعل؟',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13)),
                      TextButton(
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                        child: const Text('تسجيل الدخول'),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildField({
    required TextEditingController controller,
    required String label,
    required IconData icon,
    TextInputType? keyboardType,
    bool obscure = false,
    Widget? suffixIcon,
    String? Function(String?)? validator,
  }) {
    return TextFormField(
      controller: controller,
      keyboardType: keyboardType,
      obscureText: obscure,
      validator: validator,
      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
      decoration: InputDecoration(
        labelText: label,
        prefixIcon: Icon(icon, size: 20),
        suffixIcon: suffixIcon,
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// EMAIL VERIFICATION SCREEN
// ============================================================
class EmailVerificationScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String email;
  const EmailVerificationScreen({super.key, required this.email});

  @override
  State<EmailVerificationScreen> createState() =>
      _EmailVerificationScreenState();
}

class _EmailVerificationScreenState extends State<EmailVerificationScreen> {
  bool _checking = false;
  bool _resending = false;
  Timer? _timer;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 5), (_) => _checkNow());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _checkNow() async {
    if (_checking) return;
    setState(() => _checking = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.refreshEmailVerifiedStatus();
      final user = FirebaseService.instance.currentUser;
      if (user != null && user.emailVerified) {
        _timer?.cancel();
        if (mounted) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            const SnackBar(
              content: Text('تم تأكيد بريدك بنجاح!'),
              backgroundColor: Color(0xFF22C55E),
            ),
          );
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _checking = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _resend() async {
    setState(() => _resending = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.resendVerificationEmail();
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text('تم إرسال بريد التحقق مجدداً'),
            backgroundColor: Color(0xFF22C55E),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('تعذّر الإرسال: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _resending = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      body: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topRight,
            end: Alignment.bottomLeft,
            colors: theme.brightness == Brightness.dark
                ? [AppColors.bgDark, AppColors.surfaceDark]
                : [AppColors.softPrimary, AppColors.bgLight],
          ),
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Center(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  const GradientLogo(
                      icon: Icons.mark_email_unread_outlined, size: 120),
                  const SizedBox(height: 32),
                  Text('تحقق من بريدك الإلكتروني',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Text('أرسلنا رابطاً إلى:\n${widget.email}',
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 15,
                          color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                          height: 1.6)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  SurfaceCard(
                    color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.08),
                    child: Row(children: [
                      Icon(Icons.info_outline,
                          color: AppColors.primary, size: 22),
                      const SizedBox(width: 10),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          'افتح البريد واضغط على الرابط. سنتحقق تلقائياً كل بضع ثوانٍ.',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12.5),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ]),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 32),
                  GradientButton(
                    label: 'تحققت من بريدي الآن',
                    icon: Icons.refresh,
                    loading: _checking,
                    onPressed: _checkNow,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  OutlinedButton.icon(
                    onPressed: _resending ? null : _resend,
                    style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                      minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(52),
                    ),
                    icon: _resending
                        ? const SizedBox(
                            width: 18,
                            height: 18,
                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2))
                        : const Icon(Icons.send_outlined),
                    label: const Text('إعادة إرسال البريد'),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  TextButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () => FirebaseService.instance.signOut(),
                    icon: Icon(Icons.logout, color: AppColors.error),
                    label: Text('تسجيل الخروج',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.error)),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// PASSWORD RESET SCREEN
// ============================================================
class PasswordResetScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const PasswordResetScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<PasswordResetScreen> createState() => _PasswordResetScreenState();
}

class _PasswordResetScreenState extends State<PasswordResetScreen> {
  final _form = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _email = TextEditingController();
  bool _loading = false;
  bool _sent = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _email.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    if (!_form.currentState!.validate()) return;
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.sendPasswordResetEmail(_email.text.trim());
      if (mounted) setState(() => _sent = true);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('تعذّر الإرسال: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        leading: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_ios_new, size: 18),
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
        ),
      ),
      extendBodyBehindAppBar: true,
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Center(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: _sent ? _success() : _formView(),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _formView() => Form(
        key: _form,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            const Center(
              child: GradientLogo(icon: Icons.lock_reset, size: 90),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            Text('استعادة كلمة المرور',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Text('أدخل بريدك الإلكتروني وسنرسل لك رابطاً للاستعادة.',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    fontSize: 13, color: AppColors.textSecondary, height: 1.6)),
            const SizedBox(height: 32),
            TextFormField(
              controller: _email,
              keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'البريد الإلكتروني',
                prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.email_outlined, size: 20),
              ),
              validator: (v) =>
                  v == null || !v.contains('@') ? 'بريد غير صحيح' : null,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            GradientButton(
              label: 'إرسال رابط الاستعادة',
              icon: Icons.send_outlined,
              loading: _loading,
              onPressed: _submit,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );

  Widget _success() => Column(
        children: [
          const GradientLogo(icon: Icons.mark_email_read_outlined, size: 120),
          const SizedBox(height: 32),
          Text('تم إرسال الرابط',
              style:
                  GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(
            'تحقق من بريدك الإلكتروني:\n${_email.text.trim()}',
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            style:
                GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary, height: 1.7),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 32),
          FilledButton.icon(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
            label: const Text('العودة لتسجيل الدخول'),
          ),
        ],
      );
}

// ============================================================
// HOME SHELL
// ============================================================
class HomeShell extends StatefulWidget {
  final UserProfile profile;
  final ThemeMode themeMode;
  final ValueChanged<ThemeMode> onThemeChanged;
  const HomeShell({
    super.key,
    required this.profile,
    required this.themeMode,
    required this.onThemeChanged,
  });

  @override
  State<HomeShell> createState() => _HomeShellState();
}

class _HomeShellState extends State<HomeShell> {
  int _index = 0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _startCourseWatcher();
  }

  Future<void> _startCourseWatcher() async {
    if (widget.profile.canManageCourses) return;
    await CourseWatcherService.instance.start(
      uid: widget.profile.uid,
      courseStream: FirebaseService.instance.myCoursesStream(),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isSuper = widget.profile.isSuperAdmin;
    final isAdmin = widget.profile.isAdmin;
    final isCaptain = widget.profile.isCaptain;
    final canManage = widget.profile.canManageCourses;

    final screens = <Widget>[
      DashboardScreen(
          profile: widget.profile,
          onNavigate: (i) => setState(() => _index = i)),
      if (canManage) PlayersScreen(profile: widget.profile),
      ExercisesScreen(isAdmin: canManage, profile: widget.profile),
      CoursesScreen(isAdmin: canManage, profile: widget.profile),
      if (isSuper)
        SuperAdminPanelScreen(profile: widget.profile)
      else if (isAdmin)
        AdminPanelScreen(profile: widget.profile)
      else if (isCaptain)
        CaptainPanelScreen(profile: widget.profile)
      else
        SettingsScreen(
            themeMode: widget.themeMode, onThemeChanged: widget.onThemeChanged),
    ];

    final destinations = <NavigationDestination>[
      const NavigationDestination(
          icon: Icon(Icons.dashboard_outlined),
          selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.dashboard),
          label: 'الرئيسية'),
      if (canManage)
        const NavigationDestination(
            icon: Icon(Icons.people_outline),
            selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.people),
            label: 'اللاعبون'),
      const NavigationDestination(
          icon: Icon(Icons.fitness_center_outlined),
          selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.fitness_center),
          label: 'التمارين'),
      const NavigationDestination(
          icon: Icon(Icons.assignment_outlined),
          selectedIcon: Icon(Icons.assignment),
          label: 'الكورسات'),
      NavigationDestination(
        icon: Icon(isSuper
            ? Icons.verified_user_outlined
            : isAdmin
                ? Icons.admin_panel_settings_outlined
                : isCaptain
                    ? Icons.sports_outlined
                    : Icons.settings_outlined),
        selectedIcon: Icon(isSuper
            ? Icons.verified_user
            : isAdmin
                ? Icons.admin_panel_settings
                : isCaptain
                    ? Icons.sports
                    : Icons.settings),
        label: isSuper
            ? 'الإشراف العام'
            : isAdmin
                ? 'الإدارة'
                : isCaptain
                    ? 'الكابتن'
                    : 'الإعدادات',
      ),
    ];

    return Scaffold(
      body: IndexedStack(index: _index, children: screens),
      bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBar(
        selectedIndex: _index,
        onDestinationSelected: (i) => setState(() => _index = i),
        destinations: destinations,
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// DASHBOARD
// ============================================================
class DashboardScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final UserProfile profile;
  final ValueChanged<int> onNavigate;
  const DashboardScreen(
      {super.key, required this.profile, required this.onNavigate});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isSuper = profile.isSuperAdmin;
    final isAdmin = profile.isAdmin;
    final isCaptain = profile.isCaptain;
    final canManage = profile.canManageCourses;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
      body: StreamBuilder<List<Course>>(
        stream: FirebaseService.instance.myCoursesStream(),
        builder: (context, courseSnap) {
          final remoteCourses = courseSnap.data ?? [];
          final localCourses = canManage
              ? <Course>[]
              : StorageService.instance.loadLocalCourses();
          final allCourses = [...remoteCourses, ...localCourses];

          return StreamBuilder<List<Exercise>>(
            stream: FirebaseService.instance.exercisesStream(),
            builder: (context, exSnap) {
              final serverCount = (exSnap.data ?? []).length;
              final localCount = canManage
                  ? 0
                  : StorageService.instance.loadLocalExercises().length;
              final totalEx =
                  canManage ? serverCount : serverCount + localCount;
              final active =
                  allCourses.where((c) => c.status == 'active').length;
              final done =
                  allCourses.where((c) => c.status == 'completed').length;

              return CustomScrollView(
                slivers: [
                  SliverAppBar(
                    floating: true,
                    pinned: true,
                    centerTitle: false,
                    titleSpacing: 20,
                    title: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          'مرحباً، ${profile.displayName}',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        ),
                        Text(
                          isSuper
                              ? 'لوحة الإشراف العام'
                              : isAdmin
                                  ? 'لوحة التحكم'
                                  : isCaptain
                                      ? 'لوحة الكابتن'
                                      : 'استعد لتمرينك اليوم',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    actions: [
                      IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.logout),
                        tooltip: 'خروج',
                        onPressed: () => FirebaseService.instance.signOut(),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  SliverPadding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 32),
                    sliver: SliverList(
                      delegate: SliverChildListDelegate([
                        GradientCard(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Row(
                                children: [
                                  Container(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                                    ),
                                    child: Icon(
                                      isSuper
                                          ? Icons.verified_user
                                          : isAdmin
                                              ? Icons.admin_panel_settings
                                              : isCaptain
                                                  ? Icons.sports
                                                  : Icons.fitness_center,
                                      color: Colors.white,
                                      size: 22,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: Column(
                                      crossAxisAlignment:
                                          CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                      children: [
                                        Text(
                                          isSuper
                                              ? 'مرحباً أيها المشرف العام'
                                              : isAdmin
                                                  ? 'مرحباً أيها المدير'
                                                  : isCaptain
                                                      ? 'مرحباً أيها الكابتن'
                                                      : 'مرحباً بك',
                                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                              color: Colors.white
                                                  .withOpacity(0.85),
                                              fontSize: 12),
                                        ),
                                        Row(
                                          children: [
                                            Flexible(
                                              child: Text(
                                                profile.displayName,
                                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                                  color: Colors.white,
                                                  fontSize: 19,
                                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                                ),
                                                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                            if (profile.isVerified) ...[
                                              const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                              const Icon(Icons.verified,
                                                  color: Colors.white,
                                                  size: 20),
                                            ],
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  Container(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                        horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                                    ),
                                    child: Text(
                                      profile.roleLabel,
                                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                        color: Colors.white,
                                        fontSize: 11,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 18),
                              Row(
                                children: [
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: _heroStat(
                                      icon: Icons.assignment_turned_in_outlined,
                                      label: 'الكورسات',
                                      value: '${allCourses.length}',
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  _heroDivider(),
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: _heroStat(
                                      icon: Icons.fitness_center,
                                      label: 'التمارين',
                                      value: '$totalEx',
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  _heroDivider(),
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: _heroStat(
                                      icon: Icons.check_circle_outline,
                                      label: 'المكتملة',
                                      value: '$done',
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 24),
                        const SectionTitle(title: 'نظرة سريعة'),
                        const SizedBox(height: 12),
                        GridView.count(
                          crossAxisCount: 2,
                          shrinkWrap: true,
                          physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                          mainAxisSpacing: 12,
                          crossAxisSpacing: 12,
                          childAspectRatio: 1.55,
                          children: [
                            _statCard(
                              icon: Icons.assignment,
                              label: 'كورساتي',
                              value: '${allCourses.length}',
                              color: AppColors.primary,
                            ),
                            _statCard(
                              icon: Icons.fitness_center,
                              label: 'التمارين',
                              value: '$totalEx',
                              color: AppColors.success,
                            ),
                            _statCard(
                              icon: Icons.play_circle_outline,
                              label: 'النشطة',
                              value: '$active',
                              color: AppColors.purple,
                            ),
                            _statCard(
                              icon: Icons.check_circle,
                              label: 'المكتملة',
                              value: '$done',
                              color: AppColors.info,
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 24),
                        const SectionTitle(title: 'إجراءات سريعة'),
                        const SizedBox(height: 12),
                        if (isSuper) ...[
                          _action(
                            context,
                            icon: Icons.insights,
                            title: 'إحصائيات الفريق (مدراء/كباتن)',
                            color: AppColors.primary,
                            onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => const CoachStatsListScreen()),
                            ),
                          ),
                          _action(
                            context,
                            icon: Icons.verified_user,
                            title: 'إدارة الأدوار والصلاحيات',
                            color: AppColors.purple,
                            onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => const AdminUsersScreen()),
                            ),
                          ),
                          _action(
                            context,
                            icon: Icons.people_alt,
                            title: 'إنشاء كورس + حساب جديد',
                            color: AppColors.info,
                            onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => const PlayerInfoScreen(
                                      createAccount: true)),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ] else if (isAdmin) ...[
                          _action(
                            context,
                            icon: Icons.person_add,
                            title: 'إنشاء كورس لعميل + حساب جديد',
                            color: AppColors.primary,
                            onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => const PlayerInfoScreen(
                                      createAccount: true)),
                            ),
                          ),
                          _action(
                            context,
                            icon: Icons.cloud_upload,
                            title: 'رفع مكتبة التمارين للسيرفر',
                            color: AppColors.purple,
                            onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => const SeedExercisesScreen()),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ] else if (isCaptain) ...[
                          _action(
                            context,
                            icon: Icons.person_add,
                            title: 'إنشاء كورس للاعب + حساب جديد',
                            color: AppColors.primary,
                            onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => const PlayerInfoScreen(
                                      createAccount: true)),
                            ),
                          ),
                          _action(
                            context,
                            icon: Icons.assignment,
                            title: 'إدارة الكورسات التي أنشأتها',
                            color: AppColors.purple,
                            onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) =>
                                      const MyCreatedCoursesScreen()),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ] else ...[
                          _action(
                            context,
                            icon: Icons.add_circle,
                            title: 'إنشاء كورسي الخاص (محلي)',
                            color: AppColors.primary,
                            onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                              context,
                              MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) => const PlayerInfoScreen(
                                      createAccount: false)),
                            ),
                          ),
                          _action(
                            context,
                            icon: Icons.fitness_center,
                            title: 'تصفح التمارين',
                            color: AppColors.success,
                            onTap: () => onNavigate(2),
                          ),
                        ],
                        const SizedBox(height: 24),
                        const SectionTitle(title: 'آخر الكورسات'),
                        const SizedBox(height: 12),
                        if (allCourses.isEmpty)
                          SurfaceCard(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
                            child: Column(
                              children: [
                                Icon(Icons.assignment_outlined,
                                    size: 44,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondary
                                        .withOpacity(0.5)),
                                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                                Text('لا توجد كورسات بعد',
                                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                        fontSize: 13,
                                        color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                              ],
                            ),
                          )
                        else
                          ...allCourses.take(3).map((c) => Padding(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                                child: _recentCourseCard(
                                    context, c, canManage, profile),
                              )),
                      ]),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _heroStat({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    required String value,
  }) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Icon(icon, color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.85), size: 18),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        Text(value,
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                color: Colors.white,
                fontSize: 15,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        Text(label,
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.75), fontSize: 10.5)),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _heroDivider() => Container(
        width: 1,
        height: 32,
        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
      );

  Widget _statCard({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    required String value,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(7),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 18),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(label,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 12.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          Text(value,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 24,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: color,
                  height: 1.1)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _action(BuildContext context,
      {required IconData icon,
      required String title,
      required Color color,
      required VoidCallback onTap}) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      child: SurfaceCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 12),
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(9),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
              ),
              child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 20),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(title,
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
            ),
            Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios,
                size: 14, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _recentCourseCard(
      BuildContext context, Course c, bool canManage, UserProfile profile) {
    final statusColor = c.isPaused
        ? AppColors.error
        : c.isCompleted
            ? AppColors.info
            : AppColors.success;
    final statusLabel = c.isPaused
        ? 'موقوف'
        : c.isCompleted
            ? 'مكتمل'
            : 'نشط';

    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      onTap: c.isPaused
          ? null
          : () => Navigator.push(
                context,
                MaterialPageRoute(
                  builder: (_) =>
                      CoursePlayerScreen(course: c, isAdmin: canManage),
                ),
              ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 44,
                height: 44,
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  boxShadow: [
                    BoxShadow(
                      color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.25),
                      blurRadius: 10,
                      offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                child: Text(
                  c.player.name.isNotEmpty ? c.player.name[0] : '?',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(c.player.name,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 14.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 3),
                    Text(
                      '${c.source == 'superAdmin' ? 'من الإشراف' : c.source == 'admin' ? 'من الإدارة' : c.source == 'captain' ? 'من الكابتن' : 'محلي'} • ${dateFmt.format(c.player.startDate)}',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              MiniChip(text: statusLabel, color: statusColor),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: c.progress,
              minHeight: 6,
              backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).dividerColor.withOpacity(0.3),
              color: c.isPaused ? AppColors.textSecondary : AppColors.primary,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class SuperAdminPanelScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final UserProfile profile;
  const SuperAdminPanelScreen({super.key, required this.profile});

  @override
  State<SuperAdminPanelScreen> createState() => _SuperAdminPanelScreenState();
}

class _SuperAdminPanelScreenState extends State<SuperAdminPanelScreen> {
  GlobalStats? _global;
  bool _loading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadGlobal();
  }

  Future<void> _loadGlobal() async {
    try {
      final g = await FirebaseService.instance.getGlobalStats();
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _global = g;
          _loading = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('لوحة الإشراف العام'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
            onPressed: () {
              setState(() => _loading = true);
              _loadGlobal();
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: _loadGlobal,
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          children: [
            GradientCard(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
              gradient: const LinearGradient(
                colors: [Color(0xFF7C3AED), Color(0xFFDB2777)],
                begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                end: Alignment.bottomRight,
              ),
              child: Row(children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                  ),
                  child: const Icon(Icons.verified_user,
                      color: Colors.white, size: 26),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 14),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text('مرحباً ${widget.profile.displayName}',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              color: Colors.white,
                              fontSize: 16,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 3),
                      Text('إشراف عام • صلاحيات كاملة',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.85),
                              fontSize: 12)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ]),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            const SectionTitle(title: 'الأدوار والصلاحيات'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            if (_loading)
              const Center(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.all(20),
                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
                ),
              )
            else if (_global != null) ...[
              GridView.count(
                crossAxisCount: 2,
                shrinkWrap: true,
                physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                mainAxisSpacing: 10,

            ),
            _tile(
              context,

              title: 'إدارة كل المستخدمين والأدوار',

                    builder: (_) =>
                        const AddExerciseScreen(saveToServer: true)),
              ),

          ],
