  Widget _gStat({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    required String value,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 16),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  label,
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          Text(value,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: color)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _genderCard({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    required String value,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: color.withOpacity(0.15),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 24),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(label,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                Text(value,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 22,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: color)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _tile(
    BuildContext context, {
    required IconData icon,
    required String title,
    required String subtitle,
    required Color color,
    required VoidCallback onTap,
  }) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      child: SurfaceCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 12),
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Row(children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 22),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 14),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(title,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                const SizedBox(height: 3),
                Text(subtitle,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios,
              size: 14, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// COACH STATS LIST SCREEN
// ============================================================
class CoachStatsListScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const CoachStatsListScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<CoachStatsListScreen> createState() => _CoachStatsListScreenState();
}

class _CoachStatsListScreenState extends State<CoachStatsListScreen> {
  bool _loading = true;
  List<CoachStats> _list = [];
  String _query = '';
  String _roleFilter = 'all';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    try {
      final list = await FirebaseService.instance.getAllCoachesStats();
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _list = list;
          _loading = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _loading = false);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  List<CoachStats> get _filtered {
    return _list.where((s) {
      if (_roleFilter != 'all' && s.coach.role != _roleFilter) return false;
      if (_query.isEmpty) return true;
      final q = _query.toLowerCase();
      return s.coach.displayName.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
          s.coach.email.toLowerCase().contains(q);
    }).toList();
  }

  Color _roleColor(String role) {
    switch (role) {
      case 'superAdmin':
        return const Color(0xFFDB2777);
      case 'admin':
        return AppColors.info;
      case 'captain':
        return AppColors.purple;
      default:
        return AppColors.primary;
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final filtered = _filtered;
    int totCourses = 0, totPlayers = 0, totExercises = 0;
    int totMales = 0, totFemales = 0;
    double adherenceSum = 0;
    int adherenceCount = 0;
    for (final s in _list) {
      totCourses += s.totalCourses;
      totPlayers += s.totalPlayersWithCourses;
      totExercises += s.totalExercises;
      totMales += s.malePlayers;
      totFemales += s.femalePlayers;
      if (s.avgAdherence > 0) {
        adherenceSum += s.avgAdherence;
        adherenceCount++;
      }
    }
    final overallAdherence =
        adherenceCount > 0 ? (adherenceSum / adherenceCount).round() : 0;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('إحصائيات الفريق'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: _load),
        ],
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 12, 12, 0),
            child: SurfaceCard(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.insights, color: AppColors.primary, size: 20),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text('ملخص شامل',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                          child: _sumBox(
                              '${_list.length}', 'الفريق', AppColors.primary)),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                          child: _sumBox(
                              '$totCourses', 'كورسات', AppColors.success)),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                          child:
                              _sumBox('$totPlayers', 'لاعبون', AppColors.info)),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                          child: _sumBox(
                              '$totMales', 'ذكور', const Color(0xFF3B82F6))),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                          child: _sumBox(
                              '$totFemales', 'إناث', const Color(0xFFEC4899))),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                          child: _sumBox('$overallAdherence%', 'الالتزام',
                              AppColors.purple)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            child: TextField(
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                hintText: 'ابحث بالاسم أو البريد...',
                prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search),
              ),
              onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _query = v),
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  _roleChip('all', 'الكل'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  _roleChip('superAdmin', 'إشراف عام'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  _roleChip('admin', 'مدراء'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  _roleChip('captain', 'كباتن'),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Expanded(
            child: _loading
                ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                : filtered.isEmpty
                    ? Center(
                        child: Text('لا توجد نتائج',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                      )
                    : ListView.builder(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        itemCount: filtered.length,
                        itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                          final s = filtered[i];
                          return Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                            child: _coachCard(context, s),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _sumBox(String value, String label, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10, horizontal: 6),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.08),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.25)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Text(value,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: color)),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(label,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 10, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _roleChip(String value, String label) {
    return ChoiceChip(
      label: Text(label, style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12)),
      selected: _roleFilter == value,
      onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _roleFilter = value),
    );
  }

  Widget _coachCard(BuildContext context, CoachStats s) {
    final color = _roleColor(s.coach.role);
    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      onTap: () => Navigator.push(
        context,
        MaterialPageRoute(
          builder: (_) => CoachStatsDetailScreen(coachUid: s.coach.uid),
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 44,
                height: 44,
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withOpacity(0.15),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  s.coach.displayName.isNotEmpty ? s.coach.displayName[0] : '?',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      color: color, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Row(children: [
                      Flexible(
                        child: Text(
                          s.coach.displayName,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 14.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (s.coach.isVerified) ...[
                        const SizedBox(width: 5),
                        Icon(Icons.verified, color: AppColors.info, size: 15),
                      ],
                    ]),
                    const SizedBox(height: 3),
                    Text(s.coach.email,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              MiniChip(text: s.coach.roleLabel, color: color),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                  child: _miniStat(Icons.assignment, '${s.totalCourses}',
                      'كورسات', AppColors.primary)),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Expanded(
                  child: _miniStat(Icons.people, '${s.totalPlayersWithCourses}',
                      'لاعبون', AppColors.info)),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Expanded(
                  child: _miniStat(Icons.male, '${s.malePlayers}', 'ذكور',
                      const Color(0xFF3B82F6))),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Expanded(
                  child: _miniStat(Icons.female, '${s.femalePlayers}', 'إناث',
                      const Color(0xFFEC4899))),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 7),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: s.adherenceColor.withOpacity(0.08),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              border: Border.all(color: s.adherenceColor.withOpacity(0.25)),
            ),
            child: Row(children: [
              Icon(Icons.trending_up, size: 14, color: s.adherenceColor),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Text('الالتزام: ',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
              Text('${s.adherenceRounded}%',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: s.adherenceColor)),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Text('(${s.adherenceLabel})',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 10.5, color: s.adherenceColor)),
              const Spacer(),
              if (s.lastCourseAt != null)
                Text('آخر كورس: ${dateFmt.format(s.lastCourseAt!)}',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 10, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
            ]),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _miniStat(IconData icon, String value, String label, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8, horizontal: 6),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.08),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 15, color: color),
          const SizedBox(height: 3),
          Text(value,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: color),
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
          Text(label,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 9.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// COACH STATS DETAIL SCREEN
// ============================================================
class CoachStatsDetailScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String coachUid;
  const CoachStatsDetailScreen({super.key, required this.coachUid});

  @override
  State<CoachStatsDetailScreen> createState() => _CoachStatsDetailScreenState();
}

class _CoachStatsDetailScreenState extends State<CoachStatsDetailScreen> {
  bool _loading = true;
  CoachStats? _stats;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final s = await FirebaseService.instance.getCoachStats(widget.coachUid);
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _stats = s;
          _loading = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _error = e.toString();
          _loading = false;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('تقرير تفصيلي'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: _load),
        ],
      ),
      body: _loading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : _error != null
              ? Center(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                    child: Text(
                      'خطأ في تحميل البيانات:\n$_error',
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.error),
                    ),
                  ),
                )
              : _buildBody(_stats!),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBody(CoachStats s) {
    final coach = s.coach;
    final roleColor = coach.isSuperAdmin
        ? const Color(0xFFDB2777)
        : coach.isAdmin
            ? AppColors.info
            : coach.isCaptain
                ? AppColors.purple
                : AppColors.primary;

    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      children: [
        GradientCard(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            colors: [roleColor, roleColor.withOpacity(0.7)],
            begin: Alignment.topLeft,
            end: Alignment.bottomRight,
          ),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 80,
                height: 80,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                  border: Border.all(
                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.5), width: 3),
                ),
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                child: Text(
                  coach.displayName.isNotEmpty ? coach.displayName[0] : '?',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 32,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  Flexible(
                    child: Text(
                      coach.displayName,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontSize: 19,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (coach.isVerified) ...[
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    const Icon(Icons.verified, color: Colors.white, size: 22),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Text(coach.email,
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9), fontSize: 12)),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Wrap(
                spacing: 8,
                runSpacing: 6,
                alignment: WrapAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  _badge(coach.roleLabel),
                  if (coach.createdAt != null)
                    _badge('منذ ${dateFmt.format(coach.createdAt!)}'),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        const SectionTitle(title: 'المؤشرات الرئيسية'),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        GridView.count(
          crossAxisCount: 2,
          shrinkWrap: true,
          physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          mainAxisSpacing: 10,
          crossAxisSpacing: 10,
          childAspectRatio: 1.55,
          children: [
            _kpi('إجمالي الكورسات', '${s.totalCourses}', Icons.assignment,
                AppColors.primary),
            _kpi('كورسات نشطة', '${s.activeCourses}', Icons.play_circle_outline,
                AppColors.success),
            _kpi('كورسات موقوفة', '${s.pausedCourses}',
                Icons.pause_circle_outline, AppColors.error),
            _kpi('كورسات مكتملة', '${s.completedCourses}',
                Icons.check_circle_outline, AppColors.info),
            _kpi('لاعبون منشأون', '${s.playersCreated}', Icons.person_add,
                AppColors.purple),
            _kpi('لاعبون لديهم كورسات', '${s.totalPlayersWithCourses}',
                Icons.people, const Color(0xFFF59E0B)),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        const SectionTitle(title: 'توزيع اللاعبين حسب الجنس'),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: _genderBox(
                icon: Icons.male,
                label: 'ذكور',
                value: '${s.malePlayers}',
                color: const Color(0xFF3B82F6),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Expanded(
              child: _genderBox(
                icon: Icons.female,
                label: 'إناث',
                value: '${s.femalePlayers}',
                color: const Color(0xFFEC4899),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        const SectionTitle(title: 'مؤشر الالتزام العام'),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        SurfaceCard(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              SizedBox(
                width: 140,
                height: 140,
                child: Stack(
                  alignment: Alignment.center,
                  children: [
                    SizedBox(
                      width: 140,
                      height: 140,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                        value: s.adherenceRounded / 100,
                        strokeWidth: 12,
                        backgroundColor:
                            Theme.of(context).dividerColor.withOpacity(0.3),
                        valueColor:
                            AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(s.adherenceColor),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text('${s.adherenceRounded}%',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 28,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              color: s.adherenceColor,
                            )),
                        Text('متوسط الالتزام',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 10.5,
                              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                            )),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: s.adherenceColor.withOpacity(0.08),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  border: Border.all(color: s.adherenceColor.withOpacity(0.25)),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.emoji_events, size: 18, color: s.adherenceColor),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        'التقييم: ${s.adherenceLabel}',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 13,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          color: s.adherenceColor,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                      child: _sumBox('${s.totalExercises}', 'إجمالي تمارين',
                          AppColors.info)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                      child: _sumBox(
                          '${s.doneExercises}', 'منجزة', AppColors.success)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                      child: _sumBox('${(s.progressRatio * 100).round()}%',
                          'نسبة الإنجاز', AppColors.primary)),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        SectionTitle(title: 'كورساته (${s.courses.length})'),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        if (s.courses.isEmpty)
          SurfaceCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            child: Center(
              child: Text('لا توجد كورسات',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
            ),
          )
        else
          ...s.courses.take(20).map((c) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                child: _courseTile(c),
              )),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        SectionTitle(title: 'لاعبوه (${s.players.length})'),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        if (s.players.isEmpty)
          SurfaceCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            child: Center(
              child: Text('لا يوجد لاعبون',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
            ),
          )
        else
          ...s.players.take(30).map((p) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                child: _playerTile(p),
              )),
        const SizedBox(height: 24),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _badge(String label) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: Text(label,
          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
              color: Colors.white,
              fontSize: 11.5,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
    );
  }

  Widget _kpi(String label, String value, IconData icon, Color color) {
    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(7),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, size: 16, color: color),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(label,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          Text(value,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: color)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _genderBox({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    required String value,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: color.withOpacity(0.15),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 24),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(label,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                Text(value,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 22,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: color)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _sumBox(String value, String label, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10, horizontal: 6),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.08),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.2)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Text(value,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: color)),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(label,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 10, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _courseTile(Course c) {
    final statusColor = c.isPaused
        ? AppColors.error
        : c.isCompleted
            ? AppColors.info
            : AppColors.success;
    final statusLabel = c.isPaused
        ? 'موقوف'
        : c.isCompleted
            ? 'مكتمل'
            : 'نشط';

    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(c.player.name,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
              ),
              MiniChip(text: statusLabel, color: statusColor),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.calendar_today,
                  size: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text(dateFmt.format(c.createdAt),
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Icon(c.player.isFemale ? Icons.female : Icons.male,
                  size: 12,
                  color: c.player.isFemale
                      ? const Color(0xFFEC4899)
                      : const Color(0xFF3B82F6)),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text(c.player.isFemale ? 'أنثى' : 'ذكر',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Icon(Icons.fitness_center,
                  size: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text('${c.doneExercises}/${c.totalExercises}',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: c.progress,
              minHeight: 5,
              backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).dividerColor.withOpacity(0.3),
              color: c.isPaused ? AppColors.textSecondary : AppColors.primary,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _playerTile(UserProfile p) {
    String gender = 'male';
    for (final c in _stats?.courses ?? <Course>[]) {
      if (c.targetUserId == p.uid) {
        gender = c.player.gender;
        break;
      }
    }
    final isFemale = gender == 'female';
    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 34,
            height: 34,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.15),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            ),
            child: Text(
              p.displayName.isNotEmpty ? p.displayName[0] : '?',
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                color: AppColors.primary,
                fontSize: 14,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(children: [
                  Flexible(
                    child: Text(p.displayName,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                  ),
                  if (p.isVerified) ...[
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Icon(Icons.verified, color: AppColors.info, size: 13),
                  ],
                ]),
                Row(children: [
                  Icon(isFemale ? Icons.female : Icons.male,
                      size: 11,
                      color: isFemale
                          ? const Color(0xFFEC4899)
                          : const Color(0xFF3B82F6)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 3),
                  Text(isFemale ? 'أنثى' : 'ذكر',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 10, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Flexible(
                    child: Text(p.email,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 10.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                  ),
                ]),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          MiniChip(
            text: p.subscriptionLabel,
            color:
                p.hasActiveSubscription ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// PALETTE SETTINGS SCREEN
// ============================================================
class PaletteSettingsScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const PaletteSettingsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ValueListenableBuilder<AppPaletteMode>(
      valueListenable: AppThemeController.mode,
      builder: (context, mode, _) => Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('ألوان التطبيق')),
        body: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          children: [
            const SectionTitle(title: 'اختر ثيم الألوان'),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Text('يتم تطبيق الثيم على كل شاشات التطبيق ويُحفظ تلقائياً.',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            _paletteCard(
              context,
              current: mode,
              value: AppPaletteMode.orange,
              title: 'الثيم البرتقالي',
              subtitle: 'الافتراضي • طاقة وحيوية',
              palette: kOrangePalette,
              icon: Icons.local_fire_department,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            _paletteCard(
              context,
              current: mode,
              value: AppPaletteMode.pink,
              title: 'ثيم البنات',
              subtitle: 'وردي أنيق وناعم',
              palette: kPinkPalette,
              icon: Icons.favorite,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _paletteCard(
    BuildContext context, {
    required AppPaletteMode current,
    required AppPaletteMode value,
    required String title,
    required String subtitle,
    required AppPalette palette,
    required IconData icon,
  }) {
    final selected = current == value;
    final primary = Color(palette.primary);
    final accent = Color(palette.accent);
    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      onTap: () => AppThemeController.setMode(value),
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          border: Border.all(
            color: selected ? primary : Colors.transparent,
            width: 2,
          ),
        ),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Container(
              width: 52,
              height: 52,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  colors: [primary, accent],
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                ),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
              ),
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              child: Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 26),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 14),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(title,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 3),
                  Text(subtitle,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Icon(
              selected
                  ? Icons.radio_button_checked
                  : Icons.radio_button_unchecked,
              color: selected ? primary : AppColors.textSecondary,
              size: 22,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// SEED EXERCISES SCREEN
// ============================================================
class SeedExercisesScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SeedExercisesScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<SeedExercisesScreen> createState() => _SeedExercisesScreenState();
}

class _SeedExercisesScreenState extends State<SeedExercisesScreen> {
  bool _running = false;
  bool _onlyMissing = true;
  int _current = 0;
  int _total = 0;
  int? _added;

  Future<void> _run() async {
    setState(() {
      _running = true;
      _current = 0;
      _total = kDefaultExercises.length;
      _added = null;
    });
    try {
      final added = await FirebaseService.instance.seedDefaultExercises(
        onlyMissing: _onlyMissing,
        onProgress: (c, t) {
          if (mounted) {
            setState(() {
              _current = c;
              _total = t;
            });
          }
        },
      );
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _added = added;
          _running = false;
        });
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text(added > 0
                ? 'تم رفع $added تمرين بنجاح'
                : 'لا توجد تمارين جديدة للرفع.'),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _running = false);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('رفع مكتبة التمارين')),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          GradientCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(children: [
                  const Icon(Icons.info_outline, color: Colors.white, size: 22),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Text('معلومات',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 16,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: Colors.white)),
                ]),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  'سيتم رفع مكتبة تمارين عربية شاملة إلى مجموعة exercises في Firebase.',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    fontSize: 13,
                    height: 1.6,
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          SurfaceCard(
            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            child: SwitchListTile(
              value: _onlyMissing,
              onChanged:
                  _running ? null : (v) => setState(() => _onlyMissing = v),
              title: Text('تخطي التمارين الموجودة مسبقًا',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
              subtitle: Text('سيتم رفع التمارين الجديدة فقط',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          SurfaceCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text('إجمالي التمارين: ${kDefaultExercises.length}',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 14.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                if (_running || _added != null) ...[
                  ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                    child: LinearProgressIndicator(
                      value: _total == 0 ? 0 : _current / _total,
                      minHeight: 8,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text('$_current / $_total',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12)),
                ],
                if (_added != null)
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                    child: Text('تمت الإضافة: $_added تمرين',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 14,
                            color: AppColors.success,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          GradientButton(
            label: _running ? 'جارٍ الرفع...' : 'بدء الرفع للسيرفر',
            icon: Icons.cloud_upload,
            loading: _running,
            onPressed: _running ? null : _run,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// EXERCISES SCREEN
// ============================================================
class ExercisesScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool isAdmin;
  final UserProfile profile;
  const ExercisesScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.isAdmin,
    required this.profile,
  });

  @override
  State<ExercisesScreen> createState() => _ExercisesScreenState();
}

class _ExercisesScreenState extends State<ExercisesScreen> {
  String _query = '';
  String? _muscle;
  String _filter = 'all';

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(widget.isAdmin ? 'قاعدة التمارين (السيرفر)' : 'التمارين'),
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: () async {
          await Navigator.push(
            context,
            MaterialPageRoute(
              builder: (_) => AddExerciseScreen(saveToServer: widget.isAdmin),
            ),
          );
          if (mounted) setState(() {});
        },
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
        label: Text(
          widget.isAdmin ? 'إضافة تمرين للسيرفر' : 'إضافة تمرين محلي',
          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        ),
      ),
      body: StreamBuilder<List<Exercise>>(
        stream: FirebaseService.instance.exercisesStream(),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          final serverList = snap.data ?? [];
          final localList = widget.isAdmin
              ? <Exercise>[]
              : StorageService.instance.loadLocalExercises();
          final serverIds = serverList.map((e) => e.id).toSet();
          final all = <MapEntry<Exercise, bool>>[
            ...serverList.map((e) => MapEntry(e, true)),
            ...localList
                .where((e) => !serverIds.contains(e.id))
                .map((e) => MapEntry(e, false)),
          ];

          final filtered = all.where((entry) {
            final e = entry.key;
            final isServer = entry.value;
            if (_filter == 'server' && !isServer) return false;
            if (_filter == 'local' && isServer) return false;
            if (_filter == 'mine' &&
                !(isServer &&
                    widget.profile.isCaptain &&
                    e.createdByUid == widget.profile.uid)) return false;
            if (_query.isNotEmpty &&
                !e.nameAr.toLowerCase().contains(_query.toLowerCase()) &&
                !e.nameEn.toLowerCase().contains(_query.toLowerCase())) {
              return false;
            }
            if (_muscle != null && e.primaryMuscle != _muscle) return false;
            return true;
          }).toList();

          return Column(children: [
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              child: TextField(
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  hintText: 'ابحث...',
                  prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search),
                ),
                onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _query = v),
              ),
            ),
            if (widget.profile.isCaptain)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                child: Row(children: [
                  _filterChip('all', 'الكل'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  _filterChip('server', 'سيرفر'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  _filterChip('mine', 'تماريني'),
                ]),
              )
            else if (!widget.isAdmin)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                child: Row(children: [
                  _filterChip('all', 'الكل'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  _filterChip('server', 'سيرفر'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  _filterChip('local', 'محلي'),
                ]),
              ),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            SizedBox(
              height: 40,
              child: ListView(
                scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                children: [
                  ChoiceChip(
                    label: Text('الكل', style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12)),
                    selected: _muscle == null,
                    onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _muscle = null),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  ...muscleGroups.take(16).map((m) => Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 6),
                        child: ChoiceChip(
                          label:
                              Text(m, style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12)),
                          selected: _muscle == m,
                          onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _muscle = m),
                        ),
                      )),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Expanded(
              child: filtered.isEmpty
                  ? Center(
                      child: Text('لا توجد تمارين',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                    )
                  : ListView.builder(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                      itemCount: filtered.length,
                      itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                        final entry = filtered[i];
                        return _card(context, entry.key, entry.value);
                      },
                    ),
            ),
          ]);
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _filterChip(String val, String label) {
    return ChoiceChip(
      label: Text(label, style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12)),
      selected: _filter == val,
      onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _filter = val),
    );
  }

  Widget _card(BuildContext context, Exercise e, bool isServer) {
    final canEdit = !isServer ? true : widget.profile.canEditServerExercise(e);
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      child: SurfaceCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        onTap: () => Navigator.push(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(
            builder: (_) => ExerciseDetailScreen(
              exercise: e,
              isServer: isServer,
              profile: widget.profile,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        child: Row(children: [
          if (e.imageUrl != null && e.imageUrl!.isNotEmpty)
            ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              child: e.imageUrl!.startsWith('http')
                  ? CachedNetworkImage(
                      imageUrl: e.imageUrl!,
                      width: 60,
                      height: 60,
                      fit: BoxFit.cover,
                      errorWidget: (_, __, ___) => _placeholder(),
                    )
                  : Image.file(File(e.imageUrl!),
                      width: 60,
                      height: 60,
                      fit: BoxFit.cover,
                      errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => _placeholder()),
            )
          else
            _placeholder(),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(e.nameAr,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14.5)),
                Text(e.nameEn,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                Wrap(
                  spacing: 4,
                  runSpacing: 4,
                  children: [
                    MiniChip(text: e.primaryMuscle, color: AppColors.primary),
                    if (e.videoUrl != null && e.videoUrl!.isNotEmpty)
                      MiniChip(text: 'فيديو', color: AppColors.error),
                    MiniChip(
                      text: isServer ? 'سيرفر' : 'محلي',
                      color: isServer ? AppColors.success : AppColors.info,
                    ),
                    if (isServer &&
                        widget.profile.isCaptain &&
                        e.createdByUid == widget.profile.uid)
                      const MiniChip(
                        text: 'من رفعي',
                        color: Color(0xFFF59E0B),
                        icon: Icons.person,
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          if (canEdit)
            Icon(Icons.edit, size: 18, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _placeholder() => Container(
        width: 60,
        height: 60,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.12),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        ),
        child: Icon(Icons.fitness_center, color: AppColors.primary),
      );
}

// ============================================================
// ADD EXERCISE SCREEN
// ============================================================
class AddExerciseScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final Exercise? existing;
  final bool saveToServer;
  const AddExerciseScreen({
    super.key,
    this.existing,
    this.saveToServer = true,
  });

  @override
  State<AddExerciseScreen> createState() => _AddExerciseScreenState();
}

class _AddExerciseScreenState extends State<AddExerciseScreen> {
  final _form = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  late TextEditingController _ar, _en, _desc;
  String _primary = muscleGroups.first;
  List<String> _secondary = [];
  String _type = exerciseTypes.first;
  String _equipment = equipmentList.first;
  String _difficulty = difficulties.first;
  File? _video;
  File? _image;
  String? _videoUrl;
  String? _imageUrl;
  bool _saving = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final e = widget.existing;
    _ar = TextEditingController(text: e?.nameAr ?? '');
    _en = TextEditingController(text: e?.nameEn ?? '');
    _desc = TextEditingController(text: e?.description ?? '');
    if (e != null) {
      _primary = e.primaryMuscle;
      _secondary = List.from(e.secondaryMuscles);
      _type = e.type;
      _equipment = e.equipment;
      _difficulty = e.difficulty;
      _videoUrl = e.videoUrl;
      _imageUrl = e.imageUrl;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _ar.dispose();
    _en.dispose();
    _desc.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _pickVideo() async {
    final p = ImagePicker();
    final v = await p.pickVideo(source: ImageSource.gallery);
    if (v != null) setState(() => _video = File(v.path));
  }

  Future<void> _pickImage() async {
    final p = ImagePicker();
    final i = await p.pickImage(source: ImageSource.gallery);
    if (i != null) setState(() => _image = File(i.path));
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    if (!_form.currentState!.validate()) return;
    setState(() => _saving = true);
    try {
      final uid = FirebaseService.instance.currentUser?.uid ?? 'local';
      final ts = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;
      final isNew = widget.existing == null;

      if (widget.saveToServer) {
        if (_video != null) {
          _videoUrl = await FirebaseService.instance
              .uploadVideo(_video!, '${uid}_$ts.mp4');
        }
        if (_image != null) {
          _imageUrl = await FirebaseService.instance
              .uploadImage(_image!, '${uid}_$ts.jpg');
        }
      } else {
        _videoUrl ??= _video?.path;
        _imageUrl ??= _image?.path;
      }

      final ex = Exercise(
        id: widget.existing?.id,
        nameAr: _ar.text.trim(),
        nameEn: _en.text.trim(),
        primaryMuscle: _primary,
        secondaryMuscles: _secondary,
        type: _type,
        equipment: _equipment,
        difficulty: _difficulty,
        description: _desc.text.trim(),
        videoUrl: _videoUrl,
        imageUrl: _imageUrl,
        createdBy: widget.existing?.createdBy ?? 'user',
        createdByUid: widget.existing?.createdByUid ?? '',
      );

      if (widget.saveToServer) {
        await FirebaseService.instance.saveExercise(ex, isNew: isNew);
      } else {
        await StorageService.instance.upsertLocalExercise(ex);
      }

      if (mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _saving = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(widget.existing == null
            ? (widget.saveToServer ? 'تمرين جديد (سيرفر)' : 'تمرين جديد (محلي)')
            : 'تعديل التمرين'),
      ),
      body: Form(
        key: _form,
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          children: [
            TextFormField(
              controller: _ar,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'الاسم بالعربية'),
              validator: (v) => v == null || v.trim().isEmpty ? 'مطلوب' : null,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextFormField(
              controller: _en,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'الاسم بالإنجليزية'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
              value: _primary,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'العضلة الأساسية'),
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
              items: muscleGroups
                  .map((m) => DropdownMenuItem(value: m, child: Text(m)))
                  .toList(),
              onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _primary = v!),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
              value: _type,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'نوع التمرين'),
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
              items: exerciseTypes
                  .map((m) => DropdownMenuItem(value: m, child: Text(m)))
                  .toList(),
              onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _type = v!),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
              value: _equipment,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'المعدات'),
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
              items: equipmentList
                  .map((m) => DropdownMenuItem(value: m, child: Text(m)))
                  .toList(),
              onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _equipment = v!),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
              value: _difficulty,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'مستوى الصعوبة'),
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
              items: difficulties
                  .map((m) => DropdownMenuItem(value: m, child: Text(m)))
                  .toList(),
              onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _difficulty = v!),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextFormField(
              controller: _desc,
              maxLines: 3,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'الوصف'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            const SectionTitle(title: 'الوسائط'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(children: [
              Expanded(
                child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: _pickVideo,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.videocam),
                  label: Text(_video != null || _videoUrl != null
                      ? 'فيديو ✓'
                      : 'إضافة فيديو'),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: _pickImage,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.image),
                  label: Text(
                      _image != null || _imageUrl != null ? 'صورة ✓' : 'صورة'),
                ),
              ),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            GradientButton(
              label: 'حفظ',
              icon: Icons.save,
              loading: _saving,
              onPressed: _save,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// EXERCISE DETAIL SCREEN
// ============================================================
class ExerciseDetailScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final Exercise exercise;
  final bool isServer;
  final UserProfile profile;
  const ExerciseDetailScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.exercise,
    this.isServer = true,
    required this.profile,
  });

  bool get _canEdit {
    if (!isServer) return true;
    return profile.canEditServerExercise(exercise);
  }

  bool get _canDelete {
    if (!isServer) return true;
    return profile.canDeleteServerExercise(exercise);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(exercise.nameAr),
        actions: [
          if (_canEdit)
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.edit),
              onPressed: () => Navigator.push(
                context,
                MaterialPageRoute(
                  builder: (_) => AddExerciseScreen(
                    existing: exercise,
                    saveToServer: isServer,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          if (_canDelete)
            IconButton(
              icon: Icon(Icons.delete, color: AppColors.error),
              onPressed: () async {
                final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
                  context: context,
                  builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
                    title: const Text('حذف التمرين'),
                    content: Text('هل تريد حذف "${exercise.nameAr}"؟'),
                    actions: [
                      TextButton(
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
                        child: const Text('إلغاء'),
                      ),
                      FilledButton(
                        style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: AppColors.error),
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
                        child: const Text('حذف'),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                );
                if (ok == true) {
                  try {
                    if (isServer) {
                      await FirebaseService.instance
                          .deleteExercise(exercise.id);
                    } else {
                      await StorageService.instance
                          .deleteLocalExercise(exercise.id);
                    }
                    if (context.mounted) Navigator.pop(context);
                  } catch (e) {
                    if (context.mounted) {
                      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
                          .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
                    }
                  }
                }
              },
            ),
        ],
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          Wrap(
            spacing: 6,
            runSpacing: 6,
            children: [
              MiniChip(
                text: isServer ? 'تمرين سيرفر (عام)' : 'تمرين محلي',
                color: isServer ? AppColors.success : AppColors.info,
                icon: isServer ? Icons.cloud : Icons.phone_android,
              ),
              if (isServer && exercise.createdByUid.isNotEmpty)
                MiniChip(
                  text: exercise.createdByUid == profile.uid
                      ? 'من رفعي'
                      : 'من ${_roleLabelOf(exercise.createdBy)}',
                  color: exercise.createdByUid == profile.uid
                      ? const Color(0xFFF59E0B)
                      : AppColors.purple,
                  icon: Icons.person,
                ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          if (exercise.videoUrl != null && exercise.videoUrl!.isNotEmpty)
            ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              child: VideoPlayerWidget(url: exercise.videoUrl!),
            ),
          if (exercise.imageUrl != null && exercise.imageUrl!.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              child: exercise.imageUrl!.startsWith('http')
                  ? CachedNetworkImage(
                      imageUrl: exercise.imageUrl!, fit: BoxFit.cover)
                  : Image.file(File(exercise.imageUrl!), fit: BoxFit.cover),
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          SurfaceCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(exercise.nameEn,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                const Divider(height: 24),
                _row('العضلة', exercise.primaryMuscle),
                _row('النوع', exercise.type),
                _row('المعدات', exercise.equipment),
                _row('الصعوبة', exercise.difficulty),
                if (exercise.description.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const Divider(height: 24),
                  Text(exercise.description,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 13, height: 1.6)),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  String _roleLabelOf(String role) {
    switch (role) {
      case 'superAdmin':
        return 'إشراف عام';
      case 'admin':
        return 'مدير';
      case 'captain':
        return 'كابتن';
      default:
        return 'مستخدم';
    }
  }

  Widget _row(String k, String v) => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 5),
        child: Row(children: [
          SizedBox(
            width: 110,
            child: Text(k,
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12.5)),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(v,
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
          ),
        ]),
      );
}

// ============================================================
// VIDEO PLAYER WIDGET
// ============================================================
class VideoPlayerWidget extends StatefulWidget {
  final String url;
  const VideoPlayerWidget({super.key, required this.url});

  @override
  State<VideoPlayerWidget> createState() => _VideoPlayerWidgetState();
}

class _VideoPlayerWidgetState extends State<VideoPlayerWidget> {
  VideoPlayerController? _controller;
  bool _ready = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final isRemote =
        widget.url.startsWith('http://') || widget.url.startsWith('https://');
    try {
      _controller = isRemote
          ? VideoPlayerController.networkUrl(Uri.parse(widget.url))
          : VideoPlayerController.file(File(widget.url));
      _controller!.initialize().then((_) {
        if (mounted) setState(() => _ready = true);
      }).catchError((_) {
        if (mounted) setState(() => _ready = false);
      });
    } catch (_) {
      _ready = false;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller?.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_controller == null || !_ready) {
      return const AspectRatio(
        aspectRatio: 16 / 9,
        child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      );
    }
    return AspectRatio(
      aspectRatio: _controller!.value.aspectRatio,
      child: Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        children: [
          VideoPlayer(_controller!),
          IconButton(
            iconSize: 60,
            color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9),
            icon: Icon(_controller!.value.isPlaying
                ? Icons.pause_circle_filled
                : Icons.play_circle_fill),
            onPressed: () {
              setState(() {
                _controller!.value.isPlaying
                    ? _controller!.pause()
                    : _controller!.play();
              });
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// PLAYERS SCREEN
// ============================================================
class PlayersScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final UserProfile profile;
  const PlayersScreen({super.key, required this.profile});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isAdmin = profile.isAdmin || profile.isSuperAdmin;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(isAdmin ? 'كل اللاعبين' : 'لاعبيّ')),
      body: FutureBuilder<List<UserProfile>>(
        future: isAdmin
            ? FirebaseService.instance.getAllUsers()
            : FirebaseService.instance.getPlayersForCoach(),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          if (!snap.hasData) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          }
          final users = snap.data!.where((u) => !u.canManageCourses).toList();
          if (users.isEmpty) {
            return Center(
              child: Text(
                isAdmin ? 'لا يوجد لاعبون' : 'لم تُنشئ أي لاعبين بعد',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              ),
            );
          }
          return ListView.builder(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            itemCount: users.length,
            itemBuilder: (context, i) {
              final u = users[i];
              return Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                child: SurfaceCard(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                  onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                    context,
                    MaterialPageRoute(
                      builder: (_) => PlayerDetailsScreen(
                        player: u,
                        me: profile,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Row(children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 44,
                      height: 44,
                      alignment: Alignment.center,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        u.displayName.isNotEmpty ? u.displayName[0] : '?',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontSize: 18,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            children: [
                              Flexible(
                                child: Text(u.displayName,
                                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                        fontSize: 14.5),
                                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                              ),
                              if (u.isVerified) ...[
                                const SizedBox(width: 5),
                                Icon(Icons.verified,
                                    color: AppColors.info, size: 15),
                              ],
                            ],
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 3),
                          Text(u.email,
                              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                  fontSize: 11.5,
                                  color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          MiniChip(
                            text: u.subscriptionLabel,
                            color: u.hasActiveSubscription
                                ? AppColors.success
                                : AppColors.error,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios,
                        size: 14, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  ]),
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// COURSES SCREEN
// ============================================================
class CoursesScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final bool isAdmin;
  final UserProfile profile;
  const CoursesScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.isAdmin,
    required this.profile,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final canManage = profile.canManageCourses;
    return DefaultTabController(
      length: canManage ? 2 : 1,
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(
          title: const Text('الكورسات'),
          bottom: canManage
              ? const TabBar(
                  tabs: [
                    Tab(text: 'التي أنشأتها'),
                    Tab(text: 'كورساتي كلاعب'),
                  ],
                )
              : null,
        ),
        body: canManage
            ? TabBarView(
                children: [
                  _MyCreatedCoursesTab(profile: profile),
                  _MyPlayerCoursesTab(profile: profile),
                ],
              )
            : _MyPlayerCoursesTab(profile: profile),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _MyCreatedCoursesTab extends StatelessWidget {
  final UserProfile profile;
  const _MyCreatedCoursesTab({required this.profile});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return StreamBuilder<List<Course>>(
      stream: FirebaseService.instance.myCreatedCoursesStream(),
      builder: (context, snap) {
        if (snap.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        }
        final list = snap.data ?? [];
        if (list.isEmpty) {
          return Center(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.assignment_outlined,
                    size: 60, color: AppColors.textSecondary.withOpacity(0.5)),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Text('لم تُنشئ أي كورسات بعد',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14)),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }
        return ListView.builder(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          itemCount: list.length,
          itemBuilder: (context, i) => Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
            child: _ManagedCourseCard(
              course: list[i],
              profile: profile,
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class _ManagedCourseCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Course course;
  final UserProfile profile;
  const _ManagedCourseCard({required this.course, required this.profile});

  Color _statusColor(Course c) {
    if (c.isPaused) return AppColors.error;
    if (c.isCompleted) return AppColors.info;
    return AppColors.success;
  }

  String _statusLabel(Course c) {
    if (c.isPaused) return 'موقوف';
    if (c.isCompleted) return 'مكتمل';
    if (c.isActive) return 'نشط';
    return 'لم يبدأ';
  }

  Future<void> _pause(BuildContext context, Course c) async {
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.setCourseStatus(c.id, 'paused');
      await NotificationService.instance
          .showCoursePaused(playerName: c.player.name, courseId: c.id);
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('تم إيقاف الكورس')));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _resume(BuildContext context, Course c) async {
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.setCourseStatus(c.id, 'active');
      await NotificationService.instance
          .showCourseResumed(playerName: c.player.name, courseId: c.id);
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('تم تفعيل الكورس')));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _complete(BuildContext context, Course c) async {
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.setCourseStatus(c.id, 'completed');
