      await NotificationService.instance
          .showCourseCompleted(playerName: c.player.name, courseId: c.id);
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            const SnackBar(content: Text('تم تحديد الكورس كمكتمل')));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _delete(BuildContext context, Course c) async {
    final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('حذف الكورس'),
        content: Text('هل تريد حذف كورس "${c.player.name}"؟'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('إلغاء'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.error),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text('حذف'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (ok != true) return;
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.deleteCourse(c.id);
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('تم حذف الكورس')));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _openPlayerProfile(BuildContext context, Course c) async {
    final player =
        await FirebaseService.instance.getUserProfileById(c.targetUserId);
    if (!context.mounted) return;
    if (player == null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('لم يتم العثور على حساب اللاعب')),
      );
      return;
    }
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => PlayerDetailsScreen(
          player: player,
          course: c,
          courseId: c.id,
          me: profile,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return StreamBuilder<Course?>(
      stream: FirebaseService.instance.courseByIdStream(course.id),
      builder: (context, snap) {
        final c = snap.data ?? course;
        return _buildCard(context, c);
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCard(BuildContext context, Course c) {
    final statusColor = _statusColor(c);
    final statusLabel = _statusLabel(c);

    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(children: [
            Container(
              width: 44,
              height: 44,
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.25),
                    blurRadius: 10,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              child: Text(
                c.player.name.isNotEmpty ? c.player.name[0] : '?',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  fontSize: 18,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(children: [
                    Flexible(
                      child: Text(c.player.name,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 14.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Icon(c.player.isFemale ? Icons.female : Icons.male,
                        size: 14,
                        color: c.player.isFemale
                            ? const Color(0xFFEC4899)
                            : const Color(0xFF3B82F6)),
                  ]),
                  const SizedBox(height: 3),
                  Text('${c.player.goal} • ${c.days.length} أيام',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            MiniChip(text: statusLabel, color: statusColor),
          ]),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(
              child: ClipRRect(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                child: LinearProgressIndicator(
                  value: c.progress,
                  minHeight: 6,
                  backgroundColor:
                      Theme.of(context).dividerColor.withOpacity(0.3),
                  color:
                      c.isPaused ? AppColors.textSecondary : AppColors.primary,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Text('${(c.progress * 100).round()}%',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 13,
                    color: c.isPaused
                        ? AppColors.textSecondary
                        : AppColors.primary)),
          ]),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 7),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: c.adherenceColor.withOpacity(0.08),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              border: Border.all(color: c.adherenceColor.withOpacity(0.25)),
            ),
            child: Row(children: [
              Icon(Icons.trending_up, size: 15, color: c.adherenceColor),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Text('الالتزام: ',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
              Text('${c.adherenceScore}%',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: c.adherenceColor)),
              const SizedBox(width: 6),
              Text('(${c.adherenceLabel})',
                  style:
                      GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 11, color: c.adherenceColor)),
              const Spacer(),
              Text('${c.doneExercises}/${c.totalExercises}',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 11,
                      color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
            ]),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          CourseMessagesBubble(
            courseId: c.id,
            courseName: c.player.name,
            me: profile,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          Wrap(
            spacing: 6,
            runSpacing: 6,
            children: [
              _action(
                icon: Icons.person,
                label: 'بروفايل اللاعب',
                color: AppColors.info,
                onTap: () => _openPlayerProfile(context, c),
              ),
              _action(
                icon: Icons.edit,
                label: 'تعديل',
                color: AppColors.purple,
                onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                  context,
                  MaterialPageRoute(
                    builder: (_) => CourseBuilderScreen(
                      course: c,
                      saveToServer: true,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              if (c.isPaused)
                _action(
                  icon: Icons.play_arrow,
                  label: 'تفعيل',
                  color: AppColors.success,
                  onTap: () => _resume(context, c),
                )
              else if (!c.isCompleted)
                _action(
                  icon: Icons.pause,
                  label: 'إيقاف',
                  color: AppColors.error,
                  onTap: () => _pause(context, c),
                ),
              if (!c.isCompleted)
                _action(
                  icon: Icons.check_circle,
                  label: 'إنهاء',
                  color: AppColors.info,
                  onTap: () => _complete(context, c),
                ),
              if (!c.isPaused)
                _action(
                  icon: Icons.picture_as_pdf,
                  label: 'PDF',
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  onTap: () => PdfService.instance.preview(c),
                ),
              if (!c.isPaused)
                _action(
                  icon: Icons.share,
                  label: 'مشاركة',
                  color: AppColors.accent,
                  onTap: () => PdfService.instance.share(c),
                ),
              _action(
                icon: Icons.delete,
                label: 'حذف',
                color: AppColors.error,
                onTap: () => _delete(context, c),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _action({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    required VoidCallback onTap,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return TextButton.icon(
      onPressed: onTap,
      style: TextButton.styleFrom(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
        minimumSize: const Size(0, 34),
        tapTargetSize: MaterialTapTargetSize.shrinkWrap,
        foregroundColor: color,
        visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact,
      ),
      icon: Icon(icon, size: 15),
      label: Text(label,
          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
    );
  }
}

class _MyPlayerCoursesTab extends StatelessWidget {
  final UserProfile profile;
  const _MyPlayerCoursesTab({required this.profile});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final canManage = profile.canManageCourses;
    return StreamBuilder<List<Course>>(
      stream: FirebaseService.instance.myCoursesStream(),
      builder: (context, snap) {
        if (snap.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        }
        final remote = snap.data ?? [];
        final local =
            canManage ? <Course>[] : StorageService.instance.loadLocalCourses();
        final all = [...remote, ...local];

        if (all.isEmpty) {
          return Center(
            child: Text('لا توجد كورسات',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
          );
        }

        return ListView.builder(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          itemCount: all.length,
          itemBuilder: (context, i) => Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
            child: _PlayerCourseCard(
              course: all[i],
              isAdmin: canManage,
              me: profile,
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class _PlayerCourseCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Course course;
  final bool isAdmin;
  final UserProfile? me;
  const _PlayerCourseCard({
    required this.course,
    required this.isAdmin,
    this.me,
  });

  Future<void> _openCoachProfile(BuildContext context, Course c) async {
    if (c.ownerId.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('هذا كورس محلي بدون مدرب')),
      );
      return;
    }
    Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => CoachProfileScreen(
          coachUid: c.ownerId,
          course: c,
          me: me,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return StreamBuilder<Course?>(
      stream: FirebaseService.instance.courseByIdStream(course.id),
      builder: (context, snap) {
        final c = snap.data ?? course;
        return _buildCard(context, c);
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCard(BuildContext context, Course c) {
    final isLocal = c.source != 'superAdmin' &&
        c.source != 'admin' &&
        c.source != 'captain';
    final p = c.player;
    final paused = c.isPaused;

    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      onTap: paused
          ? null
          : () => Navigator.push(
                context,
                MaterialPageRoute(
                  builder: (_) => CoursePlayerScreen(
                    course: c,
                    isAdmin: isAdmin,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(children: [
            Container(
              width: 44,
              height: 44,
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.25),
                    blurRadius: 10,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 4),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              child: Text(
                c.player.name.isNotEmpty ? c.player.name[0] : '?',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  fontSize: 18,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(children: [
                    Flexible(
                      child: Text(c.player.name,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 14.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Icon(p.isFemale ? Icons.female : Icons.male,
                        size: 14,
                        color: p.isFemale
                            ? const Color(0xFFEC4899)
                            : const Color(0xFF3B82F6)),
                  ]),
                  const SizedBox(height: 3),
                  Text('${c.player.goal} • ${c.days.length} أيام',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            if (paused)
              MiniChip(
                text: 'موقوف',
                color: AppColors.error,
                icon: Icons.pause_circle,
              )
            else
              MiniChip(
                text: isLocal
                    ? 'محلي'
                    : c.source == 'superAdmin'
                        ? 'من الإشراف'
                        : c.source == 'admin'
                            ? 'من الإدارة'
                            : 'من الكابتن',
                color: isLocal
                    ? AppColors.info
                    : c.source == 'superAdmin'
                        ? const Color(0xFFDB2777)
                        : c.source == 'admin'
                            ? AppColors.success
                            : AppColors.purple,
              ),
          ]),
          if (c.isPrivate) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: const LinearGradient(
                  colors: [Color(0xFF8B5CF6), Color(0xFFEC4899)],
                  begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                  end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                ),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: const Color(0xFF8B5CF6).withOpacity(0.3),
                    blurRadius: 8,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 2),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  const Icon(Icons.lock_person, color: Colors.white, size: 14),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  Text(
                    'أنت لاعب خاص',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
          if (c.coachLabel.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            InkWell(
              onTap: () => _openCoachProfile(context, c),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              child: Container(
                padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.purple.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.sports, size: 14, color: AppColors.purple),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Flexible(
                      child: Text(
                        c.coachLabel,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          color: AppColors.purple,
                        ),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios,
                        size: 10, color: AppColors.purple),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
          if (me != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            CourseMessagesBubble(
              courseId: c.id,
              courseName: c.player.name,
              me: me!,
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          Row(children: [
            Expanded(
              child: ClipRRect(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                child: LinearProgressIndicator(
                  value: c.progress,
                  minHeight: 6,
                  backgroundColor:
                      Theme.of(context).dividerColor.withOpacity(0.3),
                  color: paused ? AppColors.textSecondary : AppColors.primary,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Text('${(c.progress * 100).round()}%',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 13,
                    color:
                        paused ? AppColors.textSecondary : AppColors.primary)),
          ]),
          if (paused) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.error.withOpacity(0.08),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                border: Border.all(color: AppColors.error.withOpacity(0.3)),
              ),
              child: Row(children: [
                Icon(Icons.pause_circle_outline,
                    color: AppColors.error, size: 18),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    'تم إيقاف الكورس مؤقتاً من قِبل المدرب. لا يمكن بدء التمرين حالياً.',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 11.5,
                      color: AppColors.error,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ]),
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Wrap(
            spacing: 4,
            runSpacing: 4,
            children: [
              if (!paused)
                _smallBtn(
                  icon: Icons.play_arrow,
                  label: 'ابدأ',
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                    context,
                    MaterialPageRoute(
                      builder: (_) =>
                          CoursePlayerScreen(course: c, isAdmin: isAdmin),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              if (!paused)
                _smallBtn(
                  icon: Icons.visibility,
                  label: 'معاينة',
                  color: AppColors.info,
                  onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                    context,
                    MaterialPageRoute(
                      builder: (_) => CoursePreviewScreen(course: c),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              if (!paused)
                _smallBtn(
                  icon: Icons.picture_as_pdf,
                  label: 'PDF',
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  onTap: () => PdfService.instance.preview(c),
                ),
              if (!paused)
                _smallBtn(
                  icon: Icons.share,
                  label: 'مشاركة',
                  color: AppColors.accent,
                  onTap: () => PdfService.instance.share(c),
                ),
              if (isLocal)
                _smallBtn(
                  icon: Icons.edit,
                  label: 'تعديل',
                  color: AppColors.purple,
                  onTap: () async {
                    await Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(
                        builder: (_) => CourseBuilderScreen(
                          course: c,
                          saveToServer: false,
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              if (isLocal)
                _smallBtn(
                  icon: Icons.delete,
                  label: 'حذف',
                  color: AppColors.error,
                  onTap: () async {
                    await StorageService.instance.deleteLocalCourse(c.id);
                    if (context.mounted) {
                      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                        const SnackBar(content: Text('تم حذف الكورس المحلي')),
                      );
                    }
                  },
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _smallBtn({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    required VoidCallback onTap,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return TextButton.icon(
      onPressed: onTap,
      style: TextButton.styleFrom(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
        minimumSize: const Size(0, 34),
        tapTargetSize: MaterialTapTargetSize.shrinkWrap,
        foregroundColor: color,
        visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact,
      ),
      icon: Icon(icon, size: 15),
      label: Text(label,
          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// COURSE MESSAGES BUBBLE
// ============================================================
class CourseMessagesBubble extends StatelessWidget {
  final String courseId;
  final String courseName;
  final UserProfile me;
  const CourseMessagesBubble({
    super.key,
    required this.courseId,
    required this.courseName,
    required this.me,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return StreamBuilder<List<CourseMessage>>(
      stream: FirebaseService.instance.courseMessagesStream(courseId),
      builder: (context, snap) {
        final messages = snap.data ?? [];
        final hasMessages = messages.isNotEmpty;
        final last = hasMessages ? messages.last : null;
        final count = messages.length;

        return Material(
          color: Colors.transparent,
          child: InkWell(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
            onTap: () => Navigator.push(
              context,
              MaterialPageRoute(
                builder: (_) => CourseMessagesScreen(
                  courseId: courseId,
                  courseName: courseName,
                  me: me,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: hasMessages
                    ? AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.08)
                    : Theme.of(context).dividerColor.withOpacity(0.15),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                border: Border.all(
                  color: hasMessages
                      ? AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.35)
                      : Theme.of(context).dividerColor.withOpacity(0.4),
                ),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      gradient: hasMessages ? AppColors.primaryGradient : null,
                      color: hasMessages
                          ? null
                          : AppColors.textSecondary.withOpacity(0.2),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    ),
                    child: Icon(
                      Icons.chat_bubble_outline,
                      color:
                          hasMessages ? Colors.white : AppColors.textSecondary,
                      size: 18,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            Expanded(
                              child: Text(
                                hasMessages
                                    ? 'آخر رسالة من ${last!.senderName}'
                                    : 'المحادثة مع المدرب',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                  fontSize: 12,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  color: hasMessages
                                      ? AppColors.primary
                                      : AppColors.textSecondary,
                                ),
                                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: 8),
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.15),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                              ),
                              child: Text(
                                '$count/${FirebaseService.kMaxCourseMessages}',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                  fontSize: 10,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  color: AppColors.primary,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 3),
                        Text(
                          hasMessages
                              ? last!.text
                              : 'اضغط لبدء المحادثة مع المدرب',
                          maxLines: 1,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 11.5,
                            color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                            fontStyle: hasMessages
                                ? FontStyle.normal
                                : FontStyle.italic,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Icon(
                    Icons.arrow_forward_ios,
                    size: 12,
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// COURSE MESSAGES SCREEN
// ============================================================
class CourseMessagesScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String courseId;
  final String courseName;
  final UserProfile me;
  const CourseMessagesScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.courseId,
    required this.courseName,
    required this.me,
  });

  @override
  State<CourseMessagesScreen> createState() => _CourseMessagesScreenState();
}

class _CourseMessagesScreenState extends State<CourseMessagesScreen> {
  final _textCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _scrollCtrl = ScrollController();
  bool _sending = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _textCtrl.dispose();
    _scrollCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _scrollToBottom() {
    if (_scrollCtrl.hasClients) {
      Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100), () {
        if (_scrollCtrl.hasClients) {
          _scrollCtrl.animateTo(
            _scrollCtrl.position.maxScrollExtent,
            duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
            curve: Curves.easeOut,
          );
        }
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _send() async {
    final text = _textCtrl.text.trim();
    if (text.isEmpty) return;
    setState(() => _sending = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.sendCourseMessage(
        courseId: widget.courseId,
        text: text,
      );
      _textCtrl.clear();
      FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text(e.toString().replaceAll('Exception: ', '')),
            backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
          ),
        );
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _sending = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _deleteMessage(CourseMessage m) async {
    final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('حذف الرسالة'),
        content: const Text('هل تريد حذف هذه الرسالة؟'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('إلغاء'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.error),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text('حذف'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (ok != true) return;
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.deleteCourseMessage(
        courseId: widget.courseId,
        messageId: m.id,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const Text('المحادثة'),
            Text(
              widget.courseName,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: StreamBuilder<List<CourseMessage>>(
        stream: FirebaseService.instance.courseMessagesStream(widget.courseId),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          final messages = snap.data ?? [];
          final count = messages.length;
          final remaining = FirebaseService.kMaxCourseMessages - count;

          WidgetsBinding.instance
              .addPostFrameCallback((_) => _scrollToBottom());

          return Column(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: double.infinity,
                padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
                color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.08),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      remaining > 0 ? Icons.info_outline : Icons.warning_amber,
                      size: 16,
                      color:
                          remaining > 0 ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.error,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        remaining > 0
                            ? 'عدد الرسائل: $count / ${FirebaseService.kMaxCourseMessages} (متبقٍ $remaining)'
                            : 'وصلت للحد الأقصى للرسائل ($count/${FirebaseService.kMaxCourseMessages})',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          color: remaining > 0
                              ? AppColors.primary
                              : AppColors.error,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: messages.isEmpty
                    ? Center(
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                          children: [
                            Icon(Icons.chat_bubble_outline,
                                size: 60,
                                color:
                                    AppColors.textSecondary.withOpacity(0.4)),
                            const SizedBox(height: 12),
                            Text('لا توجد رسائل بعد',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontSize: 14,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                            Text('ابدأ المحادثة مع المدرب',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontSize: 12,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                          ],
                        ),
                      )
                    : ListView.builder(
                        controller: _scrollCtrl,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        itemCount: messages.length,
                        itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                          final m = messages[i];
                          final isMine = m.senderId == widget.me.uid;
                          return _MessageBubble(
                            message: m,
                            isMine: isMine,
                            canDelete: isMine ||
                                widget.me.isAdmin ||
                                widget.me.isSuperAdmin,
                            onLongPress: () => _deleteMessage(m),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
              ),
              SafeArea(
                top: false,
                child: Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 8, 12, 12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
                    border: Border(
                      top: BorderSide(
                        color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor.withOpacity(0.4),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: TextField(
                          controller: _textCtrl,
                          enabled: remaining > 0 && !_sending,
                          maxLines: 4,
                          minLines: 1,
                          maxLength: 500,
                          textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
                          decoration: InputDecoration(
                            hintText: remaining > 0
                                ? 'اكتب رسالتك...'
                                : 'تم الوصول للحد الأقصى',
                            counterText: '',
                            contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Material(
                        color: remaining > 0 && !_sending
                            ? AppColors.primary
                            : AppColors.textSecondary.withOpacity(0.3),
                        shape: const CircleBorder(),
                        child: InkWell(
                          customBorder: const CircleBorder(),
                          onTap: remaining > 0 && !_sending ? _send : null,
                          child: Container(
                            width: 46,
                            height: 46,
                            alignment: Alignment.center,
                            child: _sending
                                ? const SizedBox(
                                    width: 18,
                                    height: 18,
                                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                                        color: Colors.white, strokeWidth: 2),
                                  )
                                : const Icon(Icons.send_rounded,
                                    color: Colors.white, size: 22),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MESSAGE BUBBLE
// ============================================================
class _MessageBubble extends StatelessWidget {
  final CourseMessage message;
  final bool isMine;
  final bool canDelete;
  final VoidCallback onLongPress;

  const _MessageBubble({
    required this.message,
    required this.isMine,
    required this.canDelete,
    required this.onLongPress,
  });

  String _timeAgo(DateTime d) {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final diff = now.difference(d);
    if (diff.inMinutes < 1) return 'الآن';
    if (diff.inMinutes < 60) return 'منذ ${diff.inMinutes} د';
    if (diff.inHours < 24) return 'منذ ${diff.inHours} س';
    if (diff.inDays < 7) return 'منذ ${diff.inDays} يوم';
    return DateFormat('dd/MM/yyyy').format(d);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment:
            isMine ? MainAxisAlignment.end : MainAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Flexible(
            child: GestureDetector(
              onLongPress: canDelete ? onLongPress : null,
              child: Container(
                constraints: BoxConstraints(
                  maxWidth: MediaQuery.of(context).size.width * 0.75,
                ),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  gradient: isMine ? AppColors.primaryGradient : null,
                  color: isMine ? null : theme.cardColor,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
                    topLeft: const Radius.circular(14),
                    topRight: const Radius.circular(14),
                    bottomLeft: Radius.circular(isMine ? 14 : 4),
                    bottomRight: Radius.circular(isMine ? 4 : 14),
                  ),
                  border: isMine
                      ? null
                      : Border.all(color: theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.6)),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Icon(
                          message.isFromCoach
                              ? (message.senderRole == 'superAdmin'
                                  ? Icons.verified_user
                                  : message.senderRole == 'admin'
                                      ? Icons.admin_panel_settings
                                      : Icons.sports)
                              : Icons.person,
                          size: 12,
                          color: isMine
                              ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9)
                              : AppColors.primary,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 4),
                        Flexible(
                          child: Text(
                            '${message.senderName} • ${message.senderRoleLabel}',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 10.5,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              color: isMine
                                  ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.9)
                                  : AppColors.primary,
                            ),
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                    Text(
                      message.text,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 13.5,
                        height: 1.5,
                        color: isMine ? Colors.white : null,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      _timeAgo(message.createdAt),
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 9.5,
                        color: isMine
                            ? Colors.white.withOpacity(0.75)
                            : AppColors.textSecondary,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// PLAYER INFO SCREEN
// ============================================================
class PlayerInfoScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final Player? existing;
  final Course? draft;
  final bool createAccount;
  const PlayerInfoScreen({
    super.key,
    this.existing,
    this.draft,
    this.createAccount = false,
  });

  @override
  State<PlayerInfoScreen> createState() => _PlayerInfoScreenState();
}

class _PlayerInfoScreenState extends State<PlayerInfoScreen> {
  final _form = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  late TextEditingController _name, _age, _weight, _height, _notes;
  late TextEditingController _email, _password, _dailyIntake;
  DateTime _start = DateTime.now();
  DateTime _end = DateTime.now().add(const Duration(days: 60));
  String _goal = goals.first;
  String _gender = 'male';
  String _activity = 'moderate';
  bool _saving = false;
  bool _isPrivate = false;

  double get _bmr {
    final a = int.tryParse(_age.text) ?? 0;
    final w = double.tryParse(_weight.text) ?? 0;
    final h = double.tryParse(_height.text) ?? 0;
    if (a <= 0 || w <= 0 || h <= 0) return 0;
    final base = 10 * w + 6.25 * h - 5 * a;
    return _gender == 'female' ? base - 161 : base + 5;
  }

  double get _activityFactor {
    switch (_activity) {
      case 'sedentary':
        return 1.2;
      case 'light':
        return 1.375;
      case 'moderate':
        return 1.55;
      case 'active':
        return 1.725;
      case 'veryActive':
        return 1.9;
      default:
        return 1.55;
    }
  }

  double get _tdee => _bmr * _activityFactor;

  double get _targetCalories {
    final t = _tdee;
    if (t <= 0) return 0;
    switch (_goal) {
      case 'تضخيم':
        return t + 300;
      case 'تنشيف':
        return t - 400;
      case 'خسارة وزن':
        return t - 500;
      case 'زيادة قوة':
        return t + 200;
      case 'لياقة':
        return t;
      default:
        return t;
    }
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final p = widget.existing;
    _name = TextEditingController(text: p?.name ?? '');
    _age = TextEditingController(text: p?.age.toString() ?? '');
    _weight = TextEditingController(text: p?.weight.toString() ?? '');
    _height = TextEditingController(text: p?.height.toString() ?? '');
    _notes = TextEditingController(text: p?.notes ?? '');
    _dailyIntake = TextEditingController(
      text: (p?.dailyCaloriesIntake ?? 0) > 0
          ? p!.dailyCaloriesIntake.toString()
          : '',
    );
    _email = TextEditingController();
    _password = TextEditingController();

    if (p != null) {
      _start = p.startDate;
      _end = p.endDate;
      _goal = p.goal;
      _gender = p.gender;
      _activity = p.activityLevel;
    }
    if (widget.draft != null) {
      _isPrivate = widget.draft!.isPrivate;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _name.dispose();
    _age.dispose();
    _weight.dispose();
    _height.dispose();
    _notes.dispose();
    _email.dispose();
    _password.dispose();
    _dailyIntake.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _pickDate(bool s) async {
    final d = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: s ? _start : _end,
      firstDate: DateTime(2020),
      lastDate: DateTime(2100),
    );
    if (d != null) {
      setState(() {
        if (s) {
          _start = d;
          if (_end.isBefore(_start)) {
            _end = _start.add(const Duration(days: 30));
          }
        } else {
          _end = d;
        }
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _proceed() async {
    if (!_form.currentState!.validate()) return;
    if (_end.isBefore(_start)) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('تاريخ النهاية قبل البداية')),
      );
      return;
    }
    setState(() => _saving = true);
    try {
      final player = Player(
        id: widget.existing?.id,
        name: _name.text.trim(),
        age: int.parse(_age.text),
        weight: double.parse(_weight.text),
        height: double.parse(_height.text),
        gender: _gender,
        startDate: _start,
        endDate: _end,
        goal: _goal,
        notes: _notes.text.trim(),
        activityLevel: _activity,
        dailyCaloriesIntake: double.tryParse(_dailyIntake.text) ?? 0,
      );

      String targetUid = '';
      if (widget.createAccount) {
        targetUid = await FirebaseService.instance.createPlayerAccount(
          email: _email.text.trim(),
          password: _password.text,
          displayName: _name.text.trim(),
        );
      }

      final me = await FirebaseService.instance.getUserProfile();
      final mySource = me?.isSuperAdmin == true
          ? 'superAdmin'
          : me?.isAdmin == true
              ? 'admin'
              : me?.isCaptain == true
                  ? 'captain'
                  : 'user';

      final course = Course(
        id: widget.draft?.id,
        player: player,
        days: widget.draft?.days ?? [],
        targetUserId: targetUid,
        source: widget.createAccount ? mySource : 'user',
        ownerName: me?.displayName ?? '',
        ownerRole: me?.role ?? '',
        ownerPhone: me?.phoneNumber ?? '',
        isPrivate: _isPrivate,
      );

      if (mounted) {
        Navigator.pushReplacement(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(
            builder: (_) => CourseBuilderScreen(
              course: course,
              saveToServer: widget.createAccount,
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _saving = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final intake = double.tryParse(_dailyIntake.text) ?? 0;
    final diff = intake > 0 ? intake - _targetCalories : 0.0;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(
            widget.createAccount ? 'بيانات العميل + إنشاء حساب' : 'بياناتي'),
      ),
      body: Form(
        key: _form,
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          children: [
            TextFormField(
              controller: _name,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'اسم اللاعب *', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.person)),
              validator: (v) => v == null || v.trim().isEmpty ? 'مطلوب' : null,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(children: [
              Expanded(
                child: TextFormField(
                  controller: _age,
                  keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                  onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'العمر'),
                  validator: (v) {
                    if (v == null || v.isEmpty) return 'مطلوب';
                    final n = int.tryParse(v);
                    if (n == null || n <= 0 || n > 100) return 'خطأ';
                    return null;
                  },
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: TextFormField(
                  controller: _weight,
                  keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                  onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'الوزن (kg)'),
                  validator: (v) {
                    if (v == null || v.isEmpty) return 'مطلوب';
                    final n = double.tryParse(v);
                    if (n == null || n <= 0) return 'خطأ';
                    return null;
                  },
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: TextFormField(
                  controller: _height,
                  keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                  onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'الطول (cm)'),
                  validator: (v) {
                    if (v == null || v.isEmpty) return 'مطلوب';
                    final n = double.tryParse(v);
                    if (n == null || n <= 0) return 'خطأ';
                    return null;
                  },
                ),
              ),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(children: [
              Expanded(
                child: DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: _gender,
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'الجنس'),
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
                  items: const [
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: 'male', child: Text('ذكر')),
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: 'female', child: Text('أنثى')),
                  ],
                  onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _gender = v!),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                flex: 2,
                child: DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                  value: _activity,
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'مستوى النشاط'),
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 13),
                  items: activityLevels.entries
                      .map((e) => DropdownMenuItem(
                            value: e.key,
                            child: Text(e.value,
                                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
                          ))
                      .toList(),
                  onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _activity = v!),
                ),
              ),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(children: [
              Expanded(
                child: InkWell(
                  onTap: () => _pickDate(true),
                  child: InputDecorator(
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'من'),
                    child: Text(dateFmt.format(_start),
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14)),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: InkWell(
                  onTap: () => _pickDate(false),
                  child: InputDecorator(
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'إلى'),
                    child: Text(dateFmt.format(_end),
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14)),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
              value: _goal,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'الهدف'),
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
              items: goals
                  .map((g) => DropdownMenuItem(value: g, child: Text(g)))
                  .toList(),
              onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _goal = v!),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextFormField(
              controller: _notes,
              maxLines: 3,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'ملاحظات'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            SurfaceCard(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      gradient: _isPrivate
                          ? const LinearGradient(
                              colors: [Color(0xFF8B5CF6), Color(0xFFEC4899)],
                            )
                          : null,
                      color: _isPrivate
                          ? null
                          : AppColors.purple.withOpacity(0.12),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    ),
                    child: Icon(
                      _isPrivate ? Icons.lock_person : Icons.person_outline,
                      color: _isPrivate ? Colors.white : AppColors.purple,
                      size: 22,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          'كورس خاص',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                        ),
                        Text(
                          'سيظهر للاعب أنه "لاعب خاص" مع شارة مميزة',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  Switch(
                    value: _isPrivate,
                    onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _isPrivate = v),
                    activeColor: AppColors.purple,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            const Divider(),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(children: [
              Icon(Icons.local_fire_department, color: AppColors.primary),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text('احتياج السعرات اليومي',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                gradient: LinearGradient(colors: [
                  AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.12),
                  AppColors.accent.withOpacity(0.08),
                ]),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                border: Border.all(color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.3)),
              ),
              child: Column(children: [
                _calRow('BMR', '${_bmr.round()} سعر'),
                _calRow('TDEE', '${_tdee.round()} سعر'),
                const Divider(height: 18),
                _calRow(
                  'المستهدف لـ "$_goal"',
                  '${_targetCalories.round()} سعر',
                  bold: true,
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                ),
              ]),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            TextFormField(
              controller: _dailyIntake,
              keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
              onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'سعراتك الفعلية اليومية',
                prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.restaurant),
              ),
            ),
            if (intake > 0 && _targetCalories > 0) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: (diff.abs() <= 150
                          ? AppColors.success
                          : (diff > 0 ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.error))
                      .withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  diff.abs() <= 150
                      ? 'سعراتك مطابقة للمستهدف'
                      : (diff > 0
                          ? 'أنت تتناول ${diff.round()} سعر حراري أكثر'
                          : 'أنت تتناول ${diff.abs().round()} سعر حراري أقل'),
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12),
                ),
              ),
            ],
            if (widget.createAccount) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              const Divider(),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text('بيانات دخول العميل',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _email,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'البريد الإلكتروني *',
                    prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.email)),
                validator: (v) {
                  if (!widget.createAccount) return null;
                  if (v == null || !v.contains('@')) {
                    return 'بريد غير صحيح';
                  }
                  return null;
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _password,
                obscureText: true,
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                    labelText: 'كلمة المرور *', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.lock)),
                validator: (v) {
                  if (!widget.createAccount) return null;
                  if (v == null || v.length < 6) {
                    return '6 أحرف على الأقل';
                  }
                  return null;
                },
              ),
            ],
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            GradientButton(
              label: 'التالي: تصميم الأيام',
              icon: Icons.arrow_forward,
              loading: _saving,
              onPressed: _proceed,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _calRow(String label, String value,
      {bool bold = false, Color? color}) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
      child: Row(children: [
        Expanded(
          child: Text(label,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: bold ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
                  color: color)),
        ),
        Text(value,
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                fontSize: bold ? 15 : 13,
                fontWeight: bold ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.w600,
                color: color)),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// COURSE BUILDER SCREEN
// ============================================================
class CourseBuilderScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final Course course;
  final bool saveToServer;
  const CourseBuilderScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.course,
    this.saveToServer = false,
  });

  @override
  State<CourseBuilderScreen> createState() => _CourseBuilderScreenState();
}

class _CourseBuilderScreenState extends State<CourseBuilderScreen> {
  late Course _course;
  bool _isEditMode = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _course = widget.course;
    _isEditMode = _course.id.isNotEmpty && _course.days.isNotEmpty;
  }

  void _addDay() {
    final c = TextEditingController();
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('إضافة يوم'),
        content: TextField(
          controller: c,
          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'اسم اليوم (مثال: صدر + ترايسبس)'),
          autofocus: true,
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('إلغاء'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () {
              if (c.text.trim().isEmpty) return;
              setState(() {
                _course.days.add(WorkoutDay(
                  number: _course.days.length + 1,
                  name: c.text.trim(),
                ));
              });
              Navigator.pop(context);
            },
            child: const Text('إضافة'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _saveCourse() async {
    if (_course.days.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('أضف يومًا واحدًا على الأقل')),
      );
      return;
    }
    try {
      if (_course.status == 'not_started') {
        _course.status = 'active';
      }
      final wasEditMode = _isEditMode;
      final targetUid = _course.targetUserId;

      if (widget.saveToServer) {
        if (wasEditMode && _course.ownerId.isNotEmpty) {
          await FirebaseService.instance.updateCourse(_course);
        } else {
          await FirebaseService.instance.saveCourseForUser(
            _course,
            targetUid: _course.targetUserId,
          );
        }

        if (targetUid.isNotEmpty) {
          if (!wasEditMode) {
            await NotificationService.instance.showCourseArrived(
              playerName: _course.player.name,
              courseName: _course.days.isNotEmpty
                  ? _course.days.first.name
                  : 'كورسك الجديد',
              source: _course.source,
              courseId: _course.id,
            );
          } else {
            await NotificationService.instance.showCourseUpdated(
              playerName: _course.player.name,
              courseId: _course.id,
            );
          }
        }
      } else {
        await StorageService.instance.upsertLocalCourse(_course);
      }

      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text(widget.saveToServer
                ? wasEditMode
                    ? 'تم تحديث الكورس'
                    : 'تم حفظ الكورس وإرساله للعميل'
                : 'تم حفظ الكورس محليًا'),
          ),
        );
        Navigator.pop(context);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(_isEditMode ? 'تعديل الكورس' : 'محرر الكورس'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.visibility),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.push(
              context,
              MaterialPageRoute(
                builder: (_) => CoursePreviewScreen(course: _course),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.save),
            onPressed: _saveCourse,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: _addDay,
        backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
        label: Text('إضافة يوم',
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          GradientCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(_course.player.name,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 18,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: Colors.white)),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  '${_course.player.age} سنة • ${_course.player.weight}kg • ${_course.player.height}cm',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 12, color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.85)),
                ),
                if (_course.coachLabel.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  Text(_course.coachLabel,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 12, color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.85))),
                ],
                if (_course.isPrivate) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  Container(
                    padding:
                        const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        const Icon(Icons.lock_person,
                            color: Colors.white, size: 12),
                        const SizedBox(width: 4),
                        Text('كورس خاص',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                color: Colors.white,
                                fontSize: 10,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          SectionTitle(title: 'الأيام (${_course.days.length})'),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          if (_course.days.isEmpty)
            SurfaceCard(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
              child: Column(children: [
                Icon(Icons.calendar_today_outlined,
                    size: 44, color: AppColors.textSecondary.withOpacity(0.5)),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Text('لم تُضف أيام بعد',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 13, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
              ]),
            )
          else
            ReorderableListView.builder(
              shrinkWrap: true,
              physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
              itemCount: _course.days.length,
              onReorder: (o, n) {
                setState(() {
                  if (n > o) n--;
                  final it = _course.days.removeAt(o);
                  _course.days.insert(n, it);
                  for (int i = 0; i < _course.days.length; i++) {
                    _course.days[i].number = i + 1;
                  }
                });
              },
              itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                final d = _course.days[i];
                return Padding(
                  key: ValueKey(d.id),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                  child: SurfaceCard(
                    padding:
                        const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                    onTap: () async {
                      await Navigator.push(
                        context,
                        MaterialPageRoute(
                          builder: (_) => DayEditorScreen(
                            day: d,
                            includeServer: true,
                            includeLocal: !widget.saveToServer,
                          ),
                        ),
                      );
                      setState(() {});
                    },
                    child: Row(children: [
                      Container(
                        width: 42,
                        height: 42,
                        alignment: Alignment.center,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        ),
                        child: Text('${d.number}',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                color: Colors.white,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                fontSize: 16)),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 12),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Text(d.name,
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                    fontSize: 14.5)),
                            const SizedBox(height: 2),
                            Text('${d.exercises.length} تمرين',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontSize: 11.5,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      IconButton(
                        icon: Icon(Icons.delete,
                            color: AppColors.error, size: 20),
                        onPressed: () =>
                            setState(() => _course.days.removeAt(i)),
                      ),
                    ]),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          GradientButton(
            label: widget.saveToServer
                ? (_isEditMode ? 'تحديث الكورس' : 'حفظ وإرسال للعميل')
                : 'حفظ الكورس محليًا',
            icon: Icons.save,
            onPressed: _saveCourse,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// DAY EDITOR SCREEN
// ============================================================
class DayEditorScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final WorkoutDay day;
  final bool includeServer;
  final bool includeLocal;
  const DayEditorScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.day,
    this.includeServer = true,
    this.includeLocal = false,
  });

  @override
  State<DayEditorScreen> createState() => _DayEditorScreenState();
}

class _DayEditorScreenState extends State<DayEditorScreen> {
  late WorkoutDay _day;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _day = widget.day;
  }

  Future<void> _pickExercise() async {
    final result = await showModalBottomSheet<Exercise>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      builder: (_) => ExercisePickerSheet(
        includeServer: widget.includeServer,
        includeLocal: widget.includeLocal,
      ),
    );
    if (result != null) {
      setState(() {
        _day.exercises.add(DayExercise(
          exerciseIdSnapshot: result.id,
          nameAr: result.nameAr,
          nameEn: result.nameEn,
          primaryMuscle: result.primaryMuscle,
          videoUrl: result.videoUrl,
          imageUrl: result.imageUrl,
          description: result.description,
        ));
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pickSuperExercise() async {
    final result = await showModalBottomSheet<List<Exercise>>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      builder: (_) => ExercisePickerSheet(
        includeServer: widget.includeServer,
        includeLocal: widget.includeLocal,
        multiSelect: true,
      ),
    );
    if (result == null || result.isEmpty) return;
    if (!mounted) return;

    final nameCtrl = TextEditingController(
      text: 'سوبر سِت (${result.length} تمارين)',
    );
    final name = await showDialog<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('اسم التمرين المركّب'),
        content: TextField(
          controller: nameCtrl,
          autofocus: true,
          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
          decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'الاسم'),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('إلغاء'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, nameCtrl.text.trim()),
            child: const Text('حفظ'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (name == null || name.isEmpty) return;

    setState(() {
      _day.exercises.add(DayExercise(
        exerciseIdSnapshot: 'super_${uuid.v4()}',
        nameAr: name,
        nameEn: 'Super Set',
        primaryMuscle: 'مركّب',
        isSuper: true,
        restSeconds: 90,
        subExercises: result
            .map((e) => SubExercise(
                  nameAr: e.nameAr,
                  nameEn: e.nameEn,
                  primaryMuscle: e.primaryMuscle,
                  videoUrl: e.videoUrl,
                  imageUrl: e.imageUrl,
                  description: e.description,
                ))
            .toList(),
        notes: '',
      ));
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text('اليوم ${_day.number}: ${_day.name}')),
      floatingActionButton: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
        children: [
          FloatingActionButton.extended(
            heroTag: 'super',
            onPressed: _pickSuperExercise,
            backgroundColor: AppColors.purple,
            foregroundColor: Colors.white,
            icon: const Icon(Icons.merge_type),
            label: Text('تمرين سوبر',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          FloatingActionButton.extended(
            heroTag: 'normal',
            onPressed: _pickExercise,
            backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
            foregroundColor: Colors.white,
            icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
            label: Text('إضافة تمرين',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: _day.exercises.isEmpty
          ? Center(
              child: Text('لا توجد تمارين',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary)))
          : ReorderableListView.builder(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              itemCount: _day.exercises.length,
              onReorder: (o, n) {
                setState(() {
                  if (n > o) n--;
                  final it = _day.exercises.removeAt(o);
                  _day.exercises.insert(n, it);
                });
              },
              itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                final e = _day.exercises[i];
                return Padding(
                  key: ValueKey(e.id),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                  child: SurfaceCard(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Row(children: [
                          if (e.isSuper)
                            Padding(
