                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 6),
                              child: Container(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                    horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: AppColors.purple.withOpacity(0.15),
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                                ),
                                child: Row(
                                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                  children: [
                                    Icon(Icons.merge_type,
                                        size: 11, color: AppColors.purple),
                                    const SizedBox(width: 3),
                                    Text('سوبر',
                                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                            fontSize: 10,
                                            color: AppColors.purple,
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(e.nameAr,
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                    fontSize: 14.5)),
                          ),
                          IconButton(
                            icon: Icon(Icons.delete,
                                color: AppColors.error, size: 20),
                            onPressed: () =>
                                setState(() => _day.exercises.removeAt(i)),
                          ),
                        ]),
                        if (!e.isSuper) ...[
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          ...e.sets.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                            final idx = entry.key;
                            final s = entry.value;
                            return Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
                              child: Text(
                                'Set ${idx + 1}: ${s.reps}عدة',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontSize: 12.5,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                              ),
                            );
                          }),
                        ] else ...[
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          ...e.subExercises.map((s) {
                            final reps =
                                s.sets.isNotEmpty ? s.sets.first.reps : 0;
                            return Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
                              child: Text(
                                '• ${s.nameAr}  (${s.sets.length} × $reps عدة)',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontSize: 12.5,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                              ),
                            );
                          }),
                        ],
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        Wrap(spacing: 6, children: [
                          OutlinedButton.icon(
                            style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                              minimumSize: const Size(0, 36),
                              padding:
                                  const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                            ),
                            onPressed: () => showModalBottomSheet(
                              context: context,
                              isScrollControlled: true,
                              builder: (_) => ExerciseSetsEditor(exercise: e),
                            ).then((_) => setState(() {})),
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.settings, size: 16),
                            label: const Text('تعديل'),
                          ),
                          if (!e.isSuper)
                            OutlinedButton.icon(
                              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                                minimumSize: const Size(0, 36),
                                padding:
                                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                              ),
                              onPressed: () => setState(
                                  () => e.sets.add(ExerciseSet(reps: 10))),
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.add, size: 16),
                              label: const Text('مجموعة'),
                            ),
                        ]),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// EXERCISE PICKER SHEET
// ============================================================
class ExercisePickerSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool includeServer;
  final bool includeLocal;
  final bool multiSelect;
  const ExercisePickerSheet({
    super.key,
    this.includeServer = true,
    this.includeLocal = false,
    this.multiSelect = false,
  });

  @override
  State<ExercisePickerSheet> createState() => _ExercisePickerSheetState();
}

class _ExercisePickerSheetState extends State<ExercisePickerSheet> {
  String _q = '';
  final Set<String> _selectedIds = {};
  final Map<String, Exercise> _selectedMap = {};

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final title = widget.multiSelect
        ? 'اختر تمارين للدمج (${_selectedIds.length} محدد)'
        : (widget.includeLocal
            ? 'اختر تمرينًا (سيرفر + محلي)'
            : 'اختر تمرينًا');

    return DraggableScrollableSheet(
      initialChildSize: 0.9,
      maxChildSize: 0.95,
      expand: false,
      builder: (context, scroll) => Column(children: [
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Container(
            width: 40,
            height: 4,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2))),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
          child: Text(title,
              style:
                  GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        ),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
          child: TextField(
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
                hintText: 'ابحث...', prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search)),
            onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _q = v),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Expanded(
          child: StreamBuilder<List<Exercise>>(
            stream: widget.includeServer
                ? FirebaseService.instance.exercisesStream()
                : Stream.value(const <Exercise>[]),
            builder: (context, serverSnap) {
              final serverList = serverSnap.data ?? [];
              final localList = widget.includeLocal
                  ? StorageService.instance.loadLocalExercises()
                  : <Exercise>[];
              final serverIds = serverList.map((e) => e.id).toSet();
              final all = <Exercise>[...serverList];
              for (final e in localList) {
                if (!serverIds.contains(e.id)) all.add(e);
              }

              final filtered = all.where((e) {
                if (_q.isNotEmpty &&
                    !e.nameAr.toLowerCase().contains(_q.toLowerCase()) &&
                    !e.nameEn.toLowerCase().contains(_q.toLowerCase())) {
                  return false;
                }
                return true;
              }).toList();

              if (filtered.isEmpty) {
                return Center(
                  child: Text('لا توجد نتائج',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                );
              }

              return ListView.builder(
                controller: scroll,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 12, 12, 100),
                itemCount: filtered.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                  final e = filtered[i];
                  final isServer = serverIds.contains(e.id);
                  final isSelected = _selectedIds.contains(e.id);

                  return Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                    child: SurfaceCard(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                      onTap: widget.multiSelect
                          ? () {
                              setState(() {
                                if (isSelected) {
                                  _selectedIds.remove(e.id);
                                  _selectedMap.remove(e.id);
                                } else {
                                  _selectedIds.add(e.id);
                                  _selectedMap[e.id] = e;
                                }
                              });
                            }
                          : null,
                      child: Row(children: [
                        if (widget.multiSelect)
                          Container(
                            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
                            width: 22,
                            height: 22,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: isSelected
                                  ? AppColors.primary
                                  : Colors.transparent,
                              border: Border.all(
                                color: isSelected
                                    ? AppColors.primary
                                    : AppColors.textSecondary,
                                width: 1.6,
                              ),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                            ),
                            child: isSelected
                                ? const Icon(Icons.check,
                                    size: 15, color: Colors.white)
                                : null,
                          ),
                        Container(
                          width: 40,
                          height: 40,
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color:
                                (isServer ? AppColors.success : AppColors.info)
                                    .withOpacity(0.12),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                          ),
                          child: Icon(
                            isServer ? Icons.cloud : Icons.phone_android,
                            color:
                                isServer ? AppColors.success : AppColors.info,
                            size: 20,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 12),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(e.nameAr,
                                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      fontSize: 13.5)),
                              Text(
                                '${e.primaryMuscle} • ${e.equipment}',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontSize: 11.5,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (!widget.multiSelect)
                          FilledButton(
                            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                              minimumSize: const Size(0, 38),
                              padding:
                                  const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14),
                            ),
                            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, e),
                            child: const Text('إضافة'),
                          ),
                      ]),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
        if (widget.multiSelect)
          SafeArea(
            top: false,
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Theme.of(context).cardColor,
                border: Border(
                  top: BorderSide(
                      color: Theme.of(context).dividerColor.withOpacity(0.4)),
                ),
              ),
              child: GradientButton(
                label: _selectedIds.isEmpty
                    ? 'اختر تمرينًا واحدًا على الأقل'
                    : 'دمج ${_selectedIds.length} تمارين',
                icon: Icons.merge_type,
                onPressed: _selectedIds.isEmpty
                    ? null
                    : () =>
                        Navigator.pop(context, _selectedMap.values.toList()),
              ),
            ),
          ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// EXERCISE SETS EDITOR
// ============================================================
class ExerciseSetsEditor extends StatefulWidget {
  final DayExercise exercise;
  const ExerciseSetsEditor({super.key, required this.exercise});

  @override
  State<ExerciseSetsEditor> createState() => _ExerciseSetsEditorState();
}

class _ExerciseSetsEditorState extends State<ExerciseSetsEditor> {
  late DayExercise _e;
  late TextEditingController _notes, _rest;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _e = widget.exercise;
    _notes = TextEditingController(text: _e.notes);
    _rest = TextEditingController(text: _e.restSeconds.toString());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _notes.dispose();
    _rest.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return DraggableScrollableSheet(
      initialChildSize: 0.9,
      maxChildSize: 0.95,
      expand: false,
      builder: (context, scroll) => Column(children: [
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Container(
            width: 40,
            height: 4,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2))),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
          child: Text(_e.nameAr,
              style:
                  GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
        ),
        Expanded(
          child: ListView(
            controller: scroll,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            children: [
              TextField(
                controller: _rest,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'الراحة (ثانية)'),
                onChanged: (v) => _e.restSeconds = int.tryParse(v) ?? 60,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              if (_e.isSuper) ...[
                const SectionTitle(title: 'التمارين المدمجة'),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                ..._e.subExercises.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                  final idx = entry.key;
                  final s = entry.value;
                  return Container(
                    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.purple.withOpacity(0.06),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      border:
                          Border.all(color: AppColors.purple.withOpacity(0.3)),
                    ),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Row(children: [
                          Container(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              color: AppColors.purple.withOpacity(0.15),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                            ),
                            child: Text('${idx + 1}',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontSize: 11,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                    color: AppColors.purple)),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(s.nameAr,
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)),
                          ),
                        ]),
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        ...s.sets.asMap().entries.map((e2) {
                          final i2 = e2.key;
                          final st = e2.value;
                          return Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
                            child: Row(children: [
                              Text('Set ${i2 + 1}',
                                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                      fontSize: 12,
                                      color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                              const SizedBox(width: 12),
                              Expanded(
                                child: TextFormField(
                                  initialValue: st.reps.toString(),
                                  keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 13),
                                  decoration: const InputDecoration(
                                    labelText: 'عدات',
                                    isDense: true,
                                    contentPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                        horizontal: 8, vertical: 8),
                                  ),
                                  onChanged: (v) =>
                                      st.reps = int.tryParse(v) ?? st.reps,
                                ),
                              ),
                              IconButton(
                                icon: Icon(Icons.remove_circle,
                                    color: AppColors.error, size: 18),
                                onPressed: () =>
                                    setState(() => s.sets.removeAt(i2)),
                              ),
                            ]),
                          );
                        }),
                        OutlinedButton.icon(
                          onPressed: () =>
                              setState(() => s.sets.add(ExerciseSet(reps: 10))),
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.add, size: 14),
                          label: const Text('مجموعة'),
                          style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                            minimumSize: const Size(0, 32),
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  );
                }),
              ] else ...[
                const SectionTitle(title: 'المجموعات'),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                ..._e.sets.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                  final idx = entry.key;
                  final s = entry.value;
                  return Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                    child: SurfaceCard(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Row(children: [
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.12),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                              ),
                              child: Text('Set ${idx + 1}',
                                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      fontSize: 12,
                                      color: AppColors.primary)),
                            ),
                            const Spacer(),
                            IconButton(
                              icon: Icon(Icons.delete,
                                  color: AppColors.error, size: 18),
                              onPressed: () =>
                                  setState(() => _e.sets.removeAt(idx)),
                            ),
                          ]),
                          const SizedBox(height: 4),
                          Row(children: [
                            Expanded(
                              child: TextFormField(
                                initialValue: s.reps.toString(),
                                keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
                                decoration:
                                    const InputDecoration(labelText: 'العدات'),
                                onChanged: (v) =>
                                    s.reps = int.tryParse(v) ?? s.reps,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ]),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                }),
                OutlinedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: () =>
                      setState(() => _e.sets.add(ExerciseSet(reps: 10))),
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                  label: const Text('إضافة مجموعة'),
                ),
              ],
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              TextField(
                controller: _notes,
                maxLines: 3,
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'ملاحظات'),
                onChanged: (v) => _e.notes = v,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              GradientButton(
                label: 'تم',
                icon: Icons.check,
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// COURSE PREVIEW SCREEN
// ============================================================
class CoursePreviewScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final Course course;
  const CoursePreviewScreen({super.key, required this.course});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('معاينة الكورس'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf),
            onPressed: () => PdfService.instance.preview(course),
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.share),
            onPressed: () => PdfService.instance.share(course),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          GradientCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text('بيانات اللاعب',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        fontSize: 16,
                        color: Colors.white)),
                const Divider(color: Colors.white30, height: 20),
                _row('الاسم', course.player.name),
                _row('العمر', '${course.player.age}'),
                _row('الوزن', '${course.player.weight} kg'),
                _row('الطول', '${course.player.height} cm'),
                _row('من', dateFmt.format(course.player.startDate)),
                _row('إلى', dateFmt.format(course.player.endDate)),
                _row('الهدف', course.player.goal),
                if (course.coachLabel.isNotEmpty)
                  _row('المدرب', course.coachLabel),
                if (course.ownerPhone.isNotEmpty)
                  _row('الهاتف', course.ownerPhone),
                if (course.isPrivate) _row('نوع الكورس', 'خاص 🔒'),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          ...course.days.map((d) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                child: SurfaceCard(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text('اليوم ${d.number}: ${d.name}',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 15,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              color: AppColors.primary)),
                      const Divider(height: 20),
                      ...d.exercises.asMap().entries.map((en) {
                        final e = en.value;
                        return Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Row(children: [
                                if (e.isSuper)
                                  Padding(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 6),
                                    child: Icon(Icons.merge_type,
                                        size: 14, color: AppColors.purple),
                                  ),
                                Expanded(
                                  child: Text('${en.key + 1}. ${e.nameAr}',
                                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                          fontSize: 13.5)),
                                ),
                              ]),
                              if (!e.isSuper)
                                ...e.sets.asMap().entries.map((s) => Text(
                                    'Set ${s.key + 1}: ${s.value.reps}عدة',
                                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                        fontSize: 11.5,
                                        color: AppColors.textSecondary)))
                              else
                                ...e.subExercises.map((s) => Padding(
                                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 16),
                                      child: Text(
                                        '• ${s.nameAr} (${s.sets.length} × ${s.sets.isNotEmpty ? s.sets.first.reps : 0} عدة)',
                                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                            fontSize: 11.5,
                                            color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                                      ),
                                    )),
                            ],
                          ),
                        );
                      }),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              )),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _row(String k, String v) => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 5),
        child: Row(children: [
          SizedBox(
            width: 100,
            child: Text(k,
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.8), fontSize: 12.5)),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(v,
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 13.5,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
          ),
        ]),
      );
}

// ============================================================
// COURSE PLAYER SCREEN
// ============================================================
class CoursePlayerScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final Course course;
  final bool isAdmin;
  const CoursePlayerScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.course,
    this.isAdmin = false,
  });

  @override
  State<CoursePlayerScreen> createState() => _CoursePlayerScreenState();
}

class _CoursePlayerScreenState extends State<CoursePlayerScreen> {
  late Course _c;
  bool _progressLoaded = false;
  StreamSubscription<Course?>? _liveSub;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _c = widget.course;
    _loadSavedProgress();
    _listenLive();
  }

  void _listenLive() {
    if (_c.source == 'user') return;
    _liveSub =
        FirebaseService.instance.courseByIdStream(_c.id).listen((remote) {
      if (remote == null) return;
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _c = remote;
      });
      _loadSavedProgress();
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _liveSub?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadSavedProgress() async {
    await StorageService.instance.applyProgressToCourse(_c);
    if (mounted) setState(() => _progressLoaded = true);
  }

  Future<void> _toggleDone(DayExercise e) async {
    setState(() => e.done = !e.done);
    await StorageService.instance.saveExerciseDone(
      courseId: _c.id,
      exerciseId: e.id,
      done: e.done,
    );
    if (_c.source == 'user') {
      await StorageService.instance.upsertLocalCourse(_c);
    } else {
      await FirebaseService.instance.syncCourseDaysToServer(_c);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text('بدء الكورس: ${_c.player.name}'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.picture_as_pdf),
            onPressed: () => PdfService.instance.preview(_c),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: !_progressLoaded
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 32),
              children: [
                _buildProgressCard(),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                ..._c.days.map(_buildDayCard),
              ],
            ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildProgressCard() {
    final progressPercent = (_c.progress * 100).round();
    return GradientCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      radius: 20,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
                child: const Icon(Icons.fitness_center,
                    color: Colors.white, size: 22),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(_c.player.name,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontSize: 18,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                    const SizedBox(height: 2),
                    Text('الهدف: ${_c.player.goal}',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.85),
                            fontSize: 12)),
                    if (_c.coachLabel.isNotEmpty) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text(_c.coachLabel,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.85),
                              fontSize: 11.5)),
                    ],
                    if (_c.isPrivate) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                            horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                        ),
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            const Icon(Icons.lock_person,
                                color: Colors.white, size: 12),
                            const SizedBox(width: 4),
                            Text('لاعب خاص',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    fontSize: 10,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Text('$progressPercent%',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      fontSize: 26,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: _c.progress,
              backgroundColor: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
              color: Colors.white,
              minHeight: 8,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDayCard(WorkoutDay d) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final doneCount = d.exercises.where((e) => e.done).length;
    final total = d.exercises.length;
    final dayProgress = total == 0 ? 0.0 : doneCount / total;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: theme.cardColor,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.4)),
      ),
      child: Theme(
        data: theme.copyWith(dividerColor: Colors.transparent),
        child: ExpansionTile(
          initiallyExpanded: d.number == 1,
          tilePadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 6),
          childrenPadding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 0, 12, 12),
          leading: Container(
            width: 42,
            height: 42,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.25),
                  blurRadius: 8,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 3),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            child: Text('${d.number}',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 16)),
          ),
          title: Text('اليوم ${d.number}: ${d.name}',
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14.5)),
          subtitle: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
            child: Row(children: [
              Text('$doneCount/$total',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      color: AppColors.primary,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      fontSize: 12)),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                  child: LinearProgressIndicator(
                    value: dayProgress,
                    minHeight: 4,
                    backgroundColor: theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.3),
                    color: AppColors.primary,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ]),
          ),
          children: [
            if (d.exercises.isEmpty)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                child: Text('لا توجد تمارين',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13)),
              )
            else
              ...d.exercises.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                return _buildExerciseRow(d, entry.value, entry.key + 1);
              }),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildExerciseRow(WorkoutDay d, DayExercise e, int index) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final hasVideo = !e.isSuper && e.videoUrl != null && e.videoUrl!.isNotEmpty;

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: e.done
            ? AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.06)
            : theme.scaffoldBackgroundColor.withOpacity(0.4),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(
          color: e.done
              ? AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.4)
              : theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.3),
          width: e.done ? 1.5 : 1,
        ),
      ),
      child: Material(
        color: Colors.transparent,
        child: InkWell(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          onTap: () => _openExerciseDetails(e),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(children: [
                  Container(
                    width: 32,
                    height: 32,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: e.done
                          ? AppColors.primary
                          : theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.2),
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    child: e.done
                        ? const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.white, size: 18)
                        : (e.isSuper
                            ? Icon(Icons.merge_type,
                                size: 16,
                                color: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.color)
                            : Text('$index',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                    fontSize: 13,
                                    color: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.color))),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(e.nameAr,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            fontSize: 14,
                            decoration:
                                e.done ? TextDecoration.lineThrough : null,
                            color: e.done ? AppColors.textSecondary : null),
                        maxLines: 2,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                  ),
                  if (hasVideo)
                    IconButton(
                      iconSize: 28,
                      icon:
                          Icon(Icons.play_circle_fill, color: AppColors.error),
                      onPressed: () => _openVideo(e),
                    ),
                  IconButton(
                    iconSize: 28,
                    icon: Icon(
                      e.done
                          ? Icons.check_circle
                          : Icons.radio_button_unchecked,
                      color:
                          e.done ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.textSecondary,
                    ),
                    onPressed: () => _toggleDone(e),
                  ),
                ]),
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6, right: 44, left: 2),
                  child: Wrap(
                    spacing: 12,
                    runSpacing: 4,
                    children: [
                      _miniInfo(Icons.repeat, '${e.totalSets} مجموعات'),
                      if (!e.isSuper)
                        _miniInfo(Icons.fitness_center, e.summaryLine)
                      else
                        _miniInfo(Icons.merge_type, 'تمرين مركّب',
                            color: AppColors.purple),
                      if (e.restSeconds > 0)
                        _miniInfo(Icons.timer_outlined, '${e.restSeconds}s'),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _miniInfo(IconData icon, String text, {Color? color}) {
    return Row(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        Icon(icon, size: 12, color: color ?? AppColors.textSecondary),
        const SizedBox(width: 3),
        Text(text,
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                fontSize: 11,
                color: color ?? AppColors.textSecondary,
                fontWeight:
                    color != null ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal)),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _openVideo(DayExercise e) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (_) => _VideoSheet(exercise: e),
    );
  }

  void _openExerciseDetails(DayExercise e) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (_) => _ExerciseDetailsSheet(
        exercise: e,
        onToggleDone: () => _toggleDone(e),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// VIDEO SHEET
// ============================================================
class _VideoSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  final DayExercise exercise;
  const _VideoSheet({required this.exercise});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return DraggableScrollableSheet(
      initialChildSize: 0.7,
      maxChildSize: 0.95,
      minChildSize: 0.4,
      expand: false,
      builder: (context, scroll) => Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: theme.cardColor,
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
        ),
        child: Column(children: [
          Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12, bottom: 8),
            width: 40,
            height: 4,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 8),
            child: Row(children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.error.withOpacity(0.15),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Icon(Icons.play_circle_fill,
                    color: AppColors.error, size: 22),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(exercise.nameAr,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
              ),
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.close),
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
              ),
            ]),
          ),
          const Divider(height: 1),
          Expanded(
            child: ListView(
              controller: scroll,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              children: [
                if (exercise.videoUrl != null && exercise.videoUrl!.isNotEmpty)
                  ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                    child: VideoPlayerWidget(url: exercise.videoUrl!),
                  )
                else
                  Container(
                    height: 200,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.2),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                    ),
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    child: Text('لا يوجد فيديو',
                        style:
                            GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                  ),
                if ((exercise.description ?? '').isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text(exercise.description!,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 13.5,
                          height: 1.6,
                          color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// EXERCISE DETAILS SHEET
// ============================================================
class _ExerciseDetailsSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  final DayExercise exercise;
  final VoidCallback onToggleDone;
  const _ExerciseDetailsSheet({
    required this.exercise,
    required this.onToggleDone,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return DraggableScrollableSheet(
      initialChildSize: 0.85,
      maxChildSize: 0.95,
      minChildSize: 0.4,
      expand: false,
      builder: (context, scroll) => Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: theme.cardColor,
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
        ),
        child: Column(children: [
          Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12, bottom: 8),
            width: 40,
            height: 4,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20, vertical: 8),
            child: Row(children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color:
                      (exercise.isSuper ? AppColors.purple : AppColors.primary)
                          .withOpacity(0.15),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Icon(
                  exercise.isSuper ? Icons.merge_type : Icons.fitness_center,
                  color:
                      exercise.isSuper ? AppColors.purple : AppColors.primary,
                  size: 22,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(exercise.nameAr,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                    if (exercise.nameEn.isNotEmpty)
                      Text(exercise.nameEn,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              IconButton(
                icon: const Icon(Icons.close),
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
              ),
            ]),
          ),
          const Divider(height: 1),
          Expanded(
            child: ListView(
              controller: scroll,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              children: [
                Wrap(
                  spacing: 8,
                  runSpacing: 6,
                  children: [
                    if (exercise.isSuper)
                      _infoChip(
                          Icons.merge_type, 'تمرين مركّب', AppColors.purple),
                    if (exercise.primaryMuscle.isNotEmpty)
                      _infoChip(Icons.adjust, exercise.primaryMuscle,
                          AppColors.primary),
                    if (exercise.restSeconds > 0)
                      _infoChip(Icons.timer_outlined,
                          'راحة ${exercise.restSeconds}s', AppColors.info),
                    _infoChip(exercise.isSuper ? Icons.layers : Icons.repeat,
                        '${exercise.totalSets} مجموعة', AppColors.success),
                  ],
                ),
                if (!exercise.isSuper &&
                    exercise.videoUrl != null &&
                    exercise.videoUrl!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text('الفيديو',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                    child: VideoPlayerWidget(url: exercise.videoUrl!),
                  ),
                ],
                if (!exercise.isSuper &&
                    exercise.imageUrl != null &&
                    exercise.imageUrl!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text('الصورة',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                    child: exercise.imageUrl!.startsWith('http')
                        ? CachedNetworkImage(
                            imageUrl: exercise.imageUrl!, fit: BoxFit.cover)
                        : Image.file(File(exercise.imageUrl!),
                            fit: BoxFit.cover),
                  ),
                ],
                if ((exercise.description ?? '').isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text('الوصف',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: theme.scaffoldBackgroundColor.withOpacity(0.5),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      border: Border.all(
                          color: theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.4)),
                    ),
                    child: Text(exercise.description!,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 13, height: 1.7)),
                  ),
                ],
                if (!exercise.isSuper) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text('المجموعات',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  ...exercise.sets.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                    return _setRow(entry.key + 1, entry.value, theme);
                  }),
                ],
                if (exercise.isSuper) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text('التمارين المدمجة (${exercise.subExercises.length})',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  ...exercise.subExercises.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                    final i = entry.key;
                    final s = entry.value;
                    return Container(
                      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: theme.scaffoldBackgroundColor.withOpacity(0.5),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        border: Border.all(
                            color: AppColors.purple.withOpacity(0.4)),
                      ),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Row(children: [
                            Container(
                              width: 26,
                              height: 26,
                              alignment: Alignment.center,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: AppColors.purple.withOpacity(0.15),
                                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              ),
                              child: Text('${i + 1}',
                                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                      fontSize: 12,
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      color: AppColors.purple)),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: 8),
                            Expanded(
                              child: Text(s.nameAr,
                                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                      fontSize: 13.5)),
                            ),
                            if (s.videoUrl != null && s.videoUrl!.isNotEmpty)
                              Icon(Icons.play_circle_fill,
                                  color: AppColors.error, size: 22),
                          ]),
                          if (s.primaryMuscle.isNotEmpty)
                            Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4, right: 34),
                              child: Text(s.primaryMuscle,
                                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                      fontSize: 11,
                                      color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                            ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 6),
                          ...s.sets.asMap().entries.map((e2) {
                            return Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 34, top: 3),
                              child: Row(children: [
                                Text('Set ${e2.key + 1}: ',
                                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                        fontSize: 12,
                                        color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                                Text('${e2.value.reps} عدة',
                                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                        fontSize: 12.5,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                              ]),
                            );
                          }),
                        ],
                      ),
                    );
                  }),
                ],
                if (exercise.notes.isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text('ملاحظات',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: AppColors.info.withOpacity(0.08),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      border:
                          Border.all(color: AppColors.info.withOpacity(0.3)),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Icon(Icons.notes, color: AppColors.info, size: 18),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(exercise.notes,
                              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                  fontSize: 12.5, height: 1.7)),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                FilledButton.icon(
                  onPressed: () {
                    onToggleDone();
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                  },
                  style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                    minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(50),
                    backgroundColor: exercise.done
                        ? AppColors.textSecondary
                        : AppColors.primary,
                  ),
                  icon: Icon(exercise.done ? Icons.undo : Icons.check_circle),
                  label: Text(exercise.done ? 'إلغاء الإكمال' : 'تحديد كمكتمل'),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _setRow(int idx, ExerciseSet s, ThemeData theme) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: theme.scaffoldBackgroundColor.withOpacity(0.4),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: theme.dividerColor.withOpacity(0.3)),
      ),
      child: Row(children: [
        Container(
          width: 32,
          height: 32,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.15),
            shape: BoxShape.circle,
          ),
          alignment: Alignment.center,
          child: Text('$idx',
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  fontSize: 13)),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: Row(children: [
            _setInfo('العدات', '${s.reps}'),
          ]),
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _infoChip(IconData icon, String text, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.12),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 14, color: color),
          const SizedBox(width: 6),
          Text(text,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 12, color: color, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _setInfo(String label, String value) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(label,
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                fontSize: 10, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
        const SizedBox(height: 2),
        Text(value,
            style:
                GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
      ],
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// COACH PROFILE SCREEN
// ============================================================
class CoachProfileScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final String coachUid;
  final Course? course;
  final UserProfile? me;
  const CoachProfileScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.coachUid,
    this.course,
    this.me,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('بروفايل المدرب')),
      body: StreamBuilder<UserProfile?>(
        stream: FirebaseService.instance.userProfileByIdStream(coachUid),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          if (snap.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          }
          final coach = snap.data;
          if (coach == null) {
            return Center(
              child: Text(
                'لم يتم العثور على بيانات المدرب',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              ),
            );
          }
          return _buildBody(context, coach);
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildBody(BuildContext context, UserProfile coach) {
    final isSuper = coach.isSuperAdmin;
    final isAdmin = coach.isAdmin;
    final roleLabel = isSuper
        ? 'إشراف عام'
        : isAdmin
            ? 'مدير'
            : coach.isCaptain
                ? 'كابتن'
                : 'لاعب';

    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      children: [
        GradientCard(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(22),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 92,
                height: 92,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                  border: Border.all(
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.55),
                    width: 3,
                  ),
                ),
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                child: Text(
                  coach.displayName.isNotEmpty ? coach.displayName[0] : '?',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 40,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  Flexible(
                    child: Text(
                      coach.displayName,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontSize: 21,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (coach.isVerified) ...[
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    const Icon(Icons.verified, color: Colors.white, size: 24),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Wrap(
                spacing: 8,
                runSpacing: 6,
                alignment: WrapAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  _badge(
                    icon: isSuper
                        ? Icons.verified_user
                        : isAdmin
                            ? Icons.admin_panel_settings
                            : Icons.sports,
                    label: roleLabel,
                  ),
                  _badge(
                    icon: coach.isVerified
                        ? Icons.verified
                        : Icons.gpp_maybe_outlined,
                    label: coach.isVerified ? 'موثّق' : 'غير موثّق',
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        const SectionTitle(title: 'معلومات التواصل'),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        SurfaceCard(
          padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          child: Column(children: [
            _infoTile(Icons.email_outlined, 'البريد الإلكتروني', coach.email),
            if ((coach.phoneNumber ?? '').isNotEmpty) ...[
              const Divider(height: 1),
              _infoTile(Icons.phone_outlined, 'رقم الهاتف', coach.phoneNumber!),
            ],
            if (coach.createdAt != null) ...[
              const Divider(height: 1),
              _infoTile(Icons.calendar_today_outlined, 'عضو منذ',
                  dateFmt.format(coach.createdAt!)),
            ],
          ]),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        const SectionTitle(title: 'حالة الحساب'),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        SurfaceCard(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(11),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color:
                      (coach.isVerified ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error)
                          .withOpacity(0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
                child: Icon(
                  coach.isVerified ? Icons.verified_user : Icons.gpp_maybe,
                  color: coach.isVerified ? AppColors.success : AppColors.error,
                  size: 24,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 14),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      coach.isVerified ? 'حساب موثّق' : 'حساب غير موثّق',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 14.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 3),
                    Text(
                      coach.isVerified
                          ? 'تم التحقق من هذا الحساب من قِبل الإدارة'
                          : 'لم يتم التحقق من هذا الحساب بعد',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        if (course != null && me != null) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          const SectionTitle(title: 'التواصل'),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          SurfaceCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
            onTap: () => Navigator.push(
              context,
              MaterialPageRoute(
                builder: (_) => CourseMessagesScreen(
                  courseId: course!.id,
                  courseName: course!.player.name,
                  me: me!,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  ),
                  child: const Icon(Icons.chat_bubble_outline,
                      color: Colors.white, size: 20),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    'إرسال رسالة للمدرب',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  ),
                ),
                Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios,
                    size: 14, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _badge({required IconData icon, required String label}) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, color: Colors.white, size: 14),
          const SizedBox(width: 5),
          Text(
            label,
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
              color: Colors.white,
              fontSize: 11.5,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _infoTile(IconData icon, String label, String value) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icon, color: AppColors.primary, size: 22),
      title: Text(label,
          style:
              GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
      subtitle: Text(value,
          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// PLAYER DETAILS SCREEN
// ============================================================
class PlayerDetailsScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final UserProfile player;
  final Course? course;
  final String? courseId;
  final UserProfile? me;
  const PlayerDetailsScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.player,
    this.course,
    this.courseId,
    this.me,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (courseId != null && courseId!.isNotEmpty) {
      return StreamBuilder<Course?>(
        stream: FirebaseService.instance.courseByIdStream(courseId!),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          final liveCourse = snap.data ?? course;
          return _buildScaffold(context, liveCourse);
        },
      );
    }
    return _buildScaffold(context, course);
  }

  Widget _buildScaffold(BuildContext context, Course? liveCourse) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('بروفايل اللاعب')),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          _header(context, liveCourse),
          if (liveCourse != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            const SectionTitle(title: 'مؤشرات الالتزام والجهد'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _commitmentCard(context, liveCourse),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            const SectionTitle(title: 'تمرين اليوم'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _todayCard(context, liveCourse),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          const SectionTitle(title: 'البيانات الشخصية'),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          _personalCard(liveCourse),
          if (liveCourse != null && liveCourse.player.targetCalories > 0) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            const SectionTitle(title: 'الاحتياج الغذائي'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _nutritionCard(liveCourse),
          ],
          if (liveCourse != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            const SectionTitle(title: 'تفصيل أيام الكورس'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _daysBreakdown(context, liveCourse),
          ],
          if (me != null && liveCourse != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            const SectionTitle(title: 'التواصل'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            SurfaceCard(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
              onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                context,
                MaterialPageRoute(
                  builder: (_) => CourseMessagesScreen(
                    courseId: liveCourse.id,
                    courseName: liveCourse.player.name,
                    me: me!,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    ),
                    child: const Icon(Icons.chat_bubble_outline,
                        color: Colors.white, size: 20),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      'إرسال رسالة للاعب',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios,
                      size: 14, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _header(BuildContext context, Course? liveCourse) {
    final canManage = player.canManageCourses;
    final roleLabel = player.isSuperAdmin
        ? 'إشراف عام'
        : player.isAdmin
            ? 'مدير'
            : player.isCaptain
                ? 'كابتن'
                : 'لاعب';

    return GradientCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 82,
            height: 82,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
              color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
              border: Border.all(
                color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.5),
                width: 3,
              ),
            ),
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            child: Text(
              player.displayName.isNotEmpty ? player.displayName[0] : '?',
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                color: Colors.white,
                fontSize: 34,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Flexible(
                child: Text(
                  player.displayName,
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 19,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              if (player.isVerified) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                const Icon(Icons.verified, color: Colors.white, size: 22),
              ],
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Wrap(
            spacing: 8,
            runSpacing: 6,
            alignment: WrapAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Container(
                padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  roleLabel,
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 11.5,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              if (canManage)
                Container(
                  padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    'صلاحيات إدارة',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      fontSize: 11.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              Container(
                padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  player.subscriptionLabel,
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 11.5,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              if (liveCourse != null && liveCourse.isPrivate)
                Container(
                  padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    gradient: const LinearGradient(
                      colors: [Color(0xFF8B5CF6), Color(0xFFEC4899)],
                    ),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      const Icon(Icons.lock_person,
                          color: Colors.white, size: 12),
                      const SizedBox(width: 4),
                      Text(
                        'لاعب خاص',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          color: Colors.white,
