                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _commitmentCard(BuildContext context, Course c) {
    final score = c.adherenceScore;
    final scoreColor = c.adherenceColor;
    final done = c.doneExercises;
    final total = c.totalExercises;
    final progress = (c.progress * 100).round();

    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Center(
            child: SizedBox(
              width: 130,
              height: 130,
              child: Stack(
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                children: [
                  SizedBox(
                    width: 130,
                    height: 130,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                      value: c.progress,
                      strokeWidth: 11,
                      backgroundColor:
                          Theme.of(context).dividerColor.withOpacity(0.3),
                      valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(scoreColor),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        '$progress%',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 30,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: scoreColor,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        'الإنجاز',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: _metric(
                  icon: Icons.trending_up,
                  label: 'مؤشر الالتزام',
                  value: '$score%',
                  color: scoreColor,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: _metric(
                  icon: Icons.emoji_events,
                  label: 'التقييم',
                  value: c.adherenceLabel,
                  color: scoreColor,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: _metric(
                  icon: Icons.check_circle,
                  label: 'تمارين مكتملة',
                  value: '$done / $total',
                  color: AppColors.success,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: _metric(
                  icon: Icons.calendar_today,
                  label: 'أيام مضت',
                  value: '${c.daysSinceStart} يوم',
                  color: AppColors.info,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: scoreColor.withOpacity(0.08),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              border: Border.all(color: scoreColor.withOpacity(0.25)),
            ),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.analytics_outlined, size: 16, color: scoreColor),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Text(
                      'التحليل',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 12.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: scoreColor,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                _analysisLine('التقدم الفعلي', progress),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                _analysisLine(
                    'التقدم المتوقع', (c.expectedProgress * 100).round()),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  _analysisText(c),
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    fontSize: 11.5,
                    height: 1.6,
                    color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  String _analysisText(Course c) {
    final score = c.adherenceScore;
    final name = c.player.name;
    if (score >= 90) {
      return 'ممتاز يا $name! التزامك بالبرنامج مثالي. حافظ على هذا المستوى الرائع';
    }
    if (score >= 70) {
      return 'أداء جيد جداً يا $name. استمر على هذا المستوى وستصل لهدفك بسرعة.';
    }
    if (score >= 50) {
      return 'أداؤك متوسط يا $name. حاول زيادة عدد التمارين المكتملة يومياً.';
    }
    if (score >= 25) {
      return 'الالتزام ضعيف يا $name. تحتاج لتحسين جدولك والانتظام أكثر.';
    }
    return '$name، أنت متأخر بشكل كبير عن البرنامج. راجع مدربك لتعديل الخطة.';
  }

  Widget _analysisLine(String label, int percent) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        SizedBox(
          width: 100,
          child: Text(label,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
        ),
        Expanded(
          child: ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: (percent / 100).clamp(0.0, 1.0),
              minHeight: 6,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        SizedBox(
          width: 40,
          child: Text(
            '$percent%',
            textAlign: TextAlign.left,
            style:
                GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _metric({
    required IconData icon,
    required String label,
    required String value,
    required Color color,
  }) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.08),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.25)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Icon(icon, size: 14, color: color),
              const SizedBox(width: 5),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  label,
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 10.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          Text(value,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: color),
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _todayCard(BuildContext context, Course c) {
    final day = c.todayDay;
    if (day == null) {
      return SurfaceCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Text('لا يوجد يوم محدد لليوم',
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 13)),
      );
    }
    final doneCount = day.exercises.where((e) => e.done).length;
    final total = day.exercises.length;
    final completed = c.completedToday;
    final statusColor = completed
        ? AppColors.success
        : (doneCount > 0 ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.error);
    final statusLabel = completed
        ? 'أكمل تمرين اليوم'
        : (doneCount > 0 ? 'قيد التنفيذ ($doneCount/$total)' : 'لم يبدأ بعد');

    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: statusColor.withOpacity(0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
                child: Icon(
                  completed ? Icons.check_circle : Icons.play_circle_outline,
                  color: statusColor,
                  size: 22,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'اليوم ${day.number}: ${day.name}',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 14.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 2),
                    Text(
                      statusLabel,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 11.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: statusColor,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Text(
                '$doneCount/$total',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 13,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  color: statusColor,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: total == 0 ? 0 : doneCount / total,
              minHeight: 6,
              color: statusColor,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          ...day.exercises.take(5).map((e) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      e.done
                          ? Icons.check_circle
                          : Icons.radio_button_unchecked,
                      size: 16,
                      color:
                          e.done ? AppColors.success : AppColors.textSecondary,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    if (e.isSuper)
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 4),
                        child: Icon(Icons.merge_type,
                            size: 12, color: AppColors.purple),
                      ),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        e.nameAr,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 12.5,
                          color: e.done ? AppColors.textSecondary : null,
                          decoration:
                              e.done ? TextDecoration.lineThrough : null,
                        ),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              )),
          if (day.exercises.length > 5)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
              child: Text(
                '+ ${day.exercises.length - 5} تمرين آخر...',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _personalCard(Course? liveCourse) {
    final age = player.playerAge ?? liveCourse?.player.age;
    final weight = player.playerWeight ?? liveCourse?.player.weight;
    final height = player.playerHeight ?? liveCourse?.player.height;

    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Column(children: [
        if (age != null) _dataTile(Icons.cake_outlined, 'العمر', '$age سنة'),
        if (weight != null) ...[
          const Divider(height: 1),
          _dataTile(Icons.monitor_weight_outlined, 'الوزن', '$weight كجم'),
        ],
        if (height != null) ...[
          const Divider(height: 1),
          _dataTile(Icons.height, 'الطول', '$height سم'),
        ],
        const Divider(height: 1),
        _dataTile(Icons.email_outlined, 'البريد', player.email),
        if ((player.phoneNumber ?? '').isNotEmpty) ...[
          const Divider(height: 1),
          _dataTile(Icons.phone_outlined, 'الهاتف', player.phoneNumber!),
        ],
        if (player.createdAt != null) ...[
          const Divider(height: 1),
          _dataTile(Icons.calendar_today_outlined, 'عضو منذ',
              dateFmt.format(player.createdAt!)),
        ],
        const Divider(height: 1),
        _dataTile(
          player.isVerified ? Icons.verified : Icons.gpp_maybe_outlined,
          'حالة التوثيق',
          player.isVerified ? 'موثّق' : 'غير موثّق',
          valueColor:
              player.isVerified ? AppColors.success : AppColors.textSecondary,
        ),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _nutritionCard(Course c) {
    final p = c.player;
    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          _calRow(
              'BMR (أيض أساسي)', '${p.bmr.round()} سعر حراري', AppColors.info),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          _calRow('TDEE (احتياج يومي)',
              '${p.dailyCaloriesNeeded.round()} سعر حراري', AppColors.purple),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          _calRow('المستهدف', '${p.targetCalories.round()} سعر حراري',
              AppColors.primary,
              bold: true),
          const Divider(height: 24),
          _calRow(
            'المُدخل الفعلي',
            p.dailyCaloriesIntake > 0
                ? '${p.dailyCaloriesIntake.round()} سعر حراري'
                : 'لم يُدخل',
            AppColors.textSecondary,
          ),
          if (p.dailyCaloriesIntake > 0) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            _calRow(
              'الفرق',
              '${p.calorieDifference > 0 ? "+" : ""}${p.calorieDifference.round()} سعر',
              p.calorieDifference.abs() <= 150
                  ? AppColors.success
                  : (p.calorieDifference > 0
                      ? AppColors.primary
                      : AppColors.error),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.08),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.info_outline, size: 16, color: AppColors.primary),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      p.calorieStatus,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 11.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        color: AppColors.primary,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
          if (p.notes.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const Divider(height: 24),
            Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.notes, size: 16, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    p.notes,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 12,
                      height: 1.6,
                      color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _calRow(String label, String value, Color color, {bool bold = false}) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Expanded(
          child: Text(
            label,
            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
              fontSize: 12.5,
              fontWeight: bold ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
              color: AppColors.textSecondary,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Text(
          value,
          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
            fontSize: bold ? 15 : 13.5,
            fontWeight: bold ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.w600,
            color: color,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _daysBreakdown(BuildContext context, Course c) {
    if (c.days.isEmpty) {
      return SurfaceCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
        child: Center(
          child: Text('لا توجد أيام في الكورس',
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
        ),
      );
    }
    final todayIdx = c.currentDayIndex;

    return Column(
      children: c.days.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
        final i = entry.key;
        final d = entry.value;
        final done = d.exercises.where((e) => e.done).length;
        final total = d.exercises.length;
        final percent = total == 0 ? 0 : ((done / total) * 100).round();
        final isToday = i == todayIdx;
        final isComplete = total > 0 && done == total;
        final color = isComplete
            ? AppColors.success
            : (isToday ? AppColors.primary : AppColors.textSecondary);

        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
          child: SurfaceCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: 42,
                  height: 42,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    gradient: isComplete
                        ? const LinearGradient(colors: [
                            Color(0xFF22C55E),
                            Color(0xFF16A34A),
                          ])
                        : (isToday ? AppColors.primaryGradient : null),
                    color: (!isComplete && !isToday)
                        ? AppColors.textSecondary.withOpacity(0.2)
                        : null,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                  alignment: Alignment.center,
                  child: isComplete
                      ? const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.white, size: 22)
                      : Text(
                          '${d.number}',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            color: isToday
                                ? Colors.white
                                : AppColors.textSecondary,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            fontSize: 15,
                          ),
                        ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(
                              d.name,
                              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                  fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                            ),
                          ),
                          if (isToday)
                            Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: 7, vertical: 2),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: AppColors.primary.withOpacity(0.15),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                              ),
                              child: Text(
                                'اليوم',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                  fontSize: 10,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  color: AppColors.primary,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 5),
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: ClipRRect(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                              child: LinearProgressIndicator(
                                value: total == 0 ? 0 : done / total,
                                minHeight: 5,
                                color: color,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 10),
                          Text(
                            '$done/$total ($percent%)',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 11,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                              color: color,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      }).toList(),
    );
  }

  Widget _dataTile(IconData icon, String label, String value,
      {Color? valueColor}) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icon, color: AppColors.primary, size: 20),
      title: Text(label,
          style:
              GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
      subtitle: Text(
        value,
        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
          fontSize: 14,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          color: valueColor,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MY CREATED COURSES SCREEN
// ============================================================
class MyCreatedCoursesScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const MyCreatedCoursesScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('كورساتي التي أنشأتها')),
      body: StreamBuilder<List<Course>>(
        stream: FirebaseService.instance.myCreatedCoursesStream(),
        builder: (context, snap) {
          if (snap.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
          }
          final list = snap.data ?? [];
          if (list.isEmpty) {
            return Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.assignment_outlined,
                      size: 60,
                      color: AppColors.textSecondary.withOpacity(0.5)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Text('لم تُنشئ أي كورسات بعد',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14)),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }
          return FutureBuilder<UserProfile?>(
            future: FirebaseService.instance.getUserProfile(),
            builder: (context, profSnap) {
              final profile = profSnap.data;
              if (profile == null) {
                return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
              }
              return ListView.builder(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                itemCount: list.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, i) => Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                  child: _ManagedCourseCard(
                    course: list[i],
                    profile: profile,
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// MY PLAYERS SCREEN
// ============================================================
class MyPlayersScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const MyPlayersScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('لاعبيّ')),
      body: FutureBuilder<UserProfile?>(
        future: FirebaseService.instance.getUserProfile(),
        builder: (context, meSnap) {
          final me = meSnap.data;
          return FutureBuilder<List<UserProfile>>(
            future: FirebaseService.instance.getPlayersForCoach(),
            builder: (context, snap) {
              if (!snap.hasData) {
                return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
              }
              final users = snap.data!;
              if (users.isEmpty) {
                return Center(
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.people_outline,
                          size: 60,
                          color: AppColors.textSecondary.withOpacity(0.5)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      Text('لم تُنشئ أي لاعبين بعد',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              color: AppColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 14)),
                    ],
                  ),
                );
              }
              return ListView.builder(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                itemCount: users.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                  final u = users[i];
                  return Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                    child: SurfaceCard(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                      onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                        context,
                        MaterialPageRoute(
                          builder: (_) => PlayerDetailsScreen(
                            player: u,
                            me: me,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      child: Row(children: [
                        Container(
                          width: 44,
                          height: 44,
                          alignment: Alignment.center,
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            gradient: AppColors.primaryGradient,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            u.displayName.isNotEmpty ? u.displayName[0] : '?',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              color: Colors.white,
                              fontSize: 18,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 12),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Row(
                                children: [
                                  Flexible(
                                    child: Text(u.displayName,
                                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                            fontSize: 14.5),
                                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                                  ),
                                  if (u.isVerified) ...[
                                    const SizedBox(width: 5),
                                    Icon(Icons.verified,
                                        color: AppColors.info, size: 15),
                                  ],
                                ],
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 3),
                              Text(u.email,
                                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                      fontSize: 11.5,
                                      color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                              const SizedBox(height: 6),
                              MiniChip(
                                text: u.subscriptionLabel,
                                color: u.hasActiveSubscription
                                    ? AppColors.success
                                    : AppColors.error,
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios,
                            size: 14, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                      ]),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              );
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// ADMIN USERS SCREEN
// ============================================================
class AdminUsersScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final String? filterRole;
  const AdminUsersScreen({super.key, this.filterRole});

  @override
  State<AdminUsersScreen> createState() => _AdminUsersScreenState();
}

class _AdminUsersScreenState extends State<AdminUsersScreen> {
  String _query = '';
  bool _loading = true;
  List<UserProfile> _users = [];
  UserProfile? _me;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    final me = await FirebaseService.instance.getUserProfile();
    final list = await FirebaseService.instance.getAllUsers();
    if (mounted) {
      setState(() {
        _me = me;
        _users = list;
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _activateDialog(UserProfile u) async {
    final remaining = u.daysRemaining;
    final hasActive = u.hasActiveSubscription && !u.canManageCourses;
    final controller = TextEditingController(text: '30');

    final plan = await showDialog<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text('إضافة أيام لاشتراك: ${u.displayName}'),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            if (hasActive)
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.success.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  border: Border.all(color: AppColors.success.withOpacity(0.3)),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.info_outline,
                        size: 16, color: AppColors.success),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        'الأيام المتبقية حالياً: $remaining يوم\nسيتم إضافتها فوق المدة الحالية ✓',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          color: AppColors.success,
                          height: 1.5,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              )
            else
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.info.withOpacity(0.1),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  border: Border.all(color: AppColors.info.withOpacity(0.3)),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.info_outline, size: 16, color: AppColors.info),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        'لا يوجد اشتراك ساري حالياً.\nسيتم بدء الاشتراك من اليوم.',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.info, height: 1.5),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            TextField(
              controller: controller,
              keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
              decoration:
                  const InputDecoration(labelText: 'عدد الأيام المُضافة'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('إلغاء'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, controller.text),
            child: const Text('إضافة'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    if (plan == null) return;
    final days = int.tryParse(plan);
    if (days == null || days <= 0) return;

    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.adminActivateSubscription(
        uid: u.uid,
        days: days,
        plan: '${days}_days',
      );
      if (mounted) {
        final newTotal = hasActive ? remaining + days : days;
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text(hasActive
                ? 'تمت إضافة $days يوم. المجموع الجديد: $newTotal يوم'
                : 'تم تفعيل $days يوم لـ ${u.displayName}'),
            backgroundColor: AppColors.success,
          ),
        );
        _load();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _extendDialog(UserProfile u) async {
    final controller = TextEditingController(text: '30');
    final result = await showDialog<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text('تمديد اشتراك: ${u.displayName}'),
        content: TextField(
          controller: controller,
          keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
          decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'عدد الأيام الإضافية'),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('إلغاء'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, controller.text),
            child: const Text('تمديد'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (result == null) return;
    final days = int.tryParse(result);
    if (days == null || days <= 0) return;

    await FirebaseService.instance.extendSubscription(u.uid, days);
    if (mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('تم تمديد $days يوم')),
      );
      _load();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _cancel(UserProfile u) async {
    final ok = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('إلغاء الاشتراك'),
        content: Text('هل تريد إلغاء اشتراك ${u.displayName}؟'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('تراجع'),
          ),
          FilledButton(
            style: FilledButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.error),
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text('إلغاء'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (ok != true) return;

    await FirebaseService.instance.adminCancelSubscription(u.uid);
    if (mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('تم إلغاء الاشتراك')),
      );
      _load();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _changeRoleDialog(UserProfile u) async {
    final me = _me;
    if (me == null) return;
    final isSuper = me.isSuperAdmin;
    final roles = <String, String>{
      'user': 'لاعب',
      'captain': 'كابتن',
      if (isSuper) 'admin': 'مدير',
      if (isSuper) 'superAdmin': 'إشراف عام',
    };

    final chosen = await showDialog<String>(
      context: context,
      builder: (_) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text('تغيير دور: ${u.displayName}'),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: roles.entries
              .map((e) => RadioListTile<String>(
                    value: e.key,
                    groupValue: u.role,
                    onChanged: (v) => Navigator.pop(context, v),
                    title:
                        Text(e.value, style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14)),
                  ))
              .toList(),
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (chosen == null || chosen == u.role) return;

    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.setUserRole(u.uid, chosen);
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
              content:
                  Text('تم تغيير دور ${u.displayName} إلى ${roles[chosen]}')),
        );
        _load();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _toggleVerify(UserProfile u) async {
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.setUserVerified(u.uid, !u.isVerified);
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text(u.isVerified
                ? 'تم إلغاء توثيق ${u.displayName}'
                : 'تم توثيق ${u.displayName}'),
            backgroundColor: u.isVerified ? AppColors.error : AppColors.success,
          ),
        );
        _load();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  Color _roleColor(String role) {
    switch (role) {
      case 'superAdmin':
        return const Color(0xFFDB2777);
      case 'admin':
        return AppColors.info;
      case 'captain':
        return AppColors.purple;
      default:
        return AppColors.primary;
    }
  }

  Color _statusColor(UserProfile u) {
    if (u.isSuperAdmin) return const Color(0xFFDB2777);
    if (u.isAdmin) return AppColors.info;
    if (u.isCaptain) return AppColors.purple;
    if (u.isTrial && u.hasActiveSubscription) return AppColors.purple;
    if (u.hasActiveSubscription) return AppColors.success;
    if (u.needsActivation) return AppColors.primary;
    return AppColors.error;
  }

  IconData _roleIcon(UserProfile u) {
    if (u.isSuperAdmin) return Icons.verified_user;
    if (u.isAdmin) return Icons.admin_panel_settings;
    if (u.isCaptain) return Icons.sports;
    if (u.isTrial) return Icons.card_giftcard;
    return Icons.person;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final me = _me;
    final isSuper = me?.isSuperAdmin ?? false;
    final filtered = _users.where((u) {
      if (widget.filterRole != null && u.role != widget.filterRole) {
        return false;
      }
      if (_query.isEmpty) return true;
      final q = _query.toLowerCase();
      return u.displayName.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
          u.email.toLowerCase().contains(q);
    }).toList();

    final title = widget.filterRole == 'captain' ? 'الكباتن' : 'المستخدمون';

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(title),
        actions: [
          IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: _load),
        ],
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            child: TextField(
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14),
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                hintText: 'ابحث بالاسم أو البريد...',
                prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search),
              ),
              onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _query = v),
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: _loading
                ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                : ListView.builder(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                    itemCount: filtered.length,
                    itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                      final u = filtered[i];
                      final statusColor = _statusColor(u);
                      final roleColor = _roleColor(u.role);
                      final canEditThis = u.uid != me?.uid &&
                          (isSuper || (!u.isSuperAdmin && !u.isAdmin));

                      return Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                        child: SurfaceCard(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                          onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                            context,
                            MaterialPageRoute(
                              builder: (_) => PlayerDetailsScreen(player: u),
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: Row(children: [
                            Container(
                              width: 44,
                              height: 44,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: statusColor.withOpacity(0.15),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                              ),
                              alignment: Alignment.center,
                              child: Icon(_roleIcon(u), color: statusColor),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: 12),
                            Expanded(
                              child: Column(
                                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                children: [
                                  Row(children: [
                                    Flexible(
                                      child: Text(u.displayName,
                                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                              fontSize: 14),
                                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                                    ),
                                    if (u.isVerified) ...[
                                      const SizedBox(width: 4),
                                      Icon(Icons.verified,
                                          color: AppColors.info, size: 15),
                                    ],
                                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                    MiniChip(
                                        text: u.roleLabel, color: roleColor),
                                  ]),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 2),
                                  Text(u.email,
                                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                          fontSize: 11.5,
                                          color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                  Text(u.subscriptionLabel,
                                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                          fontSize: 11.5,
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                          color: statusColor)),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                            PopupMenuButton<String>(
                              onSelected: (v) async {
                                switch (v) {
                                  case 'activate':
                                    await _activateDialog(u);
                                    break;
                                  case 'extend':
                                    await _extendDialog(u);
                                    break;
                                  case 'cancel':
                                    await _cancel(u);
                                    break;
                                  case 'change_role':
                                    await _changeRoleDialog(u);
                                    break;
                                  case 'toggle_verify':
                                    await _toggleVerify(u);
                                    break;
                                  case 'view_profile':
                                    if (mounted) {
                                      Navigator.push(
                                        context,
                                        MaterialPageRoute(
                                          builder: (_) =>
                                              PlayerDetailsScreen(player: u),
                                        ),
                                      );
                                    }
                                    break;
                                  case 'view_stats':
                                    if (mounted) {
                                      Navigator.push(
                                        context,
                                        MaterialPageRoute(
                                          builder: (_) =>
                                              CoachStatsDetailScreen(
                                                  coachUid: u.uid),
                                        ),
                                      );
                                    }
                                    break;
                                }
                              },
                              itemBuilder: (_) => [
                                PopupMenuItem(
                                  value: 'view_profile',
                                  child: ListTile(
                                    leading: Icon(Icons.person,
                                        color: AppColors.primary),
                                    title: const Text('عرض البروفايل'),
                                    dense: true,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                if (isSuper && u.canManageCourses)
                                  PopupMenuItem(
                                    value: 'view_stats',
                                    child: ListTile(
                                      leading: Icon(Icons.insights,
                                          color: AppColors.purple),
                                      title: const Text('عرض إحصائياته'),
                                      dense: true,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                const PopupMenuDivider(),
                                PopupMenuItem(
                                  value: 'activate',
                                  enabled: canEditThis,
                                  child: ListTile(
                                    leading: Icon(Icons.check_circle,
                                        color: AppColors.success),
                                    title: const Text('تفعيل الاشتراك'),
                                    dense: true,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                PopupMenuItem(
                                  value: 'extend',
                                  enabled: canEditThis,
                                  child: ListTile(
                                    leading: Icon(Icons.add,
                                        color: AppColors.primary),
                                    title: const Text('تمديد الاشتراك'),
                                    dense: true,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                PopupMenuItem(
                                  value: 'cancel',
                                  enabled: canEditThis,
                                  child: ListTile(
                                    leading: Icon(Icons.block,
                                        color: AppColors.error),
                                    title: const Text('إلغاء الاشتراك'),
                                    dense: true,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                const PopupMenuDivider(),
                                PopupMenuItem(
                                  value: 'change_role',
                                  enabled: canEditThis,
                                  child: ListTile(
                                    leading: Icon(Icons.swap_horiz,
                                        color: AppColors.purple),
                                    title: Text(isSuper
                                        ? 'تغيير الدور (لاعب/كابتن/مدير/إشراف)'
                                        : 'تغيير الدور (لاعب / كابتن)'),
                                    dense: true,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                PopupMenuItem(
                                  value: 'toggle_verify',
                                  enabled: canEditThis,
                                  child: ListTile(
                                    leading: Icon(
                                      u.isVerified
                                          ? Icons.gpp_bad
                                          : Icons.verified,
                                      color: u.isVerified
                                          ? AppColors.error
                                          : AppColors.success,
                                    ),
                                    title: Text(u.isVerified
                                        ? 'إلغاء التوثيق'
                                        : 'توثيق الحساب'),
                                    dense: true,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ]),
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// ⬆️ SUBSCRIPTION STATUS CARD
// ============================================================
class SubscriptionStatusCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final UserProfile profile;
  const SubscriptionStatusCard({super.key, required this.profile});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isTrial = profile.isTrial && profile.hasActiveSubscription;
    final isActive = profile.hasActiveSubscription && !isTrial;
    final isExpired = profile.isExpired;
    final needsActivation = profile.needsActivation;

    Color statusColor;
    IconData statusIcon;
    String statusLabel;
    String buttonLabel;
    String description;

    if (needsActivation) {
      statusColor = AppColors.primary;
      statusIcon = Icons.lock_outline;
      statusLabel = 'غير مفعّل';
      buttonLabel = 'تفعيل الاشتراك الآن';
      description = 'فعّل اشتراكك للوصول الكامل إلى كل الميزات.';
    } else if (isExpired) {
      statusColor = AppColors.error;
      statusIcon = Icons.error_outline;
      statusLabel = 'منتهي';
      buttonLabel = 'تجديد الاشتراك الآن';
      description = 'انتهى اشتراكك، جدّد الآن لمواصلة تمارينك.';
    } else if (isTrial) {
      statusColor = AppColors.purple;
      statusIcon = Icons.card_giftcard;
      statusLabel = 'تجريبي';
      buttonLabel = 'ترقية للاشتراك الكامل';
      description =
          'أنت في الفترة التجريبية. يمكنك الترقية الآن للاستفادة من 90 يوماً كاملة.';
    } else {
      statusColor = AppColors.success;
      statusIcon = Icons.verified_user;
      statusLabel = 'نشط';
      buttonLabel = 'تمديد / تجديد الاشتراك';
      description =
          'اشتراكك نشط حالياً. يمكنك التجديد مبكراً لإضافة 90 يوماً إضافية.';
    }

    return SurfaceCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: statusColor.withOpacity(0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
                child: Icon(statusIcon, color: statusColor, size: 22),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Text('حالة الاشتراك',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: statusColor.withOpacity(0.15),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                          ),
                          child: Text(statusLabel,
                              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                  fontSize: 11,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  color: statusColor)),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 3),
                    Text(profile.subscriptionLabel,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.info.withOpacity(0.08),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.info_outline, size: 16, color: AppColors.info),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(description,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          color: AppColors.textSecondary,
                          height: 1.5)),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          if (isActive || isTrial) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: _infoBox(
                    Icons.calendar_today,
                    'ينتهي في',
                    profile.subscriptionEnd != null
                        ? dateFmt.format(profile.subscriptionEnd!)
                        : '-',
                    AppColors.primary,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 10),
                Expanded(
                  child: _infoBox(
                    Icons.hourglass_bottom,
                    'المتبقي',
                    '${profile.daysRemaining} يوم',
                    AppColors.success,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: GradientButton(
              label: buttonLabel,
              icon: Icons.upgrade,
              onPressed: () async {
                final result = await Navigator.push<bool>(
                  context,
                  MaterialPageRoute(
                    builder: (_) => PaymentScreen(profile: profile),
                  ),
                );
                if (result == true && context.mounted) {
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    const SnackBar(
                      content: Text('🎉 تم تفعيل اشتراكك لمدة 90 يوماً!'),
                      backgroundColor: Color(0xFF22C55E),
                      duration: Duration(seconds: 4),
                    ),
                  );
                }
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _infoBox(IconData icon, String label, String value, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.08),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.2)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 16, color: color),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(label,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 10, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                Text(value,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 12.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: color),
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

                        color: AppColors.primary, size: 20),
