// ============================================================
// EDIT MY PROFILE SCREEN
// ============================================================
class EditMyProfileScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const EditMyProfileScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<EditMyProfileScreen> createState() => _EditMyProfileScreenState();
}

class _EditMyProfileScreenState extends State<EditMyProfileScreen> {
  final _form = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _age = TextEditingController();
  final _weight = TextEditingController();
  final _height = TextEditingController();
  bool _loading = false;
  bool _loaded = false;
  UserProfile? _profile;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    final p = await FirebaseService.instance.getUserProfile();
    if (mounted) {
      setState(() {
        _profile = p;
        _age.text = p?.playerAge?.toString() ?? '';
        _weight.text = p?.playerWeight?.toString() ?? '';
        _height.text = p?.playerHeight?.toString() ?? '';
        _loaded = true;
      });
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _age.dispose();
    _weight.dispose();
    _height.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    if (!_form.currentState!.validate()) return;
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.updateMyPlayerInfo(
        age: int.parse(_age.text),
        weight: double.parse(_weight.text),
        height: double.parse(_height.text),
      );
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text('تم تحديث بياناتك بنجاح'),
            backgroundColor: Color(0xFF22C55E),
          ),
        );
        Navigator.pop(context);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (!_loaded) {
      return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
    }
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('تعديل بياناتي')),
      body: Form(
        key: _form,
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          children: [
            GradientCard(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
              child: Column(children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  ),
                  child:
                      const Icon(Icons.person, color: Colors.white, size: 30),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Flexible(
                      child: Text(_profile?.displayName ?? '',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              color: Colors.white,
                              fontSize: 18,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                    ),
                    if (_profile?.isVerified == true) ...[
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      const Icon(Icons.verified, color: Colors.white, size: 22),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(_profile?.email ?? '',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.85), fontSize: 12)),
              ]),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            const SectionTitle(title: 'البيانات الشخصية'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            SurfaceCard(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text('سيتم تحديث هذه البيانات في جميع كورساتك تلقائياً',
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  TextFormField(
                    controller: _age,
                    keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(
                      labelText: 'العمر (سنة)',
                      prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.cake_outlined),
                    ),
                    validator: (v) {
                      if (v == null || v.isEmpty) return 'مطلوب';
                      final n = int.tryParse(v);
                      if (n == null || n <= 0 || n > 100) {
                        return 'قيمة غير صحيحة';
                      }
                      return null;
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  TextFormField(
                    controller: _weight,
                    keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(
                      labelText: 'الوزن (كجم)',
                      prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.monitor_weight_outlined),
                    ),
                    validator: (v) {
                      if (v == null || v.isEmpty) return 'مطلوب';
                      final n = double.tryParse(v);
                      if (n == null || n <= 0 || n > 300) {
                        return 'قيمة غير صحيحة';
                      }
                      return null;
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  TextFormField(
                    controller: _height,
                    keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 14.5),
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(
                      labelText: 'الطول (سم)',
                      prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.height),
                    ),
                    validator: (v) {
                      if (v == null || v.isEmpty) return 'مطلوب';
                      final n = double.tryParse(v);
                      if (n == null || n <= 0 || n > 250) {
                        return 'قيمة غير صحيحة';
                      }
                      return null;
                    },
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            GradientButton(
              label: 'حفظ التعديلات',
              icon: Icons.save,
              loading: _loading,
              onPressed: _save,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// 💳 PAYMENT LOG SYSTEM
// ============================================================
class PaymentTransaction {
  final String orderId;
  final String? paymentId,
      paymentMethod,
      refundId,
      refundReason,
      refundedByName;
  final String userId, userEmail, userName, currency, status;
  final int amount, days;
  final int? daysAdded;
  final bool? wasExtension;
  final DateTime? createdAt,
      completedAt,
      refundedAt,
      previousEndDate,
      newEndDate;

  PaymentTransaction({
    required this.orderId,
    this.paymentId,
    required this.userId,
    required this.userEmail,
    required this.userName,
    required this.amount,
    required this.currency,
    required this.days,
    required this.status,
    this.paymentMethod,
    this.createdAt,
    this.completedAt,
    this.refundedAt,
    this.refundId,
    this.refundReason,
    this.refundedByName,
    this.previousEndDate,
    this.newEndDate,
    this.daysAdded,
    this.wasExtension,
  });

  factory PaymentTransaction.fromDoc(DocumentSnapshot doc) {
    final d = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
    DateTime? dt(dynamic v) => v is Timestamp ? v.toDate() : null;
    return PaymentTransaction(
      orderId: d['orderId'] ?? doc.id,
      paymentId: d['paymentId'],
      userId: d['userId'] ?? '',
      userEmail: d['userEmail'] ?? '',
      userName: d['userName'] ?? '',
      amount: (d['amount'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
      currency: d['currency'] ?? 'IQD',
      days: (d['days'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
      status: d['status'] ?? 'pending',
      paymentMethod: d['paymentMethod'],
      createdAt: dt(d['createdAt']),
      completedAt: dt(d['completedAt']),
      refundedAt: dt(d['refundedAt']),
      refundId: d['refundId'],
      refundReason: d['refundReason'],
      refundedByName: d['refundedByName'],
      previousEndDate: dt(d['previousEndDate']),
      newEndDate: dt(d['newEndDate']),
      daysAdded: (d['daysAdded'] as num?)?.toInt(),
      wasExtension: d['wasExtension'] as bool?,
    );
  }

  String get statusLabel =>
      {
        'succeeded': 'ناجحة',
        'pending': 'معلقة',
        'failed': 'فاشلة',
        'refunded': 'مستردّة'
      }[status] ??
      status;

  Color get statusColor =>
      {
        'succeeded': AppColors.success,
        'pending': const Color(0xFFF59E0B),
        'failed': AppColors.error,
        'refunded': AppColors.info
      }[status] ??
      AppColors.textSecondary;

  IconData get statusIcon =>
      {
        'succeeded': Icons.check_circle,
        'pending': Icons.hourglass_top,
        'failed': Icons.error_outline,
        'refunded': Icons.undo
      }[status] ??
      Icons.receipt_long;
}

class ActivationLog {
  final String logId, userId, userName, userEmail, type;
  final String? source, orderId, paymentId, currency, reason, refundedByName;
  final String? activatedByName;
  final String? activatedByUid;
  final int? daysAdded, amount, daysRemoved;
  final bool? wasExtension;
  final DateTime? createdAt;
  final DateTime? previousEndDate;
  final DateTime? newEndDate;

  ActivationLog({
    required this.logId,
    required this.userId,
    required this.userName,
    required this.userEmail,
    required this.type,
    this.source,
    this.orderId,
    this.paymentId,
    this.daysAdded,
    this.wasExtension,
    this.amount,
    this.currency,
    this.daysRemoved,
    this.reason,
    this.refundedByName,
    this.activatedByName,
    this.activatedByUid,
    this.previousEndDate,
    this.newEndDate,
    this.createdAt,
  });

  factory ActivationLog.fromDoc(DocumentSnapshot doc) {
    final d = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
    DateTime? dt(dynamic v) => v is Timestamp ? v.toDate() : null;
    return ActivationLog(
      logId: d['logId'] ?? doc.id,
      userId: d['userId'] ?? '',
      userName: d['userName'] ?? '',
      userEmail: d['userEmail'] ?? '',
      type: d['type'] ?? '',
      source: d['source'],
      orderId: d['orderId'],
      paymentId: d['paymentId'],
      daysAdded: (d['daysAdded'] as num?)?.toInt(),
      wasExtension: d['wasExtension'] as bool?,
      amount: (d['amount'] as num?)?.toInt(),
      currency: d['currency'],
      daysRemoved: (d['daysRemoved'] as num?)?.toInt(),
      reason: d['reason'],
      refundedByName: d['refundedByName'],
      activatedByName: d['activatedByName'],
      activatedByUid: d['activatedByUid'],
      previousEndDate: dt(d['previousEndDate']),
      newEndDate: dt(d['newEndDate']),
      createdAt: dt(d['createdAt']),
    );
  }

  String get typeLabel =>
      {
        'payment_activation': 'تفعيل بالدفع',
        'manual_activation': 'تفعيل يدوي',
        'refund': 'استرداد'
      }[type] ??
      type;

  Color get typeColor =>
      {
        'payment_activation': AppColors.success,
        'manual_activation': AppColors.purple,
        'refund': AppColors.error
      }[type] ??
      AppColors.textSecondary;

  IconData get typeIcon =>
      {
        'payment_activation': Icons.payment,
        'manual_activation': Icons.admin_panel_settings,
        'refund': Icons.undo
      }[type] ??
      Icons.history;
}

class PaymentLogScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const PaymentLogScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<PaymentLogScreen> createState() => _PaymentLogScreenState();
}

class _PaymentLogScreenState extends State<PaymentLogScreen>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late TabController _tabs;
  String _query = '', _statusFilter = 'all';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabs = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabs.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('سجل الدفع والتفعيل'),
        bottom: TabBar(controller: _tabs, tabs: const [
          Tab(icon: Icon(Icons.receipt_long), text: 'عمليات الدفع'),
          Tab(icon: Icon(Icons.history), text: 'سجل التفعيلات'),
        ]),
      ),
      body: TabBarView(controller: _tabs, children: [
        _PaymentsTab(
            query: _query,
            statusFilter: _statusFilter,
            onQueryChanged: (v) => setState(() => _query = v),
            onStatusChanged: (v) => setState(() => _statusFilter = v)),
        const _ActivationsTab(),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

class _PaymentsTab extends StatefulWidget {
  final String query;
  final String statusFilter;
  final ValueChanged<String> onQueryChanged;
  final ValueChanged<String> onStatusChanged;
  const _PaymentsTab({
    required this.query,
    required this.statusFilter,
    required this.onQueryChanged,
    required this.onStatusChanged,
  });

  @override
  State<_PaymentsTab> createState() => _PaymentsTabState();
}

class _PaymentsTabState extends State<_PaymentsTab> {
  static const int _pageSize = 50;
  final List<PaymentTransaction> _items = [];
  DocumentSnapshot? _lastDoc;
  bool _loadingFirst = true;
  bool _loadingMore = false;
  bool _hasMore = true;
  String? _error;
  final ScrollController _scrollCtrl = ScrollController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _scrollCtrl.addListener(_onScroll);
    _loadFirst();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scrollCtrl.removeListener(_onScroll);
    _scrollCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onScroll() {
    if (!_scrollCtrl.hasClients) return;
    final pos = _scrollCtrl.position;
    if (pos.pixels >= pos.maxScrollExtent - 300) {
      _loadMore();
    }
  }

  Query _baseQuery() {
    return FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('payment_transactions')
        .orderBy('createdAt', descending: true);
  }

  Future<void> _loadFirst() async {
    setState(() {
      _loadingFirst = true;
      _error = null;
      _items.clear();
      _lastDoc = null;
      _hasMore = true;
    });
    try {
      final snap = await _baseQuery().limit(_pageSize).get();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _items.addAll(
          snap.docs.map((d) => PaymentTransaction.fromDoc(d)).toList(),
        );
        _lastDoc = snap.docs.isNotEmpty ? snap.docs.last : null;
        _hasMore = snap.docs.length == _pageSize;
        _loadingFirst = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _loadingFirst = false;
      });
    }
  }

  static Map<String, dynamic>? _statsCache;
  static DateTime? _statsCacheAt;

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _loadStats() async {
    if (_statsCache != null &&
        _statsCacheAt != null &&
        DateTime.now().difference(_statsCacheAt!).inMinutes < 2) {
      return _statsCache!;
    }
    try {
      final callable = FirebaseFunctions.instance.httpsCallable(
        'getPaymentStats',
        options: HttpsCallableOptions(timeout: const Duration(seconds: 15)),
      );
      final r = await callable.call();
      final data = Map<String, dynamic>.from(r.data as Map);
      _statsCache = data;
      _statsCacheAt = DateTime.now();
      return data;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('getPaymentStats ERROR: $e');
      return _computeStatsLocally();
    }
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> _computeStatsLocally() {
    int succeeded = 0, refunded = 0, failed = 0, pending = 0;
    final payers = <String>{};
    int net = 0;
    for (final t in _items) {
      switch (t.status) {
        case 'succeeded':
          succeeded++;
          net += t.amount;
          break;
        case 'refunded':
          refunded++;
          net -= t.amount;
          break;
        case 'failed':
          failed++;
          break;
        case 'pending':
          pending++;
          break;
      }
      if (t.status == 'succeeded' && t.userId.isNotEmpty) {
        payers.add(t.userId);
      }
    }
    return {
      'totalTransactions': _items.length,
      'totalSucceeded': succeeded,
      'totalRefunded': refunded,
      'totalFailed': failed,
      'totalPending': pending,
      'uniquePayers': payers.length,
      'netRevenue': net,
    };
  }

  Future<void> _loadMore() async {
    if (_loadingMore || !_hasMore || _loadingFirst) return;
    setState(() => _loadingMore = true);
    try {
      Query q = _baseQuery();
      if (_lastDoc != null) {
        q = q.startAfterDocument(_lastDoc!);
      }
      final snap = await q.limit(_pageSize).get();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _items.addAll(
          snap.docs.map((d) => PaymentTransaction.fromDoc(d)).toList(),
        );
        if (snap.docs.isNotEmpty) _lastDoc = snap.docs.last;
        _hasMore = snap.docs.length == _pageSize;
        _loadingMore = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _loadingMore = false);
    }
  }

  List<PaymentTransaction> get _filtered {
    if (widget.query.isEmpty) return _items;
    final q = widget.query.toLowerCase();
    return _items
        .where((t) =>
            t.userEmail.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
            t.orderId.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
            t.userName.toLowerCase().contains(q))
        .toList();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        FutureBuilder<Map<String, dynamic>>(
          future: _loadStats(),
          builder: (context, snap) {
            if (snap.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
              return const Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),
                child: Center(
                  child: SizedBox(
                    width: 22,
                    height: 22,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2.4),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            }
            if (snap.hasError || !snap.hasData) {
              return Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                child: Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppColors.error.withOpacity(0.08),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    border:
                        Border.all(color: AppColors.error.withOpacity(0.25)),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.warning_amber,
                          color: AppColors.error, size: 18),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          'تعذّر تحميل الإحصائيات. يمكنك تصفح السجل أدناه.',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 11.5, color: AppColors.error),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              );
            }
            final s = snap.data!;
            return Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: _statBox(
                          'إجمالي',
                          '${s['totalTransactions'] ?? 0}',
                          AppColors.primary,
                          Icons.receipt_long,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                        child: _statBox('ناجحة', '${s['totalSucceeded'] ?? 0}',
                            AppColors.success, Icons.check_circle),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                        child: _statBox('مستردّة', '${s['totalRefunded'] ?? 0}',
                            AppColors.info, Icons.undo),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: _statBox('الدافعون', '${s['uniquePayers'] ?? 0}',
                            AppColors.purple, Icons.people),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Expanded(
                        child: _statBox('الصافي', '${s['netRevenue'] ?? 0}',
                            AppColors.primary, Icons.payments),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        ),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
            child: Row(
              children: [
                _chip('all', 'الكل'),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                _chip('succeeded', 'ناجحة'),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                _chip('pending', 'معلقة'),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                _chip('failed', 'فاشلة'),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                _chip('refunded', 'مستردّة'),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          child: TextField(
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              hintText: 'ابحث بالبريد أو رقم العملية...',
              prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search),
            ),
            onChanged: widget.onQueryChanged,
          ),
        ),
        Expanded(
          child: _loadingFirst
              ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
              : _error != null
                  ? Center(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                        child: Text(
                          'تعذّر تحميل السجل:\n$_error',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.error),
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                  : _filtered.isEmpty
                      ? Center(
                          child: Text('لا توجد عمليات',
                              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                  color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                        )
                      : RefreshIndicator(
                          onRefresh: _loadFirst,
                          child: ListView.builder(
                            controller: _scrollCtrl,
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                            itemCount: _filtered.length + 1,
                            itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                              if (i == _filtered.length) {
                                return _buildFooter();
                              }
                              return _paymentCard(context, _filtered[i]);
                            },
                          ),
                        ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFooter() {
    if (_loadingMore) {
      return const Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
        child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      );
    }
    if (!_hasMore && _items.isNotEmpty) {
      return Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
        child: Center(
          child: Text(
            'لا توجد عمليات أخرى',
            style:
                GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
    return const SizedBox.shrink();
  }

  Widget _chip(String value, String label) {
    return ChoiceChip(
      label: Text(label, style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12)),
      selected: widget.statusFilter == value,
      onSelected: (_) => widget.onStatusChanged(value),
    );
  }

  Widget _statBox(String label, String value, Color color, IconData icon) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: color.withOpacity(0.08),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.25)),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, color: color, size: 18),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(value,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: color),
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
          Text(label,
              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                  fontSize: 9.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              textAlign: TextAlign.center),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _paymentCard(BuildContext context, PaymentTransaction t) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      child: SurfaceCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        onTap: () => Navigator.push(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(
            builder: (_) => PaymentDetailScreen(transaction: t),
          ),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: t.statusColor.withOpacity(0.15),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                child: Icon(t.statusIcon, color: t.statusColor, size: 20),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      t.userName.isNotEmpty ? t.userName : t.userEmail,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                    Text(
                      t.userEmail,
                      style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              MiniChip(text: t.statusLabel, color: t.statusColor),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Row(children: [
              Icon(Icons.tag, size: 13, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  t.orderId,
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 10.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              Text(
                '${t.amount} ${t.currency}',
                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    fontSize: 14,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: t.statusColor),
              ),
            ]),
            if (t.createdAt != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Row(children: [
                Icon(Icons.schedule, size: 13, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                Text(
                  dateFmt.format(t.createdAt!),
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                ),
                if (t.wasExtension == true) ...[
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  MiniChip(
                    text: 'تمديد',
                    color: AppColors.purple,
                    icon: Icons.add_circle_outline,
                  ),
                ],
                if (t.daysAdded != null && t.daysAdded! > 0) ...[
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  MiniChip(
                    text: '+${t.daysAdded} يوم',
                    color: AppColors.success,
                  ),
                ],
              ]),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ActivationsTab extends StatefulWidget {
  const _ActivationsTab();

  @override
  State<_ActivationsTab> createState() => _ActivationsTabState();
}

class _ActivationsTabState extends State<_ActivationsTab> {
  static const int _pageSize = 50;
  final List<ActivationLog> _items = [];
  DocumentSnapshot? _lastDoc;
  bool _loadingFirst = true;
  bool _loadingMore = false;
  bool _hasMore = true;
  String? _error;
  String _query = '';
  String _typeFilter = 'all';
  final ScrollController _scrollCtrl = ScrollController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _scrollCtrl.addListener(_onScroll);
    _loadFirst();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scrollCtrl.removeListener(_onScroll);
    _scrollCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onScroll() {
    if (!_scrollCtrl.hasClients) return;
    final pos = _scrollCtrl.position;
    if (pos.pixels >= pos.maxScrollExtent - 300) {
      _loadMore();
    }
  }

  Query _baseQuery() {
    return FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('activation_logs')
        .orderBy('createdAt', descending: true);
  }

  Future<void> _loadFirst() async {
    setState(() {
      _loadingFirst = true;
      _error = null;
      _items.clear();
      _lastDoc = null;
      _hasMore = true;
    });
    try {
      final snap = await _baseQuery().limit(_pageSize).get();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _items.addAll(
          snap.docs.map((d) => ActivationLog.fromDoc(d)).toList(),
        );
        _lastDoc = snap.docs.isNotEmpty ? snap.docs.last : null;
        _hasMore = snap.docs.length == _pageSize;
        _loadingFirst = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _error = e.toString();
        _loadingFirst = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _loadMore() async {
    if (_loadingMore || !_hasMore || _loadingFirst) return;
    setState(() => _loadingMore = true);
    try {
      Query q = _baseQuery();
      if (_lastDoc != null) {
        q = q.startAfterDocument(_lastDoc!);
      }
      final snap = await q.limit(_pageSize).get();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _items.addAll(
          snap.docs.map((d) => ActivationLog.fromDoc(d)).toList(),
        );
        if (snap.docs.isNotEmpty) _lastDoc = snap.docs.last;
        _hasMore = snap.docs.length == _pageSize;
        _loadingMore = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _loadingMore = false);
    }
  }

  List<ActivationLog> get _filtered {
    Iterable<ActivationLog> list = _items;
    if (_typeFilter != 'all') {
      list = list.where((l) => l.type == _typeFilter);
    }
    if (_query.isNotEmpty) {
      final q = _query.toLowerCase();
      list = list.where((l) =>
          l.userEmail.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
          l.userName.toLowerCase().contains(q));
    }
    return list.toList();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
            child: Row(
              children: [
                _chip('all', 'الكل'),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                _chip('payment_activation', 'بالدفع'),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                _chip('manual_activation', 'يدوي'),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                _chip('refund', 'استرداد'),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
          child: TextField(
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              hintText: 'ابحث بالبريد أو الاسم...',
              prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search),
            ),
            onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _query = v),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Expanded(
          child: _loadingFirst
              ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
              : _error != null
                  ? Center(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                        child: Text(
                          'تعذّر تحميل السجل:\n$_error',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(color: AppColors.error),
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                  : _filtered.isEmpty
                      ? Center(
                          child: Text('لا توجد سجلات',
                              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                  color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                        )
                      : RefreshIndicator(
                          onRefresh: _loadFirst,
                          child: ListView.builder(
                            controller: _scrollCtrl,
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                            itemCount: _filtered.length + 1,
                            itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                              if (i == _filtered.length) {
                                return _buildFooter();
                              }
                              return _logCard(_filtered[i]);
                            },
                          ),
                        ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFooter() {
    if (_loadingMore) {
      return const Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
        child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      );
    }
    if (!_hasMore && _items.isNotEmpty) {
      return Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
        child: Center(
          child: Text(
            'لا توجد سجلات أخرى',
            style:
                GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
    return const SizedBox.shrink();
  }

  Widget _chip(String value, String label) {
    return ChoiceChip(
      label: Text(label, style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12)),
      selected: _typeFilter == value,
      onSelected: (_) {
        setState(() => _typeFilter = value);
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _logCard(ActivationLog log) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      child: SurfaceCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: log.typeColor.withOpacity(0.15),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              ),
              child: Icon(log.typeIcon, color: log.typeColor, size: 20),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Flexible(
                        child: Text(
                          log.userName.isNotEmpty
                              ? log.userName
                              : log.userEmail,
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 6),
                      MiniChip(text: log.typeLabel, color: log.typeColor),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 3),
                  Text(
                    log.userEmail,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      if (log.daysAdded != null && log.daysAdded! > 0) ...[
                        Icon(Icons.add_circle_outline,
                            size: 12, color: AppColors.success),
                        const SizedBox(width: 3),
                        Text('+${log.daysAdded} يوم',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.success)),
                      ],
                      if (log.daysRemoved != null && log.daysRemoved! > 0) ...[
                        Icon(Icons.remove_circle_outline,
                            size: 12, color: AppColors.error),
                        const SizedBox(width: 3),
                        Text('-${log.daysRemoved} يوم',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.error)),
                      ],
                      if (log.wasExtension == true) ...[
                        const SizedBox(width: 8),
                        MiniChip(
                          text: 'تمديد',
                          color: AppColors.purple,
                          icon: Icons.add,
                        ),
                      ],
                      const Spacer(),
                      if (log.createdAt != null)
                        Text(
                          dateFmt.format(log.createdAt!),
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 10.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  if (log.reason != null && log.reason!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text('السبب: ${log.reason}',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 10.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class PaymentDetailScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final PaymentTransaction transaction;
  const PaymentDetailScreen({super.key, required this.transaction});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final t = transaction;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('تفاصيل العملية')),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          GradientCard(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            gradient: LinearGradient(
              colors: [t.statusColor, t.statusColor.withOpacity(0.7)],
              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
              end: Alignment.bottomRight,
            ),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  ),
                  child: Icon(t.statusIcon, color: Colors.white, size: 34),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Text(
                  '${t.amount} ${t.currency}',
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    fontSize: 28,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Container(
                  padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 5),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.25),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    t.statusLabel,
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          const SectionTitle(title: 'معلومات العميل'),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          SurfaceCard(
            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            child: Column(children: [
              _infoTile(Icons.person, 'الاسم',
                  t.userName.isNotEmpty ? t.userName : '-'),
              const Divider(height: 1),
              _infoTile(Icons.email, 'البريد', t.userEmail),
            ]),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          const SectionTitle(title: 'تفاصيل العملية'),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          SurfaceCard(
            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            child: Column(children: [
              _infoTile(Icons.tag, 'رقم العملية', t.orderId),
              if (t.paymentId != null && t.paymentId!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const Divider(height: 1),
                _infoTile(Icons.payment, 'معرف الدفع', t.paymentId!),
              ],
              const Divider(height: 1),
              _infoTile(Icons.calendar_today, 'تاريخ الإنشاء',
                  t.createdAt != null ? dateFmt.format(t.createdAt!) : '-'),
              if (t.completedAt != null) ...[
                const Divider(height: 1),
                _infoTile(Icons.check_circle, 'تاريخ الإكمال',
                    dateFmt.format(t.completedAt!)),
              ],
              if (t.refundedAt != null) ...[
                const Divider(height: 1),
                _infoTile(Icons.undo, 'تاريخ الاسترداد',
                    dateFmt.format(t.refundedAt!)),
              ],
              if (t.refundedByName != null && t.refundedByName!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const Divider(height: 1),
                _infoTile(
                    Icons.admin_panel_settings, 'استردّه', t.refundedByName!),
              ],
              if (t.refundReason != null && t.refundReason!.isNotEmpty) ...[
                const Divider(height: 1),
                _infoTile(Icons.notes, 'سبب الاسترداد', t.refundReason!),
              ],
            ]),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          const SectionTitle(title: 'تفاصيل الاشتراك'),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          SurfaceCard(
            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            child: Column(children: [
              _infoTile(Icons.timer, 'الأيام المضافة',
                  '${t.daysAdded ?? t.days} يوم'),
              const Divider(height: 1),
              _infoTile(
                t.wasExtension == true
                    ? Icons.add_circle_outline
                    : Icons.fiber_new,
                'نوع العملية',
                t.wasExtension == true ? 'تمديد' : 'تفعيل جديد',
              ),
              if (t.previousEndDate != null) ...[
                const Divider(height: 1),
                _infoTile(Icons.event_busy, 'تاريخ الانتهاء السابق',
                    dateFmt.format(t.previousEndDate!)),
              ],
              if (t.newEndDate != null) ...[
                const Divider(height: 1),
                _infoTile(Icons.event_available, 'تاريخ الانتهاء الجديد',
                    dateFmt.format(t.newEndDate!)),
              ],
            ]),
          ),
          if (t.status == 'refunded')
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 20),
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.info.withOpacity(0.08),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  border: Border.all(color: AppColors.info.withOpacity(0.3)),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.info_outline, color: AppColors.info, size: 20),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        'تم استرداد هذا المبلغ وإيقاف الاشتراك.',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                          fontSize: 12.5,
                          color: AppColors.info,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _infoTile(IconData icon, String label, String value) {
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icon, color: AppColors.primary, size: 20),
      title: Text(label,
          style:
              GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
      subtitle: Text(
        value,
        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// 👑 OWNER CONTROL PANEL & SYSTEM LOGS
// ============================================================
class OwnerControlPanelScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const OwnerControlPanelScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<OwnerControlPanelScreen> createState() =>
      _OwnerControlPanelScreenState();
}

class _OwnerControlPanelScreenState extends State<OwnerControlPanelScreen> {
  int _currentPrice = 5000;
  bool _loading = true;
  final _priceController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadSettings();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _priceController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _loadSettings() async {
    final price = await FirebaseService.instance.getSubscriptionPrice();
    if (mounted) {
      setState(() {
        _currentPrice = price;
        _priceController.text = price.toString();
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> _updatePrice() async {
    final newPrice = int.tryParse(_priceController.text);
    if (newPrice == null || newPrice <= 0) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('يرجى إدخال سعر صحيح أكبر من صفر')),
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      await FirebaseService.instance.updateSubscriptionPrice(
        newPrice: newPrice,
        performedBy: 'المالك',
      );
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _currentPrice = newPrice);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(
            content: Text('تم تحديث سعر الاشتراك بنجاح'),
            backgroundColor: Color(0xFF22C55E),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ: $e'), backgroundColor: AppColors.error),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('لوحة تحكم المالك'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.history),
            tooltip: 'سجل نشاطات النظام',
            onPressed: () => Navigator.push(
              context,
              MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const SystemLogsScreen()),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: _loading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              children: [
                GradientCard(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                  gradient: const LinearGradient(
                    colors: [Color(0xFF16A34A), Color(0xFF059669)],
                    begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                    end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.2),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                        ),
                        child: const Icon(Icons.admin_panel_settings,
                            color: Colors.white, size: 32),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 16),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Text('أهلاً بك أيها المالك',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    fontSize: 18,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                            Text('تحكم في إعدادات التطبيق الأساسية',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.85),
                                    fontSize: 12)),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                const SectionTitle(title: 'إعدادات الاشتراك'),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                SurfaceCard(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text('سعر الاشتراك الحالي: $_currentPrice دينار عراقي',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text('المدة الافتراضية: 90 يوماً',
                          style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                              fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      TextField(
                        controller: _priceController,
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 16),
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'السعر الجديد (دينار عراقي)',
                          prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.payments),
                          border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      SizedBox(
                        width: double.infinity,
                        child: GradientButton(
                          label: 'حفظ السعر الجديد',
                          icon: Icons.save,
                          onPressed: _updatePrice,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                const SectionTitle(title: 'سجلات النظام'),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                SurfaceCard(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  onTap: () => Navigator.push(
                    context,
                    MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const SystemLogsScreen()),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: AppColors.info.withOpacity(0.12),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                        ),
                        child: Icon(Icons.history,
                            color: AppColors.info, size: 24),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 16),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Text('عرض سجل النشاطات الشامل',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                            Text(
                                'تفعيل، إلغاء، تغيير أدوار، وتعديل الأسعار مع التفاصيل',
                                style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                    fontSize: 12,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios,
                          size: 16, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
    );
  }
}

// ============================================================
// 📋 SYSTEM LOGS SCREEN
// ============================================================
class SystemLogsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SystemLogsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<SystemLogsScreen> createState() => _SystemLogsScreenState();
}

class _SystemLogsScreenState extends State<SystemLogsScreen> {
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>> _logs = [];
  bool _loading = true;
  String _filterAction = 'all';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadLogs();
  }

  Future<void> _loadLogs() async {
    setState(() => _loading = true);
    try {
      Query query = FirebaseFirestore.instance
          .collection('system_logs')
          .orderBy('timestamp', descending: true)
          .limit(100);

      final snap = await query.get();
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _logs.clear();
          for (var doc in snap.docs) {
            // ✅ تصحيح الخطأ هنا:
            final data = doc.data() as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
            _logs.add({...data, 'id': doc.id});
          }
          _loading = false;
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _loading = false);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text('خطأ في تحميل السجلات: $e')),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> get _filteredLogs {
    if (_filterAction == 'all') return _logs;
    return _logs.where((log) => log['action'] == _filterAction).toList();
  }

  String _formatDate(dynamic timestamp) {
    if (timestamp == null) return 'غير معروف';
    try {
      final date = (timestamp as Timestamp).toDate();
      return DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm', 'ar').format(date);
    } catch (_) {
      return 'غير معروف';
    }
  }

  Color _getActionColor(String action) {
    if (action.contains('تفعيل')) return AppColors.success;
    if (action.contains('إلغاء')) return AppColors.error;
    if (action.contains('تغيير دور')) return AppColors.purple;
    if (action.contains('سعر')) return const Color(0xFFF59E0B);
    return AppColors.info;
  }

  IconData _getActionIcon(String action) {
    if (action.contains('تفعيل')) return Icons.check_circle;
    if (action.contains('إلغاء')) return Icons.cancel;
    if (action.contains('تغيير دور')) return Icons.swap_horiz;
    if (action.contains('سعر')) return Icons.payments;
    return Icons.history;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('سجل نشاطات النظام'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
            onPressed: _loadLogs,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  _filterChip('all', 'الكل'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  _filterChip('تفعيل اشتراك جديد', 'تفعيل'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  _filterChip('تمديد اشتراك', 'تمديد'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  _filterChip('إلغاء اشتراك', 'إلغاء'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  _filterChip('تغيير دور مستخدم', 'تغيير دور'),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  _filterChip('تعديل سعر الاشتراك', 'تعديل السعر'),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: _loading
                ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                : _filteredLogs.isEmpty
                    ? Center(
                        child: Text('لا توجد سجلات',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                color: AppColors.textSecondary)),
                      )
                    : ListView.builder(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        itemCount: _filteredLogs.length,
                        itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                          final log = _filteredLogs[i];
                          return _logCard(log);
                        },
                      ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _filterChip(String value, String label) {
    return ChoiceChip(
      label: Text(label, style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 12)),
      selected: _filterAction == value,
      onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _filterAction = value),
    );
  }

  Widget _logCard(Map<String, dynamic> log) {
    final action = log['action'] ?? 'عملية غير معروفة';
    final targetName = log['targetName'] ?? 'غير معروف';
    final performedBy = log['performedBy'] ?? 'غير معروف';
    final performedByRole = log['performedByRole'] ?? 'غير معروف';
    final details = log['details'] as Map<String, dynamic>? ?? {};
    final timestamp = log['timestamp'];

    final color = _getActionColor(action);
    final icon = _getActionIcon(action);

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      child: SurfaceCard(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: color.withOpacity(0.15),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                  ),
                  child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 20),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        action,
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        'بواسطة: $performedBy ($performedByRole)',
                        style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                            fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                Text(
                  _formatDate(timestamp),
                  style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                      fontSize: 10, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const Divider(height: 20),
            Row(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.person_outline,
                    size: 14, color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    'المستهدف: $targetName',
                    style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                        fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            if (details.isNotEmpty) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.bgLight.withOpacity(0.5),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: details.entries.map((e) {
                    return Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 4),
                      child: Row(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            '${e.key}: ',
                            style: GoogleFonts.cairo(
                                fontSize: 11,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                color: AppColors.textSecondary),
                          ),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(
                              e.value.toString(),
                              style: GoogleFonts.cairo(fontSize: 11),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    );
                  }).toList(),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
